   “iṉpamum iṭumpaiyum puṇarvum pirivum
   naṉpaka lamaiyamum iravum pōla
   vēṟuvē ṟiyala vāki māṟetirn tuḷa.”
   eṉṉum poruḷai ivai naṉkuṇarntavai yākalāṉum, muṉṉar nikaḻcciyiṟ puḻuviṉ
   ciṉattai yaṟavē maṟaiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu ciṟitu aḷavaḷāvip piṉ varumāṟu mīṭṭum
   avai collāṭal toṭaṅkiṉa.
   paruntu:- yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟai, muṉṉarē kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, uṟutinōkkum,
   oppaṉa koḷḷum uḷamum koṇṭu kēṭpaiyēṟ kūṟuval.
   puḻu :- āyiṉ, avai eṉṉiṭat tilaveṉa veṇṇuti pōlum naṉṟu naṉṟu ā
   kēḷviyilā teytum evvakaip poruḷaiyum uṟutinōkkuk koṇṭa, uḷattiṟkōṭalē
   eṉṉiyalpeṉa yāṉ kūṟiyirukku matu niṉ niṉaiviṉkaṇillai kollō
   paruntu:- “uṇarntaṉa maṟakkum, maṟantaṉavuṇarum" eṉpar periyōr. iṉi
   maṟavēṉ; poṟuttaruḷka. niṟka, yāṉ muṉpu kūṟiyaṉa niṉṉaiyum, niṉ
   ciṉaikaḷaiyum paṟṟip potuvāka vuṇarntaṉa; iṟṟaiñāṉṟu kūṟappōvatu
   niṉporuṭ ṭuṇarnta ciṟappup poruḷākum. atu, curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, nīyum
   orupakal appūcciyē yātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
   puḻu :- eṉṉai kūṟiṉai? ē, maṭapparuntē niṟuttuka niṉ coṟpoḻiviṉai!
   niṉṉoṭu nimirntañēyam ippayaṉaip payakkum nīrmaittāyiṉ, eṉ kūṟuvatu.
   pālākit tōṉṟip parukiṉār āvikoḷḷum ālāla nīrmaittē niṉṉaṉpu eṉpatē
   cālap poruntuvatu. polivu, tiṭpam mutaliyavillā yākkaiyēṉ eṉa eṇṇi
   iṉṉaṇam eṉṉai iḻittuk kūṟuvāṉ ceyta kūṟṟēyitu. aṟintēṉ. niṉ taṉmaiyai
   yāṉ iṉṟē uṇarntēṉ. nī peruṅkoṭiyai aṟivili! tirutta viyalāt tīyai
   pētai!! nāṇiṉṟi eṉ muṉṉiṟṟal niṉ māṇpiṟkē kuṟaivām. iṉi eṉakku niṉ
   poṅku cōṟum vēṇṭā: pūcārittaṉamum vēṇṭā. pōtum pōtum!!
   ivvaṇṇam kūṟiya puḻuviṉ neñcucuṭu neṭumoḻi vaṉciṟaipparuntiṉ maṉattut
   tiṉaittuṇaiyum ciṉat tī koḷuttiṟṟiṉṟu. eṉṉai, vaṉmaiyuḷ vaṉmai maṭavārp
   poṟai yākaliṉ. maṟṟu, atu, ataṉmaṉattut "tiṟaṉalla taṟpiṟar ceyyiṉum
   nōnontu aṟaṉalla ceyyāmai naṉṟu" eṉṉum teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvap payaṉai
   yeḻac ceytu, uṟuvatu tērāp puḻuviṉmāṭṭu irakkamum, ataṉ aṟiyāmaikkuk
   kavaṟciyumē tōṉṟac ceytatu. vēṟu eṉ ceyum atu appuḻuviṉai, "aṉpa, nī
   vekuṇṭu kūṟuvaṉayāvum veḷiṟṟuraikaḷē. orupuṭai, avai, nī icciṉai māṭṭup
   pūṇṭoḻukum pērātaraviṉ uṟutiyaip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa veṉiṉum, āṉṟa
   kēḷviyāṟṟōṭṭa ceviyai yallai yeṉpataik karṭṭātu kaḻiyavillai. eṉ
   coṟkaḷai nī ēṟṟuk koḷḷāyeṉap paṇṭē yāṉ kūṟiṉēṉaṉṟō," eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
   puḻu :-ām. yāṉ kēṭpaṉavaṟṟil uṟutip poruḷaik kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum
   nōkkattuṭaṉ ēṟṟuk koḷva leṉpataip paṇṭu kūṟiyatē yaṉṟi, iṉṟum
   kūṟukiṉṟēṉ, iṉi eṉṟum kūṟuvēṉ. uraippār uraippaṉavaṟṟai yuraitta vāṟṟā
   ṉuṭkoḷḷu miyalpiṉai yuṭaiyārkku, avar uṇmai kalantavaṟṟait tāmē
   yuraittal vēṇṭum. oru vāṟṟāṉu miyaipillāta voṉṟai yiyaittukkūṟiṉ yāvar
   tām ēṟpar. nilaiyāta vaṟṟai nilaiyiṉa veṉṟuṇarum pullaṟivāṇmai
   kaṭaiyātal pōla, iyaipillaṉavaṟṟai yiyaipuṭaiyavākkik kūṟalum,
   kūṟakkēṭṭalum pullaṟivāṇmai yātalōṭu kaṭaiyātalum tōṉṟiṟṟaṉṟē. naṇpa!
   cēṭappūccikaḷiṉ ciṉai poriyiṉ kīṭaṅkaḷāka māṟumeṉpatum, kīṭaṅkaḷun tam
   iyaṅkutaṟ ṟaṉmai nīṅkip paṟattaṟṟaṉmaiyiṉai yeytu meṉpatum muṟṟilum
   poruntāk kūṟṟukkaḷē. ivaṟṟōraṉṉa puṉmoḻikaḷai nī yāṇṭu, yāṅṅaṉam,
   kēṭṭaṟkut tuṇintaṉai? ivai yollumō veṉpatai nīyē āyntu nōkkuka.
   aṅṅaṉamātal orukālum muṭiyātaṉṟō!
   paruntu:- iṉi vēṟu kūṟal eṉakku iyalātu. vēṇṭumēl yāṉ aṟintavaṟṟaik
   kūṟuval; kēḷ: yāṉ vayal vaḻiyum, vāṉāṟum paṭarntu koṭpuṟum pāṉmaiyēṉ.
   aṅkaṉam, paṭaruṅkāl, iṭaiyil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷaik kāṇātu celvatu
   illai. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum niṉaivuvallār neñcum kaṭantu niṟkuntaṉmaittu.
   avaṟṟaik kaṇṇuṟṟut teḷintayāṉ ivvāṟu nikaḻtaluṅ kūṭumeṉat tuṇintēṉ. or
   kuṟukiya ellaikkaṇiyakkamum, tāṉvāḻum ilaiyaṉṟip
   piṟakaṇṭaṟiyāmaiyumuṭaiya niṉakkup piṟavaṉaittum ariyavum,
   iyaipillavum, muṭiyātavumākavē tōṉṟum, kiṇaṟṟuttavaḷai puṇariyiyalpai
   yaṟiyuṅkol!
   puḻu :- antō āyntiṭu muṇarvutaṉṉai niṉakku avvayaṉ paṭaittilaṉkol
   avaṉaik kāṇpaṉēl, aṟṟatalai pōka, aṟātatalai nāṉkiṉaiyum paṟṟittirukip
   paṟippēṉ! aṟitiyō!! ikkūṟṟu ittuṇaiyuṟutiyuṭaittu; uṇmai yuṭaittu
   eṉpaṉavaṟṟaiyumō yāṉ aṟiyēṉ ā!! ittuṇaikkālam ivvulakiṭaippiṟantuḻaṉṟa
   yāṉ ītaṟiyātu oḻivalō eṉṉē niṉmaṭamai eṉ paciya neṭiya vuṭalaiyum,
   eṇṇiṟanta kālkaḷaiyum nōkkum ivaṟṟai nēriṟ kaṇṭum, eṉakku
   ivvuṭaṉiṅkumeṉpatum, palvakai vaṇṇamamainta puttuṭaloṉṟu eytu meṉpatum
   ettuṇai iḻittakka moḻikaḷākiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṟiyāmaiyē pētamai yeṉpatu.
   kūṟuvōrkku uṇarvu kuṉṟiṉum, kēṭpōrkkum atu kuṉṟuṅ kolō?
   paruntu:- (ciṉantu) eṉṉai kūṟiṉai? “yākāvā rāyiṉum nākākka, kāvākkāṟ
   cōkāppar colliḻukkup paṭṭu” eṉṉum poyyil moḻiyiṉai maṟattiyō? eṉṉai nī
   matiyili yeṉpatu uṇmaiyē. kallā aṟivil kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta nallār
   tamatu kaṉam naṇṇutaluṇṭukol! itaṉaic ciṟitēyu maṟintiruntum, yāṉ
   ivvuṇmaiyaik kūṟiyavantaṉaṉaṉṟē! niṉ aṟivuk keṭṭātavaṟṟai vaṟpuṟuttik
   kūṟaniṉaittalum, atu paṟṟi niṉṉuḻai yeytalum eṉṉilaikku
   ēṟpaṉavallavaṉṟō! vāṉōkki yeḻuṅkāl eṉvāyvaḻi varum iṉṉoli kēṭṭōrkku
   iṉpam payantu, eṉakku matuvaḻiyēyōr ciṟappiṉaiyum nalkutal orutalai.
   itu kiṭakka, nī iṉiyēṉum varuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum, piṟar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum
   oṉṟiya vuḷḷamoṭu ēṟṟukkoḷka.
   ivvāṟu, puḻuviṉ puṉmoḻikaḷ palavum paruntiṉ ceviyiṟ pukkum atu
   vekuḷātu, nāṉātu, aṉpuṭai naṉmoḻi kūṟalum, puḻu taṉ aṟiyāmaikkum,
   ataṉāṟ ciṉantu pitaṟṟiya moḻikaṭkum uḷam veḷki, "ataṟku nī kūṟum
   annampikkaiyum uṟuti nōkkum eṉ māṭṭila veṇpāyō? eṉṟatu.
   paruntu:- uḷavēl, ittuṇai neṭumoḻikaḷ piṟakkuṅ kollō!
   puḻu :- aṟṟēl, avvuṟuti nōkku eṉṉuḻai yuṇṭātaṟku yāṉ ceyyakkaṭavateṉ?
   aṉpu kūrntu kūṟuka.
   iṅṅaṉam amaiticāṉṟa vuraiyāṭal iṭainikaḻuṅ kālattup paruntu cēṭpulam
   paṭarntatu. puḻuvum taṉṉaip paṉmuṟaiyum nōkki, acciṉaiyirunta
   murukkilaiyaic cūḻvantu koṇṭiruppa, avvilaiyait tuḷaittukkoṇṭu ēḻeṭṭup
   paccilaip puḻukkaḷ aṅkumiṅku miyaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉa. avai yāṇṭiruntu
   pōntaṉa? avai acciṉaikkaṇiṉṟu pōntaṉavē! pōtarakkaṇṭa puḻuviṉ puntiyiṟ
   kalakkamum māṉamuṅkalantu tōṉṟiṉa: kaṇṇakaṉ ñālam koṭpuṟal ceytatu;
   uṭal cumantu niṟkum taṉṉaiyē māyamō veṉṟeṇṇiṟṟu. paruntu kūṟiya moḻi
   yovvoṉṟum ataṉ uḷattut tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉṉaṇam paruntiṉ mutalurai yuṇmaiyātal
   kāṇṭaliṉ, maṟṟatum avvāṟātal kūṭumeṉum tuṇipum uṭaṉeytiya eytalum, atu
   paṭṭapāṭu yārāṟ kūṟappaṭun takaittām!!
   piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa; parantavil vulakiṟ ṟōṉṟum poruḷiṉāṉām
   kāṭciyaṟivaip paruntu uṭaṉuṭaṉ puḻuviṟ kuraittalum, uṇmaiyāytalum
   iraṇṭiṟkum marapāyiṟṟu. puḻuvum taṉṉiṉaṅkaṭkut taṉakku nēravirukkum
   nikaḻcciyaiyum, atuvē yavaṟṟiṟkumā meṉpataiyum aṟivuṟuttum; uṟuttiṉum,
   ataṉaiyavai ēlātē yoḻintaṉa. puḻumaṭṭiṟṟāṉṟaṉ meynnaṭpukkuriya
   paruntuḻaik kaṇṭa meypporuḷ nikaḻtaṟ kēṟṟa kālam eytiṟṟāka, pakalcey
   maṇṭilam paṉikkaṭal mukaṭṭeḻa, paṟavai yiṉṉicaipāṭa, curumpuntēṉum
   cūḻntārppa, kaṭikkamalam vāyviḷḷa, pūcciyiṉam poliveyta, puḻuviṉaṅkaḷ
   kuḻīi maṟuka, ippuḻuvum pūcciyāyiṟṟu; yākkaiyum kāloṭumitakkum
   kaviṉeytiṟṟu iyaṅkiyavuṭal paṟattal ceytatu; irumaruṅkum
   ciṟakukaḷeḻuntaṉa; avaṟṟiṉkaṭ palvakaiya voḷiteṟikkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷ
   tōṉṟiyaḻaku ceytaṉa.
   ivvaṇṇam, aḻakutikaḻap puttuṭal peṟṟup pūntēṉuṇṭu polivukūr cuṟṟamoṭu
   purintu vāḻnta cēṭap pūcci, piṉṉarc ciṉṉāṭkaḷuṭṭaṉ ṉiṉṉuyir nīṅkuṅ kāla
   meytalum, eṇṇaru miṉpaṅkaṉiya, “im maṟuviṉṟu viḷaṅkum māyiruñālattu,
   aṟivu vaḷarkkumāṟṟā ṉamainta poruḷkaḷ mikappalavuḷa; avaṟṟaiyāyntu
   kōṭalaṟiviṉ kaṭaṉ: ataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta vuṟuppāvatu uṟutinōkkē (Faith).
   ataṉai ataṉaruḷālē yaṭaiyappeṟṟēṉ. yāṉ peṟṟa iṉpam peṟuka ivvaiyakam!!
   iṉi evai variṉum varuka. yāṉ viṇṇakam paṭarkuval" eṉṟeḻuntu ceṉṟu
   viṇṇil maṟaintatu.
   -----
   * (karantait tamiḻc caṅkak kallūriyiṉiṉṟu veḷivanta pañcāpakēcaṉ eṉṉum
   kaiyeḻuttut tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭiṉkaṇ muṉṉar veḷivantatōr moḻipeyarppu.
   āṅkila nūṟ kaṭṭuraikaḷuḷ amiḻntu tiḷaitta nilaiyil ikkaṭṭurai āṅkilam
   taḻuviya tamiḻākkamāka amaintatu) -
   ---------

                                2. uḻaippu

   “viṉaiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāṇutal
   maṉaiyuṟai makaḷirkku āṭavar uyireṉa
   namakkurait tōrum tāmē
   aḻāaltōḻi avar aḻuṅkuvar celavē...”
         - kuṟuntokai: 135
   naṟkuṇamum naṟceykaiyum ciṟanta naṅkaiyorutti, aṟivum āṇmaiyum perukiya
   tōṉṟal oruvaṉai maṇantu illaṟam pūṇṭu vantaṉaḷ. orunāḷ avaṉ, tāṉ
   ceyvataṟkuriya viṉaiyoṉṟu kuṟittut taṉ maṉaiyiṉ nīṅkic
   cellavēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. maṇantanāḷ toṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉaip pirintaṟiyāta
   annaṅkaikku itu teriyiṉ, avaḷ peruvaruttam eytuvaḷ eṉpatai attōṉṟal
   eṇṇiṉaṉ. uṇmaik kātalvaḻi yoḻukuvārkkup pirivupōlap peruntuṉpam
   taruvatu piṟitu kiṭaiyātu. "iṉṉātu iṉaṉillūr vāḻtal, ataṉiṉum iṉṉātu
   iṉiyārp pirivu" eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl taṉ karuttiṉai,
   avaṉ, avaḷatu tōḻikku uraittu, piṉṉar mellat taṉ maṉaiyāṭkut
   terivikkumāṟu paṇittāṉ. avaḷum ataṟku oruvāṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭu, kālam vāytta
   pōtu avaṉatu karuttiṉaip paiya vuṇarttiṉāḷ. kēṭṭāḷō illaiyō, naṅkaikkup
   peruntuyar uṇṭākiviṭṭatu; kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiyaḻat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷai
   āṟṟuvikkat toṭaṅkiya tōḻi, "naṅkāy, kaṇavaṉ collaik kāppatu maṉaivikkuk
   kaṭaṉ. avar orunāḷ coṉṉatu oṉṟuṇṭu. atu niṉakkum terintatē. aḵtāvatu:
   'āṭavarkkut tāṅkaḷ ceyyum viṉaiyē uyir; maṉaiyuṟaiyum makaḷirkku
   avvāṭavarē uyir. avarkku uyirākiya viṉaimēṟ ceṉṟāltāṉē, avar niṉakku
   uyirāka iruttalkūṭum; nī aḻutāl avar viṉaimēṟcelvataik kaiviṭuvarē.
   ataṉāl ētamaṉṟō viḷaiyum, "eṉṟaṉaḷ. atu kēṭṭa annaṅkaiyum tēṟi avaṉ
   pirivai āṟṟit taṉakkuriya aṟattiṉaiyum āṟṟivantaṉaḷ.
   icciṟiya pāṭṭiṉaip pāṭiyavar pālai pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉṉum
   cāṉṟōrāvar.
   ivvāṟu viṉai mēṟ cella niṉainta talaimakaṉ orunāḷ mālaippōtu
   varakkaṇṭu, "ā. ippōtu nam kātali nam maṉaiyakattu viḷakkēṟṟi ataṉ muṉ
   niṉṟu, 'iṉṉum kātalar vantilarē' eṉṟu niṉaikkum pōtu aṉṟō?" eṉṟu
   taṉakkuḷ niṉaittāṉ. niṉaittavaṉ, immālai,
   ‘uḷḷiṉēṉ allaṉō yāṉē, uḷḷiya
   viṉaimuṭit taṉṉa iṉiyōḷ
   maṉaimāṇ cuṭaroṭu paṭarpoḻu teṉavē.”
         - naṟṟiṇai 3
   eṉat taṉakkuḷ niṉaintu iṉaintāṉ eṉa iḷaṅkīraṉār kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivviru
   ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, namakku ōr uṇmai pulappaṭum. aḵtāvatu, ōr
   āṇ makaṉukku avaṉ ceytaṟkuriya viṉaitāṉ uyir; ataṉai muṭitta
   vaḻiyuḷatākum iṉpam kātaliṉpam pōlvatu eṉpatām.
   viṉaiyeṉpatu nām ceyyum ceykaiyākum. ovvōruyirum ulakil tōṉṟumpōtē
   viṉai ceyvataṟkeṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟatu. viṉaiyē oruvaṉ eytum iṉpattukkum
   tuṉpattukkum kāraṇamāvatu. iṉpattaiyē evvuyirum peṟa virumpum;
   tuṉpattai virumpum uyir etuvumillai. tuṉpam eṉṟa collaik kēṭkumpōtē nam
   uḷḷam cuḷikkum; collutaṟkum nākkuk kūcum. iṉpattait tarum viṉaiyē
   yāvarum virumpum toḻilākum. ataṉāltāṉ iṉpam tarakkūṭiyataṉai nalviṉai,
   naṟceykai eṉṟum, tuṉpam taruvataṉait tīviṉai, tīcceykai eṉṟum
   collukiṉṟōm.
   iṉi ivviṉaiyaic ceyyumpoḻutu nam uṭalum uyirum peritum pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   ivviraṇṭum oruṅkē kūṭic ceyyāta vaḻi, entat toḻilum naṭaipeṟuvatu
   illai. ettoḻilaic ceyvatāyiṉum ivai uḻaikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. uyir
   uṭaloṭu kūṭiyiruppatu ivvuḻaippukkē; ivvuḻaippāl, uyir uṭalpaṭum
   tuṉpamaṉaittaiyum tāṉ paṭukiṟateṉṟālum, uyirkku uṭaliṉmēl uḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl
   uḻaikka uḻaikka, ataṟku ataṉmēl paṟṟu mikukiṉṟatu; "tuṉpam uḻattoṟum
   kātaṟṟu uyir"eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṉṟār. eṉavē, uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻum
   ovvōruyirkkum uḻaippu iyalpāka amaintiruppatai aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl,
   ivvuyirkaḷuḷ makkaḷuyirmaṭṭil, ēṉai uyirkaḷai viṭac ciṟitu
   mēmpaṭṭatākum. taṉ uḻaippāl āṇṭavaṉ paṭaippil uḷḷa ēṉai uyirkaḷaiyum
   uyiril poruḷkaḷaiyum muṟaiyē aṭimaiyākavum uṭaimaiyākavum ākkik
   koḷḷaṟku vēṇṭiya urimai makkaḷuyir peṟṟirukkiṟatu. ātalāṟṟāṉ iṉṟu
   maṉitaṉ nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu, vicumpu ākiya pūtam aintum tāṉ
   virumpumāṟu amaiyac ceykiṉṟāṉ. nilattiṟkuḷ nuḻaintu kuṭaintu celkiṉṟāṉ.
   nīriṉ mēlum kīḻum nimirntu celkiṉṟāṉ; kāṟṟai ēval koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ; vicumpil
   paṟantēkukiṉṟāṉ. curuṅkac coṉṉāl, maṉitaṉ āṇṭavaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka
   viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṉalām.
   maṉitaṉ ittuṇai ēṟṟamuṭaiyavaṉāka iruttaṟkuriya urimai avaṉpāl
   nilaittirukka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avaṉ calippiṉṟi uḻaittal vēṇṭum. uḻaippiṉāl
   avaṉ uṭal tūymaiyaṭaikiṟatu. ataṉāl avaṉ uḷḷam oḷipeṟukiṟatu; appōtu
   āṇṭavaṉ avaṟku aruḷiyuḷḷa urimai katirviṭṭut tikaḻkiṉṟatu. atuvaḻiyāka
   avaṉpāl uyariya eṇṇaṅkaḷum, ulavāta iṉpamum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷuṭ
   cilar, avvurimaiyaik kāttukkoḷḷum valikuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar; urimaiyai
   nilaippikkum uḻaippiṉai nekiḻttuc cōmpaluṟukiṉṟaṉar. cōmpal uṭalaik
   keṭukkiṉṟatu; ataṉāl uyir maṭaṅkukiṉṟatu; urimai nilavum uḷḷam
   maṭikiṉṟatu. ākavē, cōmpal maṭimaikku uyirai iraiyākki, uḷḷattē
   iruḷaiyum uyirkku iṟutiyaiyum viḷaivikkiṉṟatu. oru periyāratu
   uṭaṉpiṟantār iṟantupōṉār eṉpatu kēḷviyuṟṟu, vēṟoru periyār avarai
   nōkkit "taṅkaḷ tampi evvāṟu iṟantār?" eṉṟu kēṭṭataṟku, avar, "avaṉ
   uḻaittaṟku iṉṟi iṟantāṉ" eṉṟārām. ākavē, uḻaippiṉṟic cōmpiyiruppavaṉ,
   viraivil taṉ uyirai iḻakkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ uḷḷattē, uyirkkut tīṅku ceyyum
   tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷum aṟiyāmaiyumē kuṭikoḷḷum eṉa aṟiyalām.
   iṉṟu nām aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷaiyum, periya periya māṭa māḷikaikaḷaiyum,
   pacum kampaḷam virittatupōlat tōṉṟum paranta paṇpaṭṭa vayalkaḷaiyum
   kāṇkiṉṟōm. kāṭaḻintu nāṭākiyirukkiṟatu; karuṅkaṭalil peruṅkalam
   celkiṉṟatu; kampiyiṉṟiyē tanti pēcukiṟatu; viraivil, ‘'kaṇṭēṉ avar
   tiruppātam, kaṇṭaṟiyātaṉa kaṇṭēṉ" eṉa nam nāvaracar pāṭiyatai nāmum
   pāṭappōkiṉṟōm. ivaiyāvum uḻaippiṉ payaṉaṉṟō! "uḻaippiṉ vārā uṟutikaḷ
   uḷavō?" eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ uraittatu uṇmai moḻiyaṉṟō uḻaippiṉṟi uṭal
   cōmpi, uyir oyntu oṭuṅkiyirukkum oruvaṉaip pāruṅkaḷ avaṉ uḷḷattil
   oḷiyillai. ataṉai avalam, kavalai, kaiyāṟu, aḻuṅkal, ācai, vekuḷi,
   aḻukkāṟu mutaliyaṉa cūḻntu arikkiṉṟaṉa; avaṉum urukkulaintumelikiṉṟāṉ.
   avaṉai ētēṉum oru toḻilaicceyya viṭuvōm: uṭaṉē, avaṉatu uṭalum uyirum
   oruppaṭṭu attoḻiṟkaṇ uḻaikkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ vaṉmai muḻuvatum
   attoḻilai muṭittaṟ kaṇ oṉṟiviṭukiṉṟatu. avaṉ uḷḷattai yalaitta
   aḻukkuṇarvukaḷaṉaittum akalappōy eṅkēyō maṟaintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ
   maṉitaṉ, maṉitaṉāka vayaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉatu uḷḷam pēroḷi viṭṭu
   miḷirkiṉṟatu; avvaḻukkuṇarvukaḷ avvoḷiyiṉkaṇ pukaintu erintu puttoḷi
   ceykiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, oruvaṉatu uḷḷattait tūymai ceyvataṟku uḻaippē
   ciṟanta maruntāka irukkiṟateṉpatu eḷitil viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
   nām vāḻum immaṇṇulaku ātiyil ñāyiṟṟiliruntu pōntatu eṉṟum, aṉṟumutal
   iṉṟu varaiyil cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭēyirukkiṟatu eṉṟum nām aṟintirukkiṉṟōm.
   itaṉai ivvāṟu cuḻalvitta paramaṉ karuttu yātākal vēṇṭum? iṭaiyaṟātu
   cuḻalum cuḻaṟciyiṉāl nām vāḻum maṇṇulaku, viṇṇulakil vaṭṭamāka iyaṉṟu,
   pala vikāra uruvaṅkaḷaip peṟṟu, muṭivil, iṟṟaiya uruṇṭai vaṭivattaip
   peṟṟirukkiṟatu. cuḻalalāvatu niṉṟupōmāyiṉ, innilavulakam yātāy muṭiyum?
   orucār pakalum maṟṟaiya pakuti iruḷumāy evvuyirum vāḻtaṟkiṉṟi,
   epporuḷum nilaipeṟuvatiṉṟik keṭṭaḻiyumeṉpataṉaic collavum vēṇṭumō
   aḷittakka immaṇṇakam, iṭaiyaṟaviṉṟic cuḻaṉṟuḻalum uḻaippiṉāltāṉ itaṉpāl
   muṟai tirumputalum, nērmai kōṭutalum piṟa tīmaikaḷum ilavāyiṉa;
   vikāraṅkaḷ nīṅkic cīriya ivvuruvamum amaivatāyiṟṟu.
   kulālaṉatuḻaippum, avaṉatu taṇṭa cakkaramum maṉitaṉ uṇavaṭṭu
   uṇṇattoṭaṅkiya nāṇmutal aṟintaṉavākum. camaya nūlkaḷum ilakkaṇa
   nūlkaḷum, avaṉaiyē avaṉ ceyyum viṉaiyaiyum, viṉaikkuriya karuvi
   karaṇaṅkaḷaiyum aṭikkaṭi eṭuttukkāṭṭit tam aṟivup poruḷai
   vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. accakkarattiṉ naṭuvil vaikkappaṭum maṇtiraḷaip
   pāruṅkaḷ! ataṉiṭattil ētēṉum pāṇṭavamaippukkuriya vaṭivamuḷatō? aḻakiya
   uruvamum tiruntiya vaṭivamumuṭaiya maṭkalaṅkaḷ accakkarattiṉ cuḻaṟciyāl
   piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtu illaiyāyiṉ akkulālaṉ maṭkalaṉkaḷai yākkutal kūṭumō?
   ettuṇait toḻiṟciṟappum, ulaiyāvuḻaippu muṭaiyaṉāyiṉum avaṉ yātuceyya
   muṭiyum? avaṉ ceyya muyalum kalaṅkaṭku aḻakum vaṭivum amaiyuṅkol!
   iyaṟkaiyulakum, cakkarattuṇaiyillāta kulālaṉaippōla, ōyntu oṭuṅki
   niṟkumāyiṉ ataṉ payaṉ eṉṉāy muṭiyum! avvāṟu ceyyātu calippiṉṟi,
   uḻaikkumāṟu aruḷiya āṇṭavaṉai eppōtum vaṇaṅkuvōmāka.
   uḻaittaliṉṟic cōmpik kiṭakkum oruvaṉ ettuṇaip poruṇalaṅkaḷ
   uṭaiyaṉāyiṉum, avaṟku avai oru payaṉaiyum ceyyā. cakaṭacakkaramiṉṟik
   kaiyaṟṟu niṟkum kulālaṉpāl uḷḷa maṇtiraḷ pōla, uḻaippuc ciṟappillāta
   oruvaṉum veṟuntacaip potiyum ciṟappil piṇṭamumāvatallatu piṟitillai.
   kulālaṉai makaṉākavum, avaṉatu cakkarattai uḻaippākavum koṇṭu nōkkuka.
   uḻaippu uyarvu tarum; uḻaippāḷi ulakāḷvāṉ. ākavē, uḻaippup peṟṟavaṉ
   uṟutipeṟṟavaṉākiṉṟāṉ. uyir vāḻkkaiyiṉ uṟutippayaṉ avaṉ pālēyuḷatu.
   uyarvu vēṇṭuvōṉ uḻaippu vēṇṭuka. karuvaraivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum
   kaikalantu kalittōṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṭaippaṭṭa māṇporuḷai varaṉṟi yēkum
   uḻaippu vaṉmaiyāl avai kalittuc cerukkukiṉṟaṉa. aḻukkum, mācum,
   kuppaiyum, kūḷamum avaṟṟiṉ neṟiyiṟ paṭṭu alaittuk koṇṭēkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   kāl kālāy vayaliṭaippaṭintu uramākit tām paravum nilap parappaip pacum
   pullum, naṟumalarum kaṇkavar vaṉappumuṭaiya vaṇpoḻilākkum ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ
   māṇṭoḻilai eṉṉeṉpatu! avaṟṟiṉ teṇṇīr uṇṇīrākiṟatu. atu cumantu koṇarnta
   aḻukkukaḷum avaṟṟōraṉṉa mācukaḷum māyntu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālaṉṟō,
   "uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē; nīrum nilaṉum puṇariyōr, īṇṭu,
   uṭampum uyirum paṭaittici ṉōrē" eṉak kuṭapulaviyaṉārum pāṇṭiyaṉ
   neṭuñceḻiyaṟkup pāṭiyaṟivuṟuttiṉār. ittakaiya nīr yāṟē uḻaippeṉavaṟika.
   pala tiṟamāy, palvakaip poruḷkaḷaiyākki, nāṭukaḷai nāṭā
   vaḷamuṭaiyavākac ceytu, makkaḷai iṉpamum, poruḷum, aṟamum, vīṭum iṉitu
   eytuvittu, avartam vāḻkkaiyiṉ perumpayaṉai nukarvippatu uḻaippēyākum.
   ākavē, uḻaippē uyir eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. uyir uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkkai
   naṭattuntōṟum, ataṉpāl pēriṉpam peṟutalpōla, uḻaippāḷiyum uḻaikkuntōṟum
   uvaṭṭāta pēriṉpamum ūkka mikutiyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruḷ avaṉ
   uḷḷattē ūṟukiṟatu. uyar nōkkum, oḷḷaṟivum, perun taṉmaiyum avaṉpāl
   uḷavāki yōṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
   nōṉmai, tuṇivu, muyaṟci, aṟivup pēṟṟiṉkaṇ ārvam, taṉkuṟṟam tērntu
   kaṭital mutaliyaṉa iṉpa vāḻvukku ākkamākum nallaṟaṅkaḷ. uṇartta
   uṇarvatilum, ivaṟṟai nāmē nam uḻaippu vāyilāka uṇarntu naṉṉeṟikaḷiṟ
   celuttuvatē nam uyir vāḻkkaiyākum. uḻaippu vāyilāka ivaṟṟai nām uṇartal
   vēṇṭum. nōṉmai eṉpatu ettuṇai iṭukkaṇkaḷ aṭukkivariṉum, avaṟṟiṟku
   aḻiyātu, avaṟṟāl varum tuṉpattaip poṟuttu mēṟkoḷḷum taṉmaiyākum.
   aṅṅaṉam mēṟ koḷḷutaṟkuriya tuṇaiyākum maṉattiṭpamē tuṇivu eṉpatu.
   tuṇintavaḻi, iṭaiyil vantu takaiyum iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl uḷḷamuṭaiyāmal,
   ceyviṉaiyaik kaṭaipōka uḻaittu veṟṟikāṇum tiṟam muyaṟciyākum. ataṉkaṇ
   aṟiyātaṉavaṟṟai aṟintāṟṟum viraku vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, aṟivaṟiyum ārvam
   vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. avvārvattāl, mutaṟkaṇ tāmē tērtalum piṟar kāṭṭak
   kāṇṭalum ākiya iru neṟikaḷāl tam kuṟṟattaik kaṇṭu ataṉaik kaṭivatum,
   ceyta kuṟṟattai iṉic ceyyā vakaiyil tammaip pātukāttu, aṭutta muṟaiyil
   naṉkāṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum naṟpaṇpuṭaiyaṉāvatum uḻaippāḷikku
   iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavākum. ippaṇpukaḷaik kaikkoṇṭu uḻaippōrmuṉ, vāṉṟōy
   malaiyaṉaiya pēriṭarkaḷ vantu niṟkum. "naṟceyaluk kaṉṟē nāṉūṟu
   iṭaiyūṟu." āyiṉum uṇmai yuḻaippāḷiyō, ēṉaiyavaṟṟāl varum "iṉpam
   viḻaiyāṉ; iṭumpai iyal peṉpāṉ; tuṉpam uṟutal ilaṉ." karumamē kaṇṇāka
   uḻaikkum avaṉ, "mey varuttam pārāṉ; paci nōkkāṉ; kaṇ tuñcāṉ; evvevar
   tīmaiyum mēṟkoḷḷāṉ; cevviyarumaiyum pārāṉ; avamatippum koḷḷāṉ." cuṭac
   cuṭarum poṉpōl, avaṉ uḷḷam tuṉpaṅkaḷ toṭarntu pōntu cuṭaccuṭa, oṭpamum
   tiṭpamum peṟṟu ōṅki yoḷirum. nilavulakiṉum, avaṉ pukaḻē ōṅkip poṉṟātu
   niṉṟu nilavum, attuṉpaṅkaḷum "eri muṉṉar vaittuṟu pōlak keṭum.”
   ilaṇṭaṉ nakarattil uḷḷa ceyiṉṭ pāl katīṭral eṉṉum peruṅkōyil
   ulaiyāvuḻaippiṉ uruvuṭaip payaṉākum. ataṉai yeṭuttaṟku muyaṉṟa car
   kiṟistapar reṉ eṉpār, kaṇitanūl, vāṉanūl, ciṟpanūl mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ perum
   pulamaiyum viṉaittiṭpamum uṭaiyar. āyiṉum, avar itaṉaik
   kaṭṭiyamaittaṟku muyaṉṟa kālattu uḷavākiya iṭaiyūṟukaṭku aḷavēyillai.
   urōm nakarattuc ceyiṉṭ pīṭṭars peruṅ kōyil pōlavē ikkōyilaiyum eṭuttal
   vēṇṭumeṉa eṇṇi muyaṉṟa avartam eṇṇam tuārntu pōmāṟu, araciyaṟ
   pēratikārikaḷum camayap paṇiyāḷarum piṟarum maṇṇaḷḷi eṟinta vaṇṇamē
   iruntaṉar; poruḷutavi purivōr iruḷil maṟaintaṉar; kiṟistaparukkum
   iṭukkaṇ pala aṭukki varalāyiṉa. āyiṉum eṉ? "maṭuttavāyellām pakaṭaṉṉāṉ
   uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ iṭarppāṭuṭaittu" eṉṟa tiruvaḷḷuvap perumoḻipōla,
   avviṭaiyūṟukaḷum iṭarppaṭṭut tāmē yaḻintaṉa. katīṭral kaṇkavar vaṉappum
   maṇmakiḻ tōṟṟamum peṟṟu iṉṟum niṉṟu nilavum peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ tikaḻum
   cemmai yeytiṟṟu. iṉṟum ataṉaik kāṇpōr, "periyār" eṉap poṟittuḷḷa perum
   peyaraik kāṇuntōṟum, nam kiṟistapariṉ viṉaittiṭpam, muyaṟci mutaliya
   viṉaimāṇpukaḷai niṉaintu pārāṭṭā niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
   tiruvum kalviyum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nāṭukaḷuḷ ippōtu amerikka nāṭu
   ettuṟaiyilum īṭumeṭuppumiṉṟi viḷaṅkuvataṉai nām aṟikiṉṟōm. itaṉ
   uṇmaiyiṉai yuṇarntu kaṇṭu uraitta kolampas eṉṉum periyārai mēṉāṭṭavar
   aṉaivarum, nam nāṭṭavaruṭ kaṟṟavarum naṉkaṟivar. nam nāṭṭiṟkup putuneṟi
   yoṉṟu kāṇappukunta apperiyār avvamerikka nāṭṭiṉaik kaṇṭaṟintaṉarā
   yiṉum, ataṉai mutaliṟ kāṇṭaṟkaṇ avar uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ uraikkun
   taramuṭaiyataṉṟu. putuneṟi kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉṟa eṇṇam avar uḷḷattiṟ pukunta
   nāḷ mutal, putupperu nāṭākiya ataṉait tam kaṇkaḷāṟ kāṇun tuṇaiyum,
   avarai, avamatippu, accuṟuttal, aṟiyāmai, ikaḻcci, ēmāṟṟam, vacaivu,
   kolai, accam mutaliya pala ceyalkaḷ pallāṟṟālum arittu alaittaṉa.
   nāṭṭiṉ kāṭciyai yeytutaṟku iraṇṭoru nāḻikai yirukkumpōtum, avar ceṉṟa
   vaṅkattiliruntōr avaraip paṟṟik kolai ceytuviṭavum tuṇintaṉar: malaipōl
   alaiyeḻuntu muḻaṅkum maṟikaṭal, muṉṉē kiṭantu, maruṭci viḷaivittatu;
   piṉṉē avaratu nāṭu neṭuntolaiviṟ kiṭantu, vaṟitē tirumpiṉ avarai
   ikaḻnteḷḷi iṉṉaṟ , paṭuttaṟku etirnōkkiyiruntatu; arukil cūḻa viruntōr
   maṭittavāyum, veṭitta collum, kaṭutta nōkkum uṭaiyavarāyt tīviṉaiyē
   cūḻntu koṇṭiruntaṉar.
   kaṇantōṟum, nilaiyiṉṟip puraḷum alaikaḷ avartam kalattaic celavoṭṭātu
   taṭuttaṟ keḻuntaṉa pōla veḻuntu alaippa, avaṟṟiṭaiyē oṭuṅkā vuḷḷamum
   kalaṅkāk koḷkaiyu mallatu piṟitillāta avar maṉat tiṭpam, avvalaikaḷiṉ
   talaiyai mitittēkiyatu; virikaṭalaic curukkiṟṟu; neṭumpōtu kuṟum
   pōtāka, nīrkkāṟṟu nilakkāṟṟāka, pakal māya, irā vaṇuka, viṉai muṭiviṉ
   tuṉaivu mika, amerikkap putu nāṭṭiṉ aṭaikarai pulaṉāyiṟṟu, aṭart
   taṟkeḻunta alaikaḷ avarait talaimēṟ cumantu tālāṭṭiṉa; vaṉkāṟṟāy
   maṭittaṟku niṉṟa kaṭaṟkāṟṟu, meṉkāṟṟāy avar melivu tīrttu makiḻvittatu,
   ītaṉṟō viṉaimāṇpu! eṉṟum poṉṟāta iṉpavicai nalkum - viṉaiyiṉum,
   uḻaippiṉum ēṟṟamuṭaiya toṉṟu uṇṭukol! illai! illai! eññāṉṟum illai!
   iṉi ivvuḻaippiṉai nam paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr āḷ - viṉaiyeṉṟē vaḻaṅki
   yirukkiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷattiṉ āṭci vaḻi niṟkum uṭal puriyum viṉai
   uḻaippātalāl, ataṉai āḷviṉai yeṉṟatu ettuṇai aḻakuṭaittu! kāṇmiṉ,
   avvāḷviṉaiyē āṭavarkku uyir eṉpataṉāl, āṭavaṉai āṇmakaṉ eṉṟal ettuṇai
   aṟivuṭai moḻi! kāṇmiṉ āṇmakkaḷē! uṅkaḷatu āṇmai, āḷviṉaiyē, ayarā,
   uḻaippē, ataṉaiyuṭaiya nīvir eṉ ceykiṉṟīrkaḷ? ayarntu uṟaṅkukiṉṟīrkol?
   uṟaṅkaṉmiṉ; uṟaṅkutal uyvaṟṟārkkē uriyatu, ōyvu ulaiyā uḻaippuṭaiyārkkē
   uriyatu. uḻaippavar ōyvu kōṭaṟkuriyar, ōyvuṟṟavar uṟaṅkutaṟ kuriyar;
   uṟaṅkuvōr mīṭṭum uḻaittaṟku uṭaṟkum uḷḷattiṟkum urantarupavarāvār.
   uṅkaḷatu uṟakkam mīṭṭum uḻaittal vēṇṭi uṅkaṭku ūkkam ceytaṟkamaintatu;
   ataṟkuriya pōtu iravuppōtē, ākavē, pakaṟpōtu uḻaippuk kuriyatākum.
   uḻaippukkuriya appakaṟpōtu uṟaṅkutaṟkuriya taṉṟu; ataṉāl pakaṟpōtil
   naṉku uḻaimiṉ.
   ulakam virintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu; itaṉpāl uyariya payaṉ uḷatu; uḻaippavar
   appayaṉai nukartaṟkuriyar; ulakaiyaḷitta oruvaṉ uṅkaṭku uṭampu
   nalkiyuḷaṉ; avvuṭampiṉai ulakil iyakki uṟupayaṉ peṟutaṟkuriya
   uṟukaruviyāka uṅkaṭku, avaṉ uḷḷattaiyum utaviyuḷaṉ, uḷḷattāl uḷkiyavaḻi
   uḻaittaṟ kurimaiyum, āṟṟalum uṅkaḷpāl uḷa eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. avaṟṟai
   veḷippaṭuttal uṅkaḷ kaṭaṉ. āṟṟalum urimaiyumuṭaiya nīvir
   uḻaikkumiṭattu, eṅkeṅku muṟai piṟaḻcci tōṉṟukiṉṟatō, aḵtu uṅkaḷ
   uyirkkiṟuti payakkum uṟupakaiyātaliṉ, aṅkaṅku muṟaimaiyai niṟuvutal
   vēṇṭum; nelviḷaiyum vayaliṭattup pul muḷaiya viṭuvirkol turumpāyiṉum
   virumpi pīṭṭumiṉ paruttiyiṉ pañcu kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkum puṉmaittāyiṉum viṭātu
   paṟṟumiṉ, īṭṭumiṉ; pāṅkuṟa nūṉmiṉ; aḵtu aṟṟam maṟaikkum āṭaiyākumē;
   "aṇiyellām āṭaiyiṉ piṉ.” uḻaippiṟku “ūṟu ceyvaṉa piṟavum cilavuḷa. avai
   aṟiyāmaiyum, maṭamaiyumākum. aṟitaṟkriya amaiti peṟṟum, aṟiya
   vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟai aṟiyaviṭātu, maruṭciyum accamum payappittuc ceyviṉai
   citaṟuvittaliṉ, aṟiyāmai uḻaippiṟku ūṟu ākum. aṟivuṭaiyōr
   aṟivuṟuppavum, āṟṟaṟkuriya viṉaiyum urimaiyum arukil iruppavum, āḷ
   viṉaikkaṇ karuttai iruttātu, maṭimaikkaṇ maṭintu kiṭappittaliṉ,
   maṭamaiyiṉait takart teṟitalum āṇmakaṟku aṟamākum. ataṉaic ceyyavēṇṭu
   meṉpatu āṇṭavaṉ āṇai. "iṉṟu", “ippoḻutu", “ikkaṇam” eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum
   kālamē, ataṉai ayarātu āṟṟaṟkamainta arumaik kālamākum. ayarntavaḻi
   iravu vantuviṭum; akkālattil evarum uṟaṅkuvarē yaṉṟi uḻaittalaic
   ceyyār.
   ellā vakai uḻaippum ēṟṟamuṭaiyaṉavākum; avaṟṟuḷ kaiyāl uḻaikkum
   uḻaippil kaṭavuṭṭaṉmaiyuṇṭu eṉak kārlail eṉṉum periyār kaviṉak
   kūṟukiṉṟār; ikkaṭṭuraikkaṇ aṭaṅkiya karuttukaḷai vaḻaṅkiya perum
   pulavarum avarē. uḻaippu ulakēpōl paranta paṇpiṉaiyuṭaiyatu; itaṉ
   uyarvu vīṭṭulakiṉ ucciyiluḷatu. mutaṟkaṇ uṭalilum, piṉ mūḷaiyilum,
   muṭivil uḷḷattilum oṉṟiyulavip payaṉ uṟuvikkum uḻaippē, kepḷar kaṇṭa
   kōṇilaik kaṇakkum, niyūṭṭaṉ nikaḻttiya neṭum poruḷārāycciyum,
   aṟiviyaṟkalaikaḷum, āṇmaic ceyalkaḷum iṉṉuyir vaḻaṅki icai naṭum
   iyalpum yāvum tōṟṟuvitta ēṟṟamuṭaiyatu. ātalāl, āṇ kaṭaṉ iṟukkum
   māṇpuṭai makkāḷ! uḻaippē āṇṭavaṉ uruveṉavuṇarmiṉ. ataṟku aḻiyātu āṟṟalē
   avaṉatu tiruppaṇiyeṉa aṟimiṉ; paranta ulakil ciṟantōṅkiya perumakkaḷ
   kaṇṭuṇarn turaitta mutuporuḷum ituvēyām.
   uḻaippiṉai veṟuttu nonturaikkum oruvaṉ uḷaṉō? uḷaṉāyiṉ, avaṉai atu
   ceyya vēṇṭāveṉa uraimiṉ. oṭuṅkikkiṭakkum avaṉ muṉ ceṉṟu, "aṉpa,
   oṭuṅkutal oḻika. itō, uṉpōl uḻaitta tōḻarkaḷ uyarnilai yulakattil iṉpañ
   ciṟantu iṉitē uṟaikiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷaṉṟō āṅkē iṉpuṟukiṉṟavar; piṟar
   evarum ilarē in nilavulakiṉum, uḻaippunalattāl uyarnta varaṉṟō, makkaḷ
   maṉanilattil maṉṉi, vāṉavarum muṉivararum pōlak kālaṅkaḻiyiṉum kōlaṅ
   kaḻiyātu eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavukiṉṟaṉar", eṉat teruṭṭit teḷivuṟuttumiṉ.
   ------------

                         3. varaṉeṉṉum vaippu

   uṭampoṭu kalantu tōṉṟum uyirttokaiyuṭ ciṟappuṭaiyaveṉa uyarntōrāṉ
   uṇarttappeṟum makkaḷuyirkku uṟutipporuḷeṉap periyōr vakuttuk kūṟum
   nālvakaip poruḷkaḷuḷ vīṭeṉpatum oṉṟu. atu cintaiyum moḻiyum cellā
   nilaimaittu eṉap periyōr kūṟuvar. kūṟiṉum, atu ivvulakam pōl
   aḻitaṉmālaittāya iṉpattiṉait tarātu, eññāṉṟum kuṉṟā viṉpam tarum
   pērulakameṉavum, ataṉai nāṭiyē uyirkaḷ niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉavum nam āṉṟōr
   kūṟuvataṉōṭu amaiyātu, avvulakanāṭṭam uyirkaṭku iyaṟkaiyilē
   amaintuḷateṉṟum, atu, āyum uṇarvuṭaiyārkkē pulaṉāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉar;
   kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. akkūṟṟiṉai oruvāṟu ārāyntu kaṇṭa vaḻiyē, īṇṭuk
   kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu.
   puviyiṉukku aṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷtantu, caviyuṟat teḷintu, taṇṇeṉṟu
   oḻukkamum taḻuvic cāṉṟōr kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kāṉyā ṟoṉṟiṉ
   aṭaikaraiyiṉkaṇ, veyiṉuḻaipaṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻai potumpa roṉṟiruntatu. ataṉ
   kaṇ eṇṇiṟanta puḷḷiṉam, maṇṇuṟu vāḻkkaikku māṇpuṟuttum
   ceviccuvaiyamutam kēṭpōr cevippulam vaḷam peṟa aḷittu vāḻtara,
   avviṉattu ḷoṉṟāya iṉṉicaic cempuḷoṉṟu peṭaiyuṭaṉ kiḷaimalka av
   vaṭaiciṉaip potumparuḷ, peṟṟatukoṇṭu, pēraṟam nērntu, peṟalarum iṉpamē
   tuyttu vantatu. ataṉāl, atu, eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟu eṉṉum - iṟaivaṉaruḷā
   leytum itaṉaic cempakam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.
   neñciṟumāppum, paḻiyañci yīṭṭum poruḷiṉpa muṭaimaiyiṉ, vaḻiyeñcā
   vāṉporuḷum uṭaiyatāyiṟṟu. ākavē, ataṟku iṉi vēṇṭaṟpāla teṉṉām?
   oṉṟumillai yaṉṟō!
   niṟka, orunāḷ, ic cempuḷḷiṉ vāḻkkaittuṇaiyāy, ottavuṇarvum pāṉmaiyu
   muṭaiyatāy, tuṇaiccēvaṟku oruvāṟṟāṉu muyarvutāḻvillāk kaviṉuṅ kāṭciyu
   muṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkiya peṭaippuḷ, taṉ kūṭṭakat tamarntu, pārppukkaṭku
   uṇavāvaṉavaṟṟai yaḷittu uvantirukkaiyil, pakalceyyum ceññāyiṟu pāvaiyiṟ
   paṭiya, paiya iruḷ vantu parava, iṉpatteṇkāl iṭaivantulava, arukōṭiya
   aruvikka ṇeḻunta ciṟutirai porutuvīḻtaliṉ eḻunta viḻumiya vōcaiyāṅkaṇum
   parava, virikatirmatiyam viṇṇakat teḻuntu, maṇṇaka maṭantaiyiṉ mayak
   koḻippatupōlat taṉ oṇkatir parappiṟṟu. atu kālai, ap pārppukkaḷu ḷoṉṟu,
   taṉ ciṟutalaiyai nīṭṭi, aṉpukaḷiaiyum āymuka nōkki, "aṉṉāy! vāḻi! nam
   paṭappait tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya it teṇṇīryāṟu celliṭam yātukol” eṉa
   viṉava, kēṭṭa tāyppuḷ, viṭaiyaṟiyāmaiyiṉ, viḷampal kūṭātu, kuṟunakai
   kāṭṭi, “makkāḷ! yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. kaliṉ eṉak kalikkum tūkkaṇaṅkuruviyaiyātal,
   kaṭpulaṅkatuvā viṭpulam paṭarum vāṉampāṭiyai yātal kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. āṇṭut
   tōṉṟum kōṭṭeṅkiṉ kulaimītu avai orukāl vantamariṉum amarum. atu pōḻtu
   nīvir maṟavaṟka. nīvir kēṭṭatum, ataṉōraṉṉa piṟavum aṟitalām eṉṟu kūṟi,
   vāṉampāṭiyiṉ vaḷḷicaiyait taṉ meṉkuralāl, vaṭittum untiyum uṟaḻntum
   icaippa, kurīikkuñcukaḷ kuḷirppeyti, “vāṉampāṭikol ik kāṉam pāṭiyatu”
   eṉṟeṇṇalāyiṉa.
   maṟṟu, av vāṉampāṭum vaṇkuruku, taṉ mellicai koṇṭu, kāṭum, kāvum,
   kaviṉpeṟu turuttiyum, yāṟum kuḷaṉum, vēṟupal vaippum, ūrum nakaramum
   uṟaitarum illamum, ūṟucey vilaṅkum, ūṉuṇ vēṭṭuvar virikkum kaṇṇiyum,
   piṟavum meypeṟa vicaittal marapu. ataṉaiyoru kālēṉum arukamarntu naṉku
   kēṭṭaṟiyā ic cempuḷ, pāṭuṅkāl, av vicaipporuḷ muṟaiyiṟ ciṟitu
   piṟaḻtalum kuṟaitalumāyiṉa. iḻukkuṭaiya pāṭṭāyiṉum, icai naṉṟāyiṉmai
   koṇṭu pēruvakai mīturat tāmum, tam aṉṉaiyoppa, mellicai miḻaṟṟal
   toṭaṅkiṉa. iṅṅaṉam kuravarāyiṉār ceyyum ceyviṉaiyai makkaḷāyiṉār,
   nuṇuki nōkki, ammuṟaiyē ceytal uyirttokaiyuṭ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum iyalpukaḷ
   palaviṉuḷoṉṟu. ituvē, ēṉaiyaṟivuvaḷarccikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum orutalaiyāka
   vamainta aṭippaṭai yeṉpatu kalviyāḷar kōḷ. itaṉai yīṇṭuraippiṟ perukum.
   veḷḷi muḷaittatu; viṭiyal vantatu; paṟavai pāṭiṉa; palluyirum
   paravukkaṭaṉ ceytaṉa; viḻunta ñāyiṟum eḻuntatu; meṉkāṟṟu vīcaluṟṟatu;
   pūnāṉkum potuḷi veṟi ticai nāṉkum pōyulava, curumpu tōairntaṉa.
   cēkkaiyuḷuṟaṅkik kiṭanta cempuṭkuñcukaḷ, uṟakka nīṅki, orupuṭai vantu,
   uvakai kūrntaṉa. atupōtu, tāyppuḷ, taṉ celvac ciṟu kuñcukaḷiṉ
   maruṅkamarntu, taṉ mellicaiyait toṭuttu, yāṟṟunīrccelaviṉmēṟ pāṭiṟṟu.
   akkālai karaiyāk kallum pullum kaṉinturukiṉa. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ, avvicai
   yiṉpattāl,
   “maruviyakāl vicaittacaiyā; maraṅkaḷmalarc ciṉaicaliyā;
   karuvaraivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kalittōṭā;
   perumukiliṉ kulaṅkaḷpuṭai peyarntoḻiyap puṉalcōrā;
   iruvicumpiṉ iṭaimuḻaṅkā; eḻukaṭalum iṭaituḷumpā.”
   eṉun tīṅkaviyē amaiyum. -
   iṅṅaṉam ciṟantōṅkiya icaiyoṉṟil, ap puḷ vīṟuṟṟatē yoḻiya, ataṉuḷattil,
   ciṟukuñcu viṉaviya poruḷmāttiram pulaṉāyiṟṟaṉṟu. āyiṉum tāṉum
   taṉpārppukkaḷum orupakal avvāṟu celliṭam cēṟal vēṇṭu meṉṉum tippiya
   vuṇarvoṉṟu ataṉ neñcat takalātu nilavuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu nāṭkaḷ pala
   ceṉṟaṉa. appuḷḷum yāṟṟunīrccelavu poruḷākap pāṭi vantamaiyai nīkkip
   piṟitōr iṭamoṉṟaṉai nāṭip pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkālai avviṭamē, ivvāṟu
   celliṭamām eṉpatu ataṟkut tuṇipāyiṟṟu. ivviṭattaik kēṭkuntoṟum ap
   pārppukkaḷum tam tāyppaṟavaiyai, ataṉaik kuṟitta viṉākkaḷ pala
   nikaḻttātu oḻivatiṉṟu.
   niṟka, ivviṭam yātāka virukkalām? cilar itaṉai nāmyāṇṭiruntu pōntōmō
   āṇṭaiyatu eṉpar. vēṟu cilar nām iṉic cellumiṭam yātu atu veṉpar. cilar,
   ittakaiya iṭamē illai yeṉpar. vēṟu palar nāmum piṟavum nīṅkiyatum,
   nīkkamaṟa iṉic cēruvatu māya iṭam eṉpar. itupaṟṟik kūṟaṟpālaṉa oruvāṟu
   kōṭaṟpāla valla veṉpatu kāṭci yoṉṟaiyē aḷavaiyāk koḷvār tuṇipu. maṟṟu,
   nām kāṇṭal, karutal, urai yeṉṉu mūṉṟaiyum aḷavaiyāk koḷḷum pāṉmaiyē
   mākalāṉum, avaṟṟāṉārāyuṅ kālattu, uḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟum cila vuṇarvukaḷum,
   uṇarntōr palar kūṟiya uraikaḷum appeṟṟittāya iṭamoṉṟuṇṭeṉpataṉai
   valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, ataṉai oruvāṟu nām kāṇṭaṟkumuṉ aṟiyāvulakam
   eṉa vaḻaṅkuvōm. mutaṉūlārum ivvāṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. aṉṟiyum, ivvulakaip
   paṟṟi, iruṇṭa vuṇarvukaḷē ap paṟavaiyiṉ uṇarvuṭ kalantu kiṭantaṉa.
   iṉi, at tāyppuḷ, tāṉē taṉittamarntu, avvulaku paṟṟiya vuṇarvukaḷ taṉ
   maṉatteḻuntōṟum, icaittuk kuḷirntu nāvil ūṟāta amiḻtūṟa. uṭal puḷakittu
   uḷḷamellām urukik kaḷikkum. ik kaḷiyāṭṭiṉaip piṉṉar atu, taṉ pārppukkaḷ
   kāṇavum kēṭkavum ceytayartal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuk kāraṇam
   yātākum? ap pārppukkaḷum itaṉait toṭarvaṉavallavō? avaṟṟiṟkum avvulaka
   vuṇarviṉai yuṇarttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? atu pōlum kāraṇam!
   iv vaṇṇam nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. orunāḷ ak kuñcukaḷu ḷoṉṟu, āymukanōkki,
   av vulaku yāṇṭuḷa teṉṟu kēṭpa, atu, “avvulakam uṇṭeṉpatu uṇmai: maṟṟu,
   yāṉ aḵtu uṟumiṭam aṟiyēṉ. ākaliṉ,
   ----
   “I may call it the Unknown Land."
   -------------
   avviṭattai yāṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu" eṉṟatu. eṉṟalum, avaṟṟuṭṭalaik kuñcu
   muṉṉiṉṟu, "iv yāṟu cellumiṭam yātu, atu av aṟiyāvulakam!" eṉṟatu .itu
   uṇmai kol? aṉṟu. av yāṟu, maṟuvilmāṉavar malinta mūtūr veṟitu cēṟal
   viḻuppamataṉṟeṉa malaipaṭuporuḷum kāṉpaṭu celvamum talaimaṇantukoṇṭu,
   perunakaraṭaintu, kālvaḻic ceṉṟu, vayaliṭaip paṭarntu, ataṉkaṇ viḷaiyum
   paiṅkūḻ vaḷarttu, vaṇikar marakkalam aṇipeṟa mitappa, maṅkaiyar
   kuṭaintu māṇalam puṉaiya, purinūl mārpar aruṅkaṭaṉāṟṟa, mīṉuṇkurukum,
   tuṇṭil vēṭṭuvarum, tiṇṭimilvāḻnarum celvam mīkkoḷap parantu ceṉṟu,
   kōṉōkki vāḻuṅ kuṭiyaṉaittum vāṉōkkaliṉ, ataṟkiṉṟiyamaiyāp perumaḻai
   piṟakkum peruntiraip puṇariyaip puṇarum! ippuṇariyō aṟiyā
   ulakeṉappaṭuvatu pakaliṉ pāṉmaiyum iraviṉ eḻilum puḷḷiṉattuk
   korupolivum māviṉattukkorumāṇpum vayaṅkat taruvaṉavāka, ivaiyaṉaittum
   tanta voruvaṉ, ivai celliṭamoṉṟaic ceyyātu viṭuvaṉō?
   itu niṟka, "aṉṉāy aṟiyāvulakiṉ aḻakiṉaik kūṟuvatāl ākumpyaṉ yātukol
   ataṉaip palakālum pāṭip pukaḻtal eṟṟukku?" eṉṟu toṭaṅkiya oru kuṟumpuḷ.
   "tēṉuāṟṟeṭuppa maṇanāṟum pūmpoḻilakattaiviṭṭu nām vēṟiṭam cēṟal eṉṉai?
   meṉṟaḷirum naṟumalarum mikaipaṭac ceṟinta kūṭoṉṟākki, nām ēṉ eṉṟum īṇṭē
   vāḻtal kūṭātu? vāṉkuyilka ḷicaipāṭa varivaṇṭu pāṇmiḻaṟṟa, māṉkaṉṟu
   payiṉṟuḷḷa, palluyirum poliveyta viḷaṅkum ivviḷamarakkāviṉum iṉitukol
   av aṟiyāvulakam ? āruyiraṉṉāy! nām vēṟiṭañcēṟal vēṇṭā. itupōtu,
   cintāmaṇi teṇkaṭalamirtam tillaiyāṉaruḷāl vantālikaḻappaṭumē
   muyaṟciyiṉṟiyē perum payaṉ eytutaṟkuriya iṭam atuvāyiṉum, yāṉ ivviṭam
   viṭṭup peyarēṉ. oḻika. iṉi vēṟupulañ cēṟal. paintāṭ kōraiyum, paciya
   taḷirniṟainta putalum mikka iv vaṭaikaraiyiṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam
   ākaliṉ, aṉṉāy! iṉippāṭutaloḻika! pāṭutaṟku uḷam oruppaṭutalum oḻika!"
   eṉṟatu.
   piṉṉar attāyppuḷ, taṉmaṉattup palavēṟuvakaippaṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷ paṭarnteḻun
   taṭaṅka, veytuyirttu, vēṟu kūṟā toḻiya, maṟṟoru ciṟukuruku kūṟum. atu,
   ‘iṟutta iruḷ keṭa, yāṇṭum iḷaṅkālum, iṉṉoliyum eḻa, poṉṉurukkeṉṉa
   iḷaveyil parappi, viṭiyaliṟ kīḻkkaṭaliṉ mukaṭṭeḻum iḷañāyiṟṟiṉaik
   kāṇāy! teṇṇiriṉ tiraiyiṭai, acceññāyiṟṟiṉ ceṅkatirkaḷ tavaḻtalum, āṇṭup
   pal vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi miḷirtal kāṇāy! varikkeṇṭaiyum paruvarālum
   naṇpakaloḷiyil miṉceytu maṟaitalum, iḷantaḷirkaḷ oḷiteṟittalum piṟavum
   kāṇāy! taṇṭeṉṟal taḷiracaippa, pārppukkaḷ kiḷaipaṭarntu icaipāṭa,
   putumalariṉ maṇam parava, kālaiyaṇintu pakalellām oḷiyiṉṟiyirunta
   kaviṉaṉaittum, mālaiyiṟṟuvara muṭittut tōṉṟum iyalaṇaṅkiṉ eḻiṟ cevviyai
   maṟattalum kūṭumā? īṇṭāyiṉum, āṇṭāyiṉum, yāṇṭāyiṉum amarka. veṇmatiyiṉ
   taṇkatirkaḷ pain taḻaikaḷiṉmītu paravi, kalittōṭum aruvinīril āṭal
   payiṉṟu, pūṅkāvai vāṉamākki, kāṉyāṟṟai, attiṅkaṭ puttē ḷurntu cellun
   tērvaḻiyākka, viḷaṅkum iravup polivai niṉaittoṟum neñcam
   vēṟōriṭattaiyum niṉaikkuṅ kol tiṇkalamum ciṟunāvāyum celvuḻiccelvuḻi
   yeḻum oyyeṉum ōcai nam uṟakkam keṭuppa, neṭuntaruviṉ paiṅkiḷai paṭarntu
   pāṭalai nam iṉam payila, iṉpakkāṭciyē yāṇṭum ilaṅkum in nilattiṉum av
   aṟiyānilam aḻakuṭaittō?
   "tuñcuvatu pōla iruḷi, viṇpaka
   imaippatu pōla miṉṉi, uṟaikkoṇ(ṭu)
   ēṟuvatu pōlap pāṭuciṟan(tu) uraii
   nilaneñ(cu) uṭka ovātu cilaittāṅ(ku)
   ārtaḷi poḻinta vārpeyaṟ kaṭaināḷ.”
   nām nam paṭaiyamai cēkkaiyaip paṭarntu, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu pulli irukkiṉ
   kārceypaṇippum kalaṅkutuyar ceyyātāka, eḻunta vemmaiyāl iṉpam mikum
   ivvirum poḻiliṉum, nī kūṟum appūm poḻil ēṟṟamuṭaittāmō? ātaliṉ, aṉṉāy
   vēṟupulam muṉṉutalai viṭṭoḻittu, ippulam paṟṟiya iṉṉicaiyē miḻaṟṟuti!’
   eṉṟatu.
   piṉṉar, attāyāya cempuḷ, iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya taṉ celvakkuñciṉ peruñcoṟkaḷaik
   kēṭṭu, oruvāṟu, uḷattait tirutti, “celvaṅkāḷ iṉi nīvir virumpiya
   vaṇṇamē vēṟoru pāṭṭē pāṭuval. pōṟṟik kēṇmiṉ eṉṟu oru mellicai, poruḷ
   ceṟiyap pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. apporuḷ:
   “paranta ulakiṭait tōṉṟiya yāvum taṉ poṟipulaṉkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē maṉattiṟkuk
   kuṉṟāviṉpamum, accamaṟiyā viyalpum nalkat tāṉ peṟṟuvāḻntamaiyum, av
   vāḻkkaiyil nāṭpala cella, taṉakku uḷḷiruntē, iḵtu niṉṉiṭamaṉṟu eṉa
   vōrporuṇmoḻi yeḻunticaittamaiyum; ammoḻi, piṉ palamuṟaiyum icaippak
   kēṭṭa tāṉ, orukāl uvakaiyum, orukāl veṟuppum, palakālum kēṭṭaṟ
   koruppaṭātu cēṟalum koṇṭiruntamaiyum; irukkaiyil, tāṉ vāḻumiṭamē
   mētakka iṭamāvatu, ataṉai nīṅkutal aṟavē kūṭātu eṉṉum uvakaiyuṇarvu
   tōṉṟiṉamaiyum; piṉṉarttaṉ maṉakkiṉiya tuṇaippaṟavai pōtantu, kātal
   ceytu kaḷittuk kalantamaiyum; atukālai, ammoḻip poruḷ paṉmuṟaiyum
   eḻuntu, ūṉkalantu, uṭalkalantu, uyirkalantu, piṉṉar uṇarvuṅ kalantu
   orōvōcaiyāy uruveṅkuṅ kalantuniṉṟamaiyum; akkalappē, taṉ
   tuṇaippuḷḷiṟkum nikaḻa, iraṇṭum uḷaṅkalantu ikkāṉyāṟṟaṭaikarai
   yaṭaintu, ciṟṟiṟ camaittuc cevviya vāḻkkai naṭātti vantamaiyum
   piṟavumām.
   itaṉaikkēṭṭa pārppiṉuḷ oṉṟu, "aṟṟēl, aṉṉāy! nī paṇṭē yirunta iṭam
   yāṇṭaiyatu? arukuḷatāyiṉ, yām aṉaivarum āṇṭē paṭarntu, iṉpantuyttu
   iṉitu vāḻalāmē!” eṉṟatu. eṉavum, tāyppuḷ uḷaṅkalaṅki, āruyirc celvamē!
   ataṉaiyeṅkuḷateṉpēṉ aṟiyāvulaka meṉṭēṉō aṉṟi mikamikac cēymaiyiluḷḷatōr
   viṇṇiṭam eṉpēṉō yātu kūṟuvēṉ! yāṉ aṟiyēṉ.
   āyiṉum, aṉṟeḻun ticaitta poruṇmoḻi iṉṟu meḻuntu īrkiṉṟatu. atu aṉṟiṭṭa
   āṇaiyai mēṟ koṇṭaṭaṅkiya yāṉ iṉṟu viṭuttal kūṭumō?
   ayarāviṉpattiṟṟiḷaittirunta aṉṟē avvāṇaiyai yēṟṟa yāṉ, iḷamaiyum,
   ataṉāṉām iṉpamum iḻanta innilaiyiṟ koḷḷātoḻital muṟaiyō? ākavē, eṉ
   arumaic celvaṅkaḷē! av aṟiyāvulakam yāṇṭamain tiruppiṉum irukka. nām
   aṉaivarum ataṉainōkkic celvam. vammiṉ yāṉ kūṟuvatu uṇmaiyē eṉṉum
   uṟutinōkkaṅ (Faith) koṇṭu oruppaṭumiṉ’ eṉṟatu.
   eṉṟalum, “nīyum emmoṭu varutiyaṉṟē. āyiṉ, yāṉ iṉṉē varuval” eṉṟu
   mikacciṟiyatōr pārppu viḷampit taṉ tāyicaitta taṉiyicaiyiṟ ṟāṉuṅ
   kalantu pāṭal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
   iṉṉaṇam, collāṭalālum icaiyālum poḻutu kaḻiya, “veñcuṭar veppan tīrat
   taṇṇaṟuñcōlai tāḻntu niḻaṟceyyavum, taṇpatampaṭṭa teṇkaḻi mēyntu
   palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa puḷḷellām kuṭampai nōkki uṭaṅkupeyaravum, puṉṉai
   mutaliya pūviṉāṟṟam muṉṉiṉṟu kañaṟṟavum, neṭuntirai yaḻuvattu
   nilākkatir parappavum’ mālai vantatu. varakkaṇṭa tāyppuḷ, taṉ
   pārppukkaḷait tamittuviṭṭu iraitēṭuvāṉ ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟuḷoṉṟu
   aṇmaiyilōṭiya aruviyiṉ maruṅkuniṉṟa putaṟkaṇamarntu, aruviyiṉ kalippum,
   aṭaiciṉai yacaitali ṉeḻu mārppum kēṭṭalum kaḷimīkkūrntu, aṅku miṅku
   mōṭiyōṭit taṉṉallicai koṇṭu iṉṉicai miḻaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkālai atu
   icaikka muyaṉṟa icaikaḷ palavum iyaipupaṭātoḻiya, iṟutiyiṟ ṟaṉṟāy
   pāṭiya aṟiyāvulakavicaiyai iyakkippārkka, atu naṉkaṉam kaivaravuvantu
   ataṉaiyē eṭuttum paṭuttum iṉitu pāṭik koṇṭiruntatu.
   "ā! yāḻō! kuḻalō!! iṉṟēṉ perukkō!!! eṉpelām urukkum iṉṉicai
   miḻaṟṟavallatu yātukol! ā! tarukkaḷum caliyā; munnīrc calatiyum kaliyā,
   nīṇṭa poruppiṉiṉṟum iḻiyum aruvikkālum natikaḷum puraṇṭu tuḷḷā;
   epporuḷaiyum taṉvayamākki, uḷniṟai yuyirum meyyum urukkukiṉṟatē!’ eṉap
   pukaḻvatu pōlum iḻittakka kūṟikkoṇṭē puṟavamoṉṟu āṇṭu ataṉarukē
   vantatu. itu puṟaṅkuṉṟi kaṇṭaṉaiyatēṉum mūkkiṟ karumaiyu muṭaittu;
   aṉṟiyum oṇmai yuṭaiyam yām eṉṉum veṇmai talai ciṟantatu. ekkuṭikeṭukvō,
   etaṉalam tūṟṟavō īṇṭu iḵtu vantuḷatu. itaṉiyalpaṟiyāc ciṟucempuḷ, tāṉ
   kēṭṭa itaṉ poyyuṟu pukaḻcciyai meyyuṟu pukaḻcciyeṉ ṟeṇṇi, peritum
   muyaṉṟu pāṭalum, immaṭappuṟavam, taṉ ciṟutalaiyaip paiyattuḷakki,
   ‘naṉṟu, naṉṟu, yāṉum pala viṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟuḷēṉ. pala iṉṉicaikaḷāṟ
   ṟōṭkap peṟṟu muḷēṉ. orukāl, makkaḷakattup pōṟṟi vaḷarkkap peṟum pēṟum
   peṟṟuḷēṉ. eṉavē, icainalaṅ kāṇṭaliṟ ciṟanta vaṉmai peṟṟuḷēṉ eṉpatu
   naṉku tōṉṟum. yāṉaṟintavaḷavil, āṇaiyiṭṭuk kūṟuvēṉ, niṉṉoppār ‘
   nallicai vallār makkaḷiṉum ilar. āyiṉum oṉṟu, niṉṉāṟ pāṭappeṟum
   poruḷkaḷ yāvai? avai yāṉ itu kāṟuṅ kēṭṭaṉavallavē!!’ eṉac ciṟitu
   cuḷittu viṉaviṟṟu.
   kuṟumpuḷ:- ā. avai yāvum aṟiyāvulaku paṟṟiyaṉa.
   puṟavam :- evai? aṟiyā...
   kuṟumpuḷ:- avai aṟiyā vulakiṉ aḻaku, iṉpam, vāḻkkaiyiyalpu,
   ataṉaiyaṭaiyumāṟu mutaliyavaṟṟaik kūṟuvaṉa?
   puṟavam :-(nakaittu) atu aṟiyāvulakāyiṟṟē! ataṉaip paṟṟi nī
   aṟintatiyāṅṅaṉam? ammamma muṟṟāk kāyellām muḻutta kēḷvi
   vallavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kāṭum ceṭiyum avāvaṟuttal meyyuṇartal mutaliyaṉa
   ceykiṉṟaṉavē! meyyāka, yāṉ ivaṟṟai yaṟital vēṇṭum. mūttu mutirnta
   mutumai mikka yāṉ, avvulakiṉaiyaṟintu āvaṉa ceytukōṭal vēṇṭumē! naṉṟu!
   aṟiyāvulakameṉṟu nī kūṟuvatu yātu? yāṇṭuḷatu? aruḷceytu kūṟuka.
   kuṟumpuḷ:- yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. (uṇarvukalaṅki) orunāḷ nāṅkaḷ avvulakiṉukkuc
   cēṟalkūṭum eṉpataiyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum yāṉ aṟiyēṉ.
   puṟavam :- naṉṟu. aṟintēṉ. pētaiyōr pētaiyōr eṉa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
   avar yāvar, yāṇṭuḷar eṉa āyntukoṇṭē varutal eṉ iyaṟkai. aṉpē iṉṟu
   kaṇṭēṉ. niṉmāṭṭē annūlkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu vāymaiyātal kaṇṭēṉ. nīyē ataṟkut
   takka cāṉṟu. ākavē, nīviṟ aṟiyāvulakiṟkuc cellum celavu mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷīr.
   āṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉṟiyē pōlum appeṟṟittāya celavu nī nayantaṉirāyiṉ, num
   naṉṉar ceñcattu iṉṉacai vāyppa, muṉṉiya yāvaiyum iṉṉē peṟuka! yāṉ
   varuval eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭē paṟantu ciṟitu cella, ak kuṟumpuḷ,
   virutuppaṭṭikku viraintu pōṅ kaliyaik kuṟukkiṭṭu uṟuvilai koṭuttu
   vāṅkiyāṅku, “niṟka ciṟitu tāḻttal vēṇṭum. naṉṟu, tāṅkaḷ kūṟiyatu yātu?
   eṉ aṟivu mayaṅkukiṉṟatu” eṉṟatu.
   puṟavam :- aṟiyāvulakattiṟku amaintuḷḷa nūntam celavu mētakkatē.
   āyiṉum, iṭaippaṭṭa eṉakkum uṉakkumē ac celavu ceytaṟkēṟṟa neṟi tōṉṟātu
   pōlum.
   kuṟumpuḷ:- ō, ataṉai viṭuka. avvulakupaṟṟiya ummuṭaiya kōḷ yātu? ataṉai
   yēṉum kūṟuka.
   puṟavam :- eṉṉai? ē turumpē! eṉṉaiyō ikaḻkiṉṟaṉai. naṉṟu! uṉṉotta
   cīriya icaip pulamaiyum, nuṭpamum tiṭpamumamaintu aṟivumuṭaiya
   evvuyirēṉum aṟiyāta oṉṟiṉmāṭṭu veṟitē niṉaintu cāmpuṅkol vēṇṭiṉ, nī
   niṉṉaiyē koḷḷalām. uṉṉaippōl, vāḷātu poḻutu kaḻippatāyiṉ eṉṉaiyum
   koḷḷalām. oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ; yāvarukkum olluva tollum eṉpatē ciṟantatu.
   mikuttuk kūṟal mikka ētamē tarum.
   kuṟumpuḷ :- aṟṟēl, nīr av vaṟiyāvulakiṟkup pōkīr pōlum!
   puṟavam :- orukālum illai. mutaṟkaṇ, yāṉuṟaiyu miṭam, muḻaṅkukaṭal
   ñālam muḻuvatum variṉum, vaḻaṅkat tavā vaḷappamum, viḻaivu viṭutta
   viḻumiyōrkkum viḻaivu tōṟṟuvikkum viḻuppamum vāyntatu. ākaliṉ yāṉ
   vēṟiṭam virumpēṉ. mēlum, peṟṟatu koṇṭu peṟum pēṟē pēṟeṉap paṭuvatu.
   iraṇṭāvatāka, kēṭkappaṭuvaṉa yāvum vāymaiya eṉa eḷitu ārāyātu koḷḷum
   nūmmōraṉṉōr pōlum ciṟṟaṟivuṭaiyēṉ yāṉalaṉ. aṉṟiyum, nīr kūṟum
   ulakamoṉṟu uṇṭeṉpataiyēṉum yāṉ eṅṅaṉam aṟivatu?
   kuṟumpuḷ :- eṉṉai īṉṟōr eṉakku aruḷiṉar.
   puṟavam :-īṉṟōrkaḷō kūṟiṉar aṅṅaṉamā aṟṟēl, nī mikamikac cīriyatōr
   paṟavaiyē! niṉ īṉṟōr kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟaik kūṟiyavāṟē uḷaṅkoḷum nīrmai
   vēṇṭaṟpālatē avarkaḷ orupakal, nī ceññāyiṟṟaic ceṉṟu cērntu vāḻtaluṅ
   kūṭum eṉparāyiṉ, nī āmeṉṟē koḷḷuvāy pōlum!
   kuṟumpuḷ :-(cīṟiyatu pōl) īṉṟu puṟantanta eṉ kuravar orupoḻutēṉum eṉṉai
   vañcittatiṉṟē!
   puṟavam :-eṉṉai, yeṉṉai vekuḷuvateṉṉai? eṉṉai ceyti niṉpeṟṟōr niṉṉai
   vañcittaṉar; vañcar eṉṟu oruvarum kūṟiṟṟilarē! avar, niṉṉai
   vañcittilarāyiṉ, oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ, avar pētaiyarē. aṉṟēl, poruḷaṟiyātārē.
   nūmmōraṉṉōr māṭṭu oṉṟukūṟal viḻaivōr, uṇmaiyil, uḷanēry kōṭal ulakiyal,
   'ēvavum ceykalāṉ, tāṉtēṟāṉ, avvuyir pōomaḷavum ōr nōy.' niṟka, avar
   kūṟṟu vañcaṉaiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, poruḷaṟiyāmaiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, nīyē
   ārāyka. ulakiyalum uṇmaiyaṟivu milarōṭu uḷḷōr uraiyāṭal oruvāṟṟāṉum
   ollātākaliṉ, yāṉ ceṉṟu varuval. vāḻka! vāḻka!! (maṟaikiṉṟatu.). iṅṅaṉam
   am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya coṟkaḷāṟ ṟaṉ uṇmaiyaṟivu kalakkaṅ koṇṭa kuṟumpuḷ
   oru vāṟu teḷitaṟkuḷ, atu kaṭpulaṅ kaṭantatu.
   maṟupakal, vēṉil veppamum, vevviya kāṟṟum, ilavāka, taṉ taṉmaiyilōr
   tiripuṇmaiyaik kāṭṭiṟṟu. nīṉiṟa vāṉam māṉiṟaṅ koḷḷak koṇmūviṉam viṇ
   muṭiṉa. kārkōḷ mukanta kamañcūl māmaḻai vaḷḷuṟai citaṟit talaippeyal
   peytatāka, karaiporu tiraṅkum kāṉyāṟuvāṉ yāṟoppap perukkiṭṭōṭi,
   kuḷirppu mikutalum, uṟaintu paṭa, māmēyal maṟantaṉa; manti kūrntaṉa;
   paṟavaipaṭivaṉa vīḻntaṉa; kaṟavai kaṉṟukō ḷoḻintaṉa; kuṭāvaṭi uḷiyam
   mutaliyaṉa peruṅkal viṭaraḷaiyiṟ ceṟintaṉa. āmā nallēṟu cilaittaṉa;
   kāṟṟuṟ ṟeṟitalāl mayiliṉam vīṟṟuviṟ ṟōṭiṉa. iṭampeṟa vamainta
   kuṭampaikaḷ, paṟavaiyum pārppum uṟaiyuḷiṉṟoḻiya, nilampaṭa vīḻntaṉa.
   varaṉeṉum vaippil uraṅkoṇṭa aṉpu pūṇṭa cempuḷḷiṉ cēkkaimaṭṭil ūṟoṉṟiṉṟi
   iṉitiruntatu. ataṉuḻai yirunta kuñcukaḷ iv vaṉaittuṅ kāṇṭalum,
   peruviyappuṟṟu, pakalōṉ karantaṉaṉ; paṉi mikuntuḷatu eṉkol eṉum eṇṇan
   talaikkoṇṭiruppa, oṉṟu, "peruvāṉam virikati riṉṟiyum, mukiṟkulam
   paravappeṟṟum uḷatu! teṇṇīryāṟu maṇṇīrmaiyuṟṟu acaiviṉṟi yuḷatu
   pakalavaṉ oḷiyē yiṉṟip, pārmuṟṟum paciya iruḷ ceṟiyappeṟṟuḷatu eṉṉō”
   eṉṟatu.
   maṟṟum oṉṟu:- nerunal uḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟillai yeṉṉum perumai ivvulaku
   uṭaittaṉṟō!
   tāyppuḷ:- (nakaittu) ām! āyiṉum, virikatirc celvaṉai nām nāḷai kāṇṭal
   kūṭum. iḵtuṇmai! niṟka, ikkālattup pakaṟpoḻutu kuṉṟivarutalai nīvir
   aṟintilīr pōlum! iṉṟeḻunta puyalē ippakaṟ poḻutiṉaik kuṟaittatu;
   ātalāṟṟāṉ, ceññāyiṟṟiṉ veṅkatirum, im mukilvaḻi nuḻaintu veyileṟittal
   kūṭātu pōyiṟṟu. itukiṭakka. it talaippeyal nam kuṭampai muṟṟum
   īrañceytiṟ ṟiṉṟākaliṉ, uḷḷē vammiṉ kuḷirppu nīṅka, yāṉ ap perum peyarc
   celaviṉmēṟ pāṭṭoṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟēṉ. pētaiyir, nīvir ip pakaṟ poḻutē eṉṟum
   kāṇṭal kūṭu meṉa niṉaikkiṉṟīrkollō?
   in nilavulakiṉ kāṭcinalaṉait tūkki yuraitta ciṟupuḷ muṉpōntu, "aṉṉāy!
   yāṉ, paṇṭu, eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭu meṉṟē eṇṇiṉēṉ, niṉaittēṉ; maṟṟu, iṉṟē yāṉ
   ataṉkaṇṇum tiripuṇmai kaṇṭēṉ. āyiṉum yāṉ ataṟku iṉi añcēṉ paruti
   paravai paṭiya, karumukil peru maḻai poḻiya, vāṉaṅ karuka, kāṉyāṟu
   perukiṉ varum nōytīr maruntum aṟintuḷēṉ. atu, nām aṭaiya virumpum
   aṟiyāvulakiṉ āynta nallicai. kēṭṭa tāy, uvakai kiḷarnteḻa, nuṉikkompa
   rēṟi, kēṭkum ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyir uḷḷiṭṭa yāvum
   meypaṇikkumvaṇṇam, tēmpiḻipōlum iṉṉicai miḻaṟṟiṟṟu. atukālai, ataṉōṭu
   cēvaṟpuḷḷum, ciṟupuḷḷum otticaittu uvakaittēṉ cuvaittaṉa. yāvum
   otticaittaṉa veṉiṉum oṉṟumaṭṭil antō uḷam oruppaṭātu, uvakaiyuṅ kūrātu
   veṟutta nōkkoṭuniṟpa, icaipperukku niṉṟatu. niṟṟalum atu, ēṉaic
   ciṟupuṭkaḷai viḷittu, "ivvicai, itaṉiṉum pattaṭuttatām, ataṉuṭ
   kūṟappeṟum ulakupaṟṟiya yāvum uḷḷavāṟu uṇarappeṟiṉ" eṉṟatu. ivvāṟē,
   pirivu kāraṇamākat tuyaruṟ ṟirunta peṭaicciṟupuḷḷoṉṟu, nakaittu,
   "ataṉaip paṟṟi nām mikaipaṭa vuṇarvēmāyiṉ, īṇṭai nalattāṉ namakku
   maṉavamaiti yuṇṭākā taṉṟō!" eṉṟatu. ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭa maṟṟoṉṟu, “ām, nām
   avvulakucārpāka oṉṟum aṟintilē maṉṟō uṇmaiyil, aṅṅaṉamōr ulaku
   uṇṭeṉpatai yēṉum eṅṅaṉam aṟitalām?" eṉṟalum, maṟṟoru peṭaippuḷ, "aṉṟu,
   aṅṅaṉa moṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatai nām naṉku uṇarkiṉṟōm. yātō? nam aṉṉai kūṟiya
   uḷḷoli eṉṉuḷ eḻunticaittatu! yāṉ uṇarntēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
   ik kūṟṟiṉaik kēṭṭa talaikkuñcu, "uṉṉuḷ eḻunticaittatāka nī
   niṉaittaṉaikollō atu tāṉē ā ivaiyāvum veṟṟuraikaḷē. iṅṅaṉam niṉṉaik
   kūṟumāṟu nam aṉṉai kūṟiṉāḷpōlum ammamma! nī kūṟuvaṉavum, aṉṉai
   kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavum vīṇ poruḷil yāppukkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟitillai. nuṇṇiya
   kēḷviyaṟivu mikiṉum iyaṟkaiyaṟivu kūṭiṉaṉṟō atu māṭci peṟum yāṉum
   appullaṟivē mēṟkoṇ ṭiruppiṉ, av vuḷḷoli eṉṉuḷḷu meḻunticaitta teṉpēṉ.
   iṉi yāṉ av vulaku cēṟum celavaiyum niṉaiyēṉ. cēṟaṟku uṭaṉpaṭalum paṭēṉ.
   celka" eṉac ciṟitu vekuṇṭuraittatu.
   “iḵtaṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam’ eṉpatu poruḷāka vamainta nallicai yoṉṟai
   eṭutticaittavaṇṇam, kuṭampaiyiṉ puṟattē tāy vara, akattirunta
   pārppukkaḷuṭ cila, tāy pāṭi yataiyē tāmum pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉa. annilaiyil,
   at tāyp puḷḷiṉ uḷḷum av voli yeḻuntatu. eḻavē, maṟumuṟaiyum, atu an
   nallicaiyaiyē toṭaṅki, “iḻumeṉōtaiyiṉ aruvi yiraṅkalum, maruṅku karai
   koṉṟu kāṉyāṟu cēṟalum, viṇṇakaṅ kaṭantu koṇmū vēkalum, mālvarai
   tuḷaṅkak kālporu tōṭalum, virikatirpparuti paravaiyiṉ maṟaitalum
   epporuḷ nāṭic ceykiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇamāvatu, avaṟṟi ṉakatteḻuntu,
   “iḵtu aṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam” eṉ ṟolikkum aruḷoli yaṉṟō yāṟṟoṭu kūṭuka
   mukiloṭu moḻika kāliṉai viṉavuka! nīvir celliṭam yāteṉṟu aṟiyāvulakamē!
   varaṉeṉum vaippēyaṉṟō! 'nī celliṭam yā teṉa veyilavaṉai viḷittuk
   kēṇmiṉ. avaṉ cēṟumiṭaṉum ap peṟalarum ulakē! kuṟitta nāḷ varuṅkāl nām
   aṉaivarum āṇṭup paṭarkuvam" eṉa muṭittatu.
   iv vicaivayappaṭṭu maṉantiruntiya attalaikkuñcu, “aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy! nī
   kūṟuvatu vāymai kollō yāṉ koḷḷumāṟu yātu? av vulakam uṟumiṭam
   yāṇṭaiyatu? aruḷ ceyka," eṉak kacintaḻutu, taṉ tāymuka nōkki, taṉakku
   am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya aṉaittum muṟaiyum poruḷum vaḻātu viḷampiṟṟu.
   viḷampuṅkāṟum amaitikoṇṭēṟṟa ac cempuḷ, “arumanta celvamē! alamaral
   vēṇṭā, yāṉ vēṟōr nallicai yicaippal kēṭṭi" eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tāṉ paṇṭē
   yakaṉṟa ulakiyalaippaṟṟik kūṟal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
   aṅṅaṉam, avviṭattiṉiṉṟum atu akaṉṟañāṉṟu, tāṉ ēṉ akaṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟu
   niṉaittatuṇṭō eṉiṉ iṉṟu; cellumiṭam iṟṟeṉa aṟiyāmē, paṇivum,
   uṟutinōkkum, nīṅkuta ṉalattiṟkeṉum tiṇṇiya veṇṇamum āya ivaiyē ataṉ
   uḷapporuḷā yamaintiruntaṉa. peruṅkaṭalulakiṟ pirintu varunāḷ, ac
   celavukkuriya kāraṇap poruḷai yuṇarttuvārēṉum, “iḵtaṉṟu, atu niṉ neṟi"
   eṉak kūṟuvārēṉum, atu kaṇṭatuṇṭō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu. aññāṉṟu, am maṭappuṟavam
   kaṇṭiruttal kūṭumāyiṉ, atu tāṉum ivvāṟu kūṟaṟpālatāmō? ivai yaṉaittum
   ārāyātu pōnta ac cempuḷ tāṉum piṉṉar uriya nalattait tuykkā toḻintatō?
   ataṉai yaṅṅaṉam cēṟal ceyyumāṟu ūkkiya poruḷāya uḷḷoli yātām? atuvē
   tiruvaruḷ eṉpatu. appaṟavai, ak kāṉyāṟ ṟaṭaikarai yaṭainta nāḷē
   uṇmaiyai oruvāṟuṇarntatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, āṇṭut tāṉ atu ciṉṉāḷvarait taṅkutal
   viḻaintatu. piṉṉarē, ataṉ kātaṟ ṟuṇaippaṟavai pōtantatum, vāḻkkaiyiṉpam
   nukarntatum piṟavum nikaḻntaṉa. aṉparkaḷē! atu pōtu, kātaṟ cēvaloṭu
   kalantu atu kuṭampai yamaikkumpōtu, taṉvayiṟ piṉṉart tōṉṟuvaṉavāya
   pārppukkaṭkeṉa ak kuṭampaiyaic camaikkum pōtu, am maṭappuṟavaṅ
   kaṇṭirukkumēl, ettuṇai ikaḻcci ceytirukkuṅkollō! piṟa vāp pārppukkaṭkuṅ
   kūṭamaittal pētaimai eṉ ṟeḷḷiyirukkuṅ kollō! aṉṟi, “etirviṉai yaṟital
   yāṅṅaṉam? nī iṅṅaṉ ceytal vīṇ poruḷilyāppē, iḵtu aṟivuṭaiyār toḻilaṉṟu"
   eṉṟellām viritturait tirukkumō! yātō? niṟka, ic ceykaikkellā māvatōr
   kāraṇattaiyēṉum ārāyātu atu ceytatu vañcaṉaiyō? aṉṟu. ivaiyaṉaittum at
   tiruvaruḷiṉ ceyalē yām kūṭamaittuc cēvalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkkaic cākā
   ṭukaittaṟku, itaṟkup pāṅkā yamaintatu avvuḷḷoli yāya tiruvaruḷaṉṟō
   ataṉvaḻi niṉṟataṉālaṉṟō, arumanta pārppukkaḷ peṟṟu, ayarāviṉpattil atu
   tiḷaittiruntatu! itu kālai atu, taṉ celvaṅkaṭkuc celvakkālattum allaṟ
   kālattum kōṭaṟpālaṉavāy uṟutip poruḷkaḷai yuṇarttaṟkēṟṟa aṟivamaiti
   peṟṟatō? iṉṟō? "ivai yaṉaittum poruḷil puṇarcci, kaṭṭukkatai, pulavar
   puraṭci". eṉṟellām pitaṟṟiya am maṭappuṟavaiyē viṉavumiṉ, avvuḷḷoli
   yataṉai vañcittatō veṉṟu. aṉṟiyum, ac cempuḷ, “celvaṅkāḷ eṅku, ēṉ cēṟal
   vēṇṭumeṉṉum ārāycciyiṉṟi, iṉṉē oruppaṭumiṉ. ikkālai yām
   aṟintilōmāyiṉum, maṟṟorukālai yaṟital kūṭum" eṉṟum, "paṇivum
   (Obedience) aṟivum (Faith) paṇpaṭṭa kālattaṉṟē, meyyuṇarvum,
   poruṇalamum, viḷaṅkat tōṉṟalum kāṭṭappeṟutalum uḷavām," eṉṟum
   kūṟiyataṉai appuṟavam uṇaral muṭiyātu pōlum!
   piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. kārum kūtirum nīṅkiṉa. maḻaiyum paṉippum
   mikuntamaiyiṉ, varaṉeṉṉum vaippup paṟṟiya nallicai, avaṟṟiṭai,
   ēnticaiyum tūṅkicaiyumākip piṉṉarc ceppalōcaiyāy, icaikkuntoṟum
   kēṭṭavai meycilirppavum, kaṇṇir vāravum, ūṉkalanta uyirkalantu,
   uḷaṅkalantu uṭalamellām uvaṭṭāniṟkum tēṉkalantu,
   ---
   paṇivu - vāveṉa varutalum pōveṉap pōtalum eṉṉum kēḷvippayaṉ. aṟivu -
   nallataṉkaṇ nalaṉum, tīyataṉkaṇ tīmaiyum kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum
   uṟutinōkkiṉaip payappatu.
   ------------
   tittikkum taṉmaiyiṟ ciṟantatu. iḵtuṇṭātaṟkuk kāraṇam, eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, avai
   uṇmai yaṟintaṉa; avvuṇmaiyaiyuṇarntaṉa; avvuṇmaiyai uṟuti yeṉavum
   koṇṭaṉa. ākalāṟṟāṉ, avai āṇṭai yiṉpattaiyum īṇṭēyuṇaralāyiṉa.
   niṟka, orunāḷ, ciṟukuñcukaḷ kuṭampaiyiṉiṉṟum veḷic ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟiṉ
   uḷaṅ kalaṅkutaṟ kētuvāya pēroliyoṉṟu arukē yeḻuntatu. eḻa,
   pēraccaṅkoṇṭu, avai tam kūṭaṭaintaṉa. kūṭu akkālai, nilaikulaintu,
   taḷarntu, nīrāl naṉaippuṇ ṭiruntatu. iruppiṉum, avai, oṉṟai
   yoṉṟiṟukappullic ciṟitu pōtu kiṭappa, āyppuḷ illāmai terintatu. etir
   nōkkiya kaṇkaḷ pūttuppōyiṉa; maṉam peruntuyar pūṇṭatu. eṉ ceyyum!
   piṉṉar avai, kūṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷippōntu, nōkku miṭantoṟum tam
   tāyppaṟavaiyait tēṭicceṉṟu, taḷarvum, kaḷaippum mītura, īṉṟāraik kāṇātu
   ēkkuṟṟa neñcam tuyarntu cāmpa, kāṇṭal kūṭumō kūṭātō eṉṉum eṇṇam
   orupuṭai yalaippa, perumpētuṟṟakālai cēṟum nīrum kalantu ciṟiya
   nīrnilaiyum, cuṟṟu mōṅkuta lillāp putalum uḷḷatōr tuṉpanilaiyam avaṟṟiṉ
   muṉṉart tōṉṟiṟṟu. kūrntu nōkkiyakālattu, paṟavaic ciṟakum ciṟumayirum
   cāmpiya vōcaiyum āṇṭut tōṉṟiṉa. kaṇṭa kuñcukaḷ. vēṇavā mīkkoḷa, aṅkuc
   ceṉṟu, iṟanta toṉṟum, iṟakkap pōvatoṉṟumāya iraṇṭu puṭkaḷaik kaṇṭu,
   iṟakkappōvatu tam yāyātal uṇarntu, paṭṭatai eṉ kūṟuvatu paṭuvataik
   kaṇṭa tāyppaṟavai, celvaṅkāḷ "eḻumiṉ ceṉmiṉ| tiruvaruḷiṉ kāriyamāya
   uḷḷoli untam uḷattum iṉṉē eḻunticaikkiṉṟatu. ceṉmiṉ ceṉmiṉ paṇimiṉ
   paṭarumiṉ tāḻttaṟkuc ciṟitu pōtum iṉṟu! iṉpanāṭṭamē koṇmiṉ!!" eṉṟatu.
   eṉakkēṭṭa kuṟum puṭkuḻām, "eṉṉai? entai yāṇṭuḷār! aiyakō! aṉṉāy!
   aṉṉāy!” eṉṟu alaṟik kūyiṉa. "amaimiṉ! amaimiṉ! yāṅkaḷ āṇṭu ummōṭu cūḻa
   viruttal muṭiyātu; kūṭātu. iṉi..." eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē taṉ talaiyaik kīḻē
   cāyttu, varaṉeṉṉum vaippil muḷaittatu.
   niṟka, itaṉai yīṇṭu uraittukkoṇṭē celliṉ perukum.
   tiruvaruḷ kaikūṭṭumāṟṟiṉai vāḻkkaimukattāṉ uṇartta vuṇarnta
   paṟavaikkuñcukaḷ, iṉpavulakai yeṇṇit tāmum pēraṟam nērntu, ap
   pērulakiṉai yaṭaital vēṇṭi muṭittaṉavāka, palavum palavēṟu neṟiyiṟ
   paṭarntu ceṉṟaṉa. avai yāṇṭuc celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟēṉum, ēṉ cellukiṉṟaṉa
   veṉṟēṉum oruvarēṉum kaṭṭuraittal kūṭātu. kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭaṉa voppa, tam
   tāyniṉaivum, tam paṇṭaik kūṭṭi ṉuṟaivum piṉṉar avaṟṟiṉ pulattut
   tōṉṟalum maṟaitalumāyiṉa. peṟṟatukoṇṭu pēriṉpan tuykkuṅ kālattut tam
   tāyniṉaivu tōṉṟic ciṟitu mayakkalum, avai piṉṉart teḷitalum āya
   iṉṉōraṉṉa pala nikaḻuṅkālattu, oṉṟu kūṟiyatu, ‘itaṉiṉum ciṟantatōr
   iṉpavulakātal vēṇṭum nam tāy paṭarnta vulakam. aṉṉatēyāka, aṅṅaṉam nam
   tāy kūṟiyatum uṇmaiyē pōlum” eṉpatu.
   ----------

                      4. piṟappokkum : ciṟappovvā

   vaṭuvilā vaiyattu maṉṉiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ naṭuvaṇateyta iru talaiyumeytum
   eṉṉum cemporuḷuṇarntu, annaṭuvaṇatāya poruḷīṭṭal karutit
   tiraikaṭalvaḻiyum, maivaraivaḻiyum pala nāṭukaḷukkuc cellum makkaḷ
   uḷarātalpōla, ciṟṟuyirkaḷuḷ tēṉ tēṭippalaticaikaḷukkuñ celvaṉa
   palavuḷa. avaṟṟiṟkut tēṉ eṉavum, aḷiyeṉavum, vaṇṭeṉavum, karumpeṉavum
   palapeyarkaḷ uṇṭu. niṟka, īṇṭut tēṉ tēṭicceṉṟ vaṇṭoṉṟiṉ varalāṟṟiṉaiyē
   yāṉ kūṟattoṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
   pututtaḷir tōṉṟa, pūkkaḷ malara, naṟumaṇam eṅkum naṉku parava,
   malaiyānilam maruṅkuvantacaiya, yāṇṭum iṉpamē ilakittēṉāṟum
   iḷavēṉiṟkālattu mutupakaloṉṟiṉ piṟpakalil, tēṉiyoṉṟu naṟuntēṉ vēṇṭik
   kāvum, cōlaiyum, kaviṉpūnturuttiyum pukku, āṇṭāṇṭu malarntu vayaṅkum
   pūttoṟum ceṉṟu, koḷaṟkariya tēṉ mikaippaṭakkoṇṭu, ataṉāleḻunta
   uvakaiyāl viraintu eḻuntu, taṉkūṭu nōkkic cellat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āyiṉum,
   atupōtu, atu pūviyal naṟavam māntip punti mayakkuṟṟiruntamaiyāl,
   vaḻiyaṟiyātu mēleḻuntu ceṉṟu, arukirunta or pērakattiṉ mēṟcāḷarattiṉuṭē
   ataṉ uṭpuṟam aṭaintatu.
   akkālai appērakattiṉkaṇ ētō oruciṟappu naṭaipeṟṟatu: iṉiyavum
   maṇamikkavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷ pala malintiruntaṉa. mikappalamakkaḷum
   kūṭiyiruntatōṭamaiyātu collāṭalālum, icaikkaruvikaḷālum piṟavaṟṟāṉum
   pērōcaiyum ceyvārāyiṉar. ivaṟṟaikkaṇṭa tēṉīyiṉ karuttil accantōṉṟi oru
   puṭaivaruttiṉum, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvaiyum maṇamum ataṉai veḷiyē kaviṭātu
   orupuṭait takaintaṉa. takaiyavē, tēṉiyum naṟumaṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ vaittuḷḷa
   taṭṭoṉṟiṉ puṟattētaṅki, avaṟṟiṉ maṇattai nukarntum cuvaiyait tērntum
   ciṟitupōtu kaḻittu niṟka, "ā! itō pār. tēṉī! tēṉī!" eṉakkūṟikkoṇṭē
   ciṟuvar iruvar ataṉarukēvantaṉar. iccoṟkaḷaik kēṭṭa avviyum, uṭaṉē
   accattāl uṭal naṭuṅka veḷiccelvāṉ muyaṉṟu orucāḷarattiṉ mēṟpāyntatu.
   pāvam! ataṉ katavu kaṇṇāṭiyālāyatākaliṉ, akkaḷivaṇṭu
   ataṉaiyuṇaraviyalātu mayaṅki, āṭiyil mōtuṇṭu kalaṅkik kīḻēvīḻntu,
   piṉṉarc ciṟitu tēṟi, cāḷaraccaṭṭattiṉ kīḻppuṟattē tāṉ mayakkattālum
   accattālum iḻanta maṉavamaitiyum vaṉmaiyaiyum mīṭṭum peṟaṟporuṭṭu
   viḻuntu kiṭantatu.
   niṟka, itaṉaikkaṇṭa ciṟuvarkaḷiruvarum arukēpōntu, itaippaṟṟiya oru
   collāṭal nikaḻttuvārāyiṉar.
   ciṟuvaṉ :-taṅkāy! itutāṉ tēṉi. itaṉait toḻilpuri tēṉi eṉpatum vaḻakku.
   itaṉ irutuṭaikaḷukku miṭaiyiluḷḷa meḻukkup paiyiṉaiyum kāṇ. ā! eṉṉa
   ūkkamum uḻaippu muṭaiyatu itu teriyumā!
   ciṟumi :- aṇṇāl meḻukkaiyum tēṉaiyum ākkuvatu -
   ittēṉi tāṉō?
   ciṟuvaṉ :-ām. pūkkaḷiṉ uḷḷirukkum iṉiyatēṉai itu avaṟṟiṉ uṭceṉṟu
   koṇarkiṟatu. muṉṉoru nāḷ nām nampūmputariṉ akattum puṟattum "kam kam"
   eṉa eṇṇiṟanta īkkaḷ moyttu icaittatu kaṇṭō maṉṟō avai aṅkumiṅkum
   paṟantu tirintataik kaṇṭu nakaittōmallavā? iḷantaḷirkaḷiṉ mītu ivai
   paṭaruṅ kālattu ivaṟṟiṉ meyyaḻaku nam uḷḷamellām koḷḷai koḷḷavillaiyā?
   niṟka, itu iṉṟu kālaiyil tēṉiṭṭuṅ kālattum yāṉ pārttatuṇṭu. itaṟkut
   tēṉiṭṭaloṉṟu tāṉ toḻileṉal ākātu.
   itaṉoṭu vēṟupala kāriyaṅkaḷum itu ceytal vēṇṭum. tēṉ kūṭu kaṭṭutal,
   ataṉai yaḻaku ceyal mutaliya ellāk kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum itutāṉ ceytal
   vēṇṭum. itu paṟṟiyētāṉ itu toḻilpuri tēṉi eṉṟaḻaikkap peṟutalum kāṇka.
   pāpam! pāpam!!
   ciṟumi :- toḻilpuritēṉi eṉpatu yātu? aṇṇā tāṅkaḷ "pāpam! pāpam!" eṉṟu
   itaṉmāṭṭu iraṅkuvatēṉ!
   ciṟuvaṉ :- ēṉ teriyātu? nam attāṉ nēṟṟu namakkuc coṉṉatai maṟantuviṭṭāy
   pōlum: tammaic ciṟitum ompātu piṟarnalamē yōmpi yavarkkē āṭpaṭṭuc ciṟu
   toḻil puriyum makkaḷaṉaivarum iraṅkaṟpālarē! avar vakaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē
   ittēṉiyumākaliṉ, yāṉ itaṉ māṭṭum maṉamiraṅkal vēṇṭiṟṟu. ivvīkkaḷukku
   araca ī oṉṟuḷatu. ataṉai īyaraci yeṉpatuvē peru vaḻakku. atu oru
   ciṟutoḻilum, ceyyātu, eññāṉṟum toḻilīkkaḷ amaitta kūṭṭiṉkaṇṇē iruntu,
   avai koṇarum tēṉaiyuṇṭalum, koṇarnta īkkaṭku vēṟupaṇi paṇittalum,
   tāṉpayanta ciṟṟikkaḷaip purattalum, ivaṟṟōraṉṉa piṟavumē ceytu
   koṇṭuvarum. ataṟku vēṇṭum ciṟupaṇikaḷai, maruṅkiruntu ārkkum vaṇṭukaḷ
   ceyyāniṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟāka veṟṟikkaḷ palavuḷa. avai oru toḻilum
   ceyyātu vīṇē aṅkumiṅkun tirintukoṇṭiruppavai. ēṉaiyavai yāvum
   ivviyaippōṉṟu tēṉiṭṭip piṟavaṟṟiṟkaḷittalōṭu, avaṟṟiṟkām ciṟutoḻilaip
   puripavaiyē yākum. ikkūṟiyavaṟṟai nam attāṉ aṟivārēl avarāl nakaittu
   aṭaṅkutal muṭiyātu.
   ciṟumi:- āṉāl, icceytikaḷ attāṉukkut teriyāvō?
   ciṟuvaṉ :- illai. avarukkut teriyum eṉṟu yāṉ - niṉaikkavillai.
   teriyumāyiṉ, avar ivaṟṟaip piṭittalum piṭittuttuṉpuṟuttalum ceyyāraṉṟē.
   yāṉ evvāṟu aṟintēṉeṉiṉ, eṉakku nam cōlaikkāvalaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. niṟka,
   nerunal avar kūṟiyavaṟṟai nōkkiṉaiyā? ivvulakil, aracar, araciyār eṉa
   uyirkaḷuṭ cilariruttal iyaṟkaikkē māṟāṉatām; ellārum orē accāka
   iruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām; iyaṟkaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum ovvoru poruṭkum
   piṟappiṉāl oruvakai vēṟupāṭum iṉṟām; eṉavē, aracar eṉavum
   araciyareṉavum iruppavar naṭunilai varampaik kaṭantavarē yāvarām.
   ciṟumi :-āṉāl, ivaṟṟai uyttuṇarntu kōṭal ivvikkaḷukku iyalāteṉa yāṉ
   niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ.
   ciṟuvaṉ :- niṉaippateṉṉa uṇmaiyilē atu avaṟṟiṟkuk kiṭaiyātutāṉ.
   cōlaikkāvalaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟai maṭṭil ittoḻilīkkaḷ kēṭṭirukkumāyiṉ,
   ettakaiya ciṉaṅ koṇṭirukkum! nīyē niṉaittuppār!!
   ciṟumi :- avaṉ kūṟiyateṉṉa?
   ciṟuvaṉ :- araciyaiyum āṭpaṭṭoḻukum ciṟṟikkaḷaiyum cīr tūkkuṅkāl
   piṟappiṉāl avaṟṟiṟkiṭaiyil oruvakai vēṟupāṭum illai. piṟappum tōṟṟamum
   oṉṟē. eṉiṉum ivvēṟupāṭṭait taruvaṉa uṇṭivakaiyum, uṟaiyuḷiṉ
   taṉmaiyumēyām. icciṟṟikkaḷai vaḷarkkum peṭai vaṇṭukaḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟku
   oruvakai uṇavum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvakai uṇavum; cilavaṟṟiṟku oru
   vakaikkūṭum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟuvakaiyāṉa kūṭum tarukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl
   cila īyaracikaḷākavum, cila toḻilīkkaḷākavum māṟukiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruttē
   nerunal nam attāṉ mēṉmakkaḷaiyum toḻil makkaḷaiyumpaṟṟik kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ
   amaintu kiṭantatu. ‘piṟappokkum ellāvuyirkkum.” eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟai.
   niṟka, uṇṇuṅkālam āyiṟṟu. vā, pōkalām.
   ciṟumi :- aṇṇāl ciṟitu nilluṅkaḷ. yāṉ ivviyai veḷiyē viṭuttuvarukiṟēṉ.
   iṅṅaṉam kūṟikkoṇṭē, acciṟumi taṉ kaittuṇiyāl ataṉaip paiyaveṭuttu
   aruḷoḻuka nōkki, “antō ē! koṅkutēr vāḻkkai añciṟaittumpi!
   kuḻavipparuvattē uṉṉai vaḷarttōr uṉakku uṇṭiyum uṟaiyuḷum uyarntaṉa
   koṭuttirupparēl, ā! iṉṟunīyum oru īyaraciyāka iruppāyaṉṟō! eṉṉa iḻinta
   nilaiyai uṉakku avar tantuḷḷār: aṟitiyō? kiṭakka, nī iṉi veḷic ceṉṟu,
   uṉvāṉāḷ muṟṟilum meḻuku ceytalilum, tēṉiṭṭalilum celaviṭal vēṇṭum.
   celka. celka. nī mēṟkoṇṭa ciṟutoḻil cīrttoḻilāmāka!" eṉa vāḻtti,
   cāḷarattiṉ puṟattē viṭuttaṉaḷ. veḷippōnta vaṇṭu, kaḻipēruvakaikoṇṭu taṉ
   vaḻiccelkai toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
   ā! eṉṉa iṉiymālaikkālam! kāṭcikkiṉiyamālaik kālam! kātalarkūṭik
   kalanturaiyāṭum mālaikkālam! iṅkaṉam iḷavēṉiliṉ mutumālai, maṉṉuyirkkup
   pēriṉpam payantu iṉitāyt tōṉṟa, attoḻilikkumaṭṭil attaṉmaiyiṉ
   māṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉ maṉam ētō or poruḷāṟ paṟṟappeṟṟut
   tuyarpūṇṭiruntatu; aṭikkaṭi atu oruvakaiyuṇarcciyāl nāṇi mika varuntat
   toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ivvāṟu varuttamum tuyaramum mayaṅkiya uḷḷamoṭu atu taṉ
   kūṭṭai - paṇiceytu kiṭappatē taṉṉaṟamākum eṉṉum koḷkaiyuṭaṉ aṉṟu
   kālaiyil viṭṭuppirinta taṉ kūṭṭai aṭaintatu. aṭaitalum, aṭakkalākā
   veṟuppum vekuḷiyum ataṉ uḷḷattut tōṉṟa, atu tāṉ koṇarnta tēṉpaiyai
   nekiḻtteṟintu, maṉam calippaṭaiya, “ellāvuyiriṉum kaṭaippaṭṭēṉ yāṉē
   kāṇ" eṉa aṟaintatu.
   akkālai, aṅkuc ciṟutoḻil ceytukoṇṭirunta - maṟṟoru toḻil itaṉaik kēṭṭu,
   "eṉṉai ceyti? eṉṉē niṉ ceykai yiruntavāṟu veṟimikka vēṅkaiyiṉ tēṉ
   uṇṭaṉaikollō aṉṟi akkoṭiya paccilaippuḻu nam kūṭukaḷil taṉ
   muṭṭaiyiṭṭuḷatu paṟṟic ciṉaṅ koṇṭaṉaiyō? eṉṉai kāraṇam?’ eṉa viṉaviṟṟu.
   ataṟku itu, “nī kūṟiya yātēṉum vēṇṭuṅ kāraṇamākātu. aiyō pāvam! avaiyeṉ
   ceyyum? tāntāmuṉ ceytaviṉaiyait tāmē tuyppa tallatu piṟar ceyvateṉ?"
   eṉaviṭaiyiṟuppa, atu, "piṉṉar eṉṉaiyō kāraṇam. uraitti" eṉa mīṭṭum
   viṉava, itu kūṟum:- "illai. aṉpa! iṉṟu kālaiyiṟ ceṉṟa yāṉ mikka cēṇiṟ
   ceṉṟēṉ. ceṉṟaviṭattu yāṉ, itukāṟum kēṭṭaṟiyātaṉavellām kēṭṭēṉ.
   kēṭṭavaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷ “nāmellōrum iḻitoḻilpuriyum iḻittakka
   uyirkaḷāvōm eṉpatē.”
   ivvurai ataṉ cevippaṭalum, atu tiṭukkiṭṭu, "nī itukāṟum aṟiyāta itaṉai
   uṉakku uṇarttiyavar yāvar?” eṉak kēṭpa, itu cālavekuṇṭu, uṇmaiyai
   yuṇarttupavar yāvarāyiṉumeṉ! yāṉ iṉṟu aṟintatu muṟṟilum uṇmaiyē.
   itumaṭṭil uṟutiyē." eṉṟatu.
   eṉavē, atu, “oru kālum nī kūṟiyatu uṟutiyuṭaittaṉṟu. pētaicciṟṟuyiroṉṟu
   iṅṅaṉam pitaṟṟiyatu koṇṭu, piḻaipaṭṭoḻukutal peruntakaimaiyēyaṉṟu. nī
   iḻittakkōy eṉa uṇarvuṇarttiṉārā luraikkappaṭṭa acciṟu poḻutu kāṟum nī
   appeṟṟiyai yallai yeṉpatai nīyē naṉkaṟivāy. ākavē, avar uraittāreṉa nī
   kūṟum urai veṟṟuraiyeṉpataṉiṉum veḷiṟṟuraiyeṉavē yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ. iṉi itaip
   paṟṟik kūṟutalum aṟamākātu." eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭē tāṉ muṉṉark kuṟaipaṭaviṭṭa
   kāriyattaic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. -
   eṉiṉum, ivvuraimuḻuvatum ceviṭaṉ kātil ūtappeṟṟa tivavoliyē pōlap
   payaṉiṉṟi yoḻintatu. āṉāl, itumaṭṭil, taṉṉotta īkkaḷ palavaṟṟai
   oruṅkaḻaittut tāṉ tēṉṟēṭic ceṉṟaviṭat taṟintavai yaṉaittaiyum teḷivākak
   kūṟiṟṟu. kēṭṭavaṟṟuṭ cila peruviyappum, cila peruṅkalakkamuṅ koṇṭaṉa;
   muṭivil avvīkkuḻātti ṉiṭaiyil oru peruṅkuḻappam uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
   piṉṉarc cila īkkaḷ eḻuntu, uṇmaiyil tām piṟappiṉāl oppāṉavai yeṉiṉum,
   tammai araca īkkaḷ naṭāttu muṟai iḻivāṉatē yeṉavum, atu
   tammaiyaṭimaippaṭuttuva toṉṟeṉavum, ataṉai viraiviṟ pōkkuvatu
   mutaṟkaṭaṉ eṉavum muṭivu ceytaṉa. iṭaiyiṟ cila īkkaḷ muṉvantu tām
   toṭaṅkiya iyakkattukkut talaimai tāṅkit tammai uriya neṟiyil
   naṭattuvataṟkēṟṟavar acciṟuvar tam attāṉē yāvar eṉavum kūṟiṉa. atupōtu,
   vēṟu cila eḻuntu, muṉṉark kuṟikkappeṟṟavarē talaivarāyiṉ, avar tammai
   oruṅkē yaracaīkka ḷākkutalō ṭamaiyātu tam kūṭukaḷaiyum cevvaṉam
   purantu, tamakku oru mētakka ciṟappurimaiyum tantaruḷvāreṉavum
   vaḻimoḻiya, okkum okkum eṉṉum pēroli kammeṉa akkuḻāttiṭai yeḻuntatu."
   ā! ituvum takumā? yāvarum civikaiyūrat toṭaṅkiṉ, ataṉaik kāvuvār yār
   innilaiyil, ciṟitu nāḷ mutirnta toḻiliyoṉṟu, akkūṭṭattiṭait taṉ
   talaiyai nīṭṭi, “piṟar nalattukkeṉat toḻil puriyum īkkaḷaṉaittum araca
   īkkaḷāka māṟiṉ, naṉṟu! naṉṟu!! avaṟṟiṟku āmpaṇiyaip puripavai yāvō?"
   eṉṟatu. kallā aṟiviṟ kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta nallār tamatu kaṉam
   naṇṇuvarō? naṇṇāraṉṟē! ākaliṉ, avvikka ḷaṉaittum, itaṉaik kēṭṭatum
   cālavekuṇṭu, matiyili eṉavum, taṉṉurimai yaṟiyāt taṟutalaiyeṉavum kūṟi,
   “nām aṉaivarum aracākka ḷākuṅkāl, iṟṟaip pōtu aracu pūṇṭiruppaṉavum,
   avaṟṟiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷum tām uḷḷaḷavum namakkup paṇiceytu kiṭakkalāmē!”
   eṉṟaṉa. eṉṟalum, av ī maṟumuṟaiyum eḻuntu, "avaṟṟiṟkup piṉṉar attoḻilai
   mēṟkoḷḷuvōr yār" eṉak kēṭpa, kam, kam eṉṉum pēroli āṅku mikkeḻuntatu.
   uṭaṉē, atuvum taṉ nāvaiyaṭakkik koṇṭatu.
   maṟṟoru ī eḻuntu, "aṉpuṭaiyīr! eṉakku muṟpēciya īkku nikaḻnta vacai
   aḻakaṟṟatākum. atu namakkuṭ piḷavaiyuṇṭupaṇṇiṉum paṇṇum. paṇṇuṅkāl, nām
   kaikkoṇṭa iyakkam veṟṟipeṟātu. nam koḷkaikku māṟākavuḷḷa, paḻaiya
   nilaiyilē tam vāṇāḷaic celavaḻittārkkup putiya koḷkaikaḷum,
   uṇarccikaḷum, iyakkaṅkaḷum, piṟavum veṟuppait taruvaṉavākaliṉ,
   avveṟuppai yuṭaiyavaraiyum nam iyakkattiṟkuk koṇartal iṉṟiyamaiyātu.
   "niṟka, tēṉiṭṭalum meḻuku ceytalumāya toḻil kaḷaic ceyyum nām aṉaivarum
   īyaracikaḷāka māṟiviṭuvōmāyiṉ, namakkāka attoḻilkaḷaip
   puripavaiyilavāka, nam nilai mikka tuṉpa nilaiyāy muṭiyum. itu nīṅkaḷ
   naṉkaṟinta toṉṟu. itaṉai nīṅkaḷē eṇṇip pāruṅkaḷ.
   "eṉṟālum, eṉakku oru eṇṇam tōṉṟukiṟatu; ataṉai acaṭṭai ceyyātu kēṭṭu,
   amaivuṭaittāyiṉ ēṟṟuṅ koḷḷuṅkaḷ. atu yāteṉiṉ, nām aṉaivarum
   īyaracikaḷātaliṉum, avvaracurimaiyaiyē muṟṟilum kaḷaintu viṭṭu, aracaī
   mutal veṟṟivaraiyuḷḷa aṉaittīkkaḷum toḻilīkkaḷākamāṟi, namakkuḷ
   oruvakai yuyarvu tāḻvumiṉṟi orumittu vāḻtal vēṇṭum" eṉṟōr coṟpoḻivu
   ceytatu.
   iṭaiyil, muṉṉar vacaipeṟṟuc ceṉṟa toḻili maṟumuṟaiyum eḻuntu, "nām
   aṉaivarum iṉṟuvarait toḻilīkkaḷākavē uḷḷōm. aṟṟākaviṉ, namakku
   ivviyakkattāl oru vakaippayaṉum eytutal muṭiyāteṉa niṉaikkiṟēṉ" eṉṟatu.
   eṉṟalum eḻuntaṉa. īkkaḷ. pukaippaṭalam pōlat tiraṇṭeḻuntu kam kam eṉap
   pēroli ceytu, tam ciṉattai evvemmuṟaiyil ataṟkuk kāṭṭutal kūṭumō avvam
   muṟaiyil kāṭṭiṉa. kāṭṭak kaṇṭañciya atu muṉpōlavē taṉ iruppiṭam
   aṭaintatu. aṭaitalum, pakalavaṉum maṟaintaṉaṉ, paṟavaippāṭṭu aṭaṅkiṉa;
   eṅkum iḷaiyiruḷ parantatu; nīlavāṉattu nittilam patittāṟpōlak kōla
   mīṉiṉam tam ciṟṟoḷi parappiṉa; piṟpakal muṟṟum kuḻappattiṭaiyuḻanta
   īkkaḷ tattam kūṭaṭaintu uṟaṅkuvavāyiṉa. piṉṉar avviravu muṟṟum vēṟu
   kuḻappamiṉṟi yoḻiya yāṇṭum amaitiyē talaiciṟantu niṉṟatu.
   veḷḷi muḷaippa, viṭiyal vantatu; tāmaraiyiṟ ṟuñciya kāmar vaṇṭiṉam,
   kaṭkamaḻum neytalukkuc ceṉṟu, tātutip piṉṉar, kaṇpōl malarnta
   kāmarcuṉaimalarai yaṭaintu oli ceyyā niṟpa, muṉṉāḷ nikaḻnta kuḻappattil
   īṭupaṭṭa īkkaḷ maṭṭil oruṅku kūṭi mīṭṭum tam nilaimaiyaiyum, ataṟkēṟṟa
   muṟaikaḷaiyum paṉṉippaṉṉip pēcattoṭaṅkiṉa. vēṟu cila, kūṭukaḷil,
   araciyar ēviya paṇikaḷai iṉitu ceytu vantavai, ikkuḻappattaip
   paṟṟiyēṉum, ataṉiṭaiyē nikaḻum virivuraikaḷaiyēṉum oru poruḷāka
   niṉaiyātu tam toḻililē tam karuttaic celutti niṉṟaṉa. niṟka,
   kuḻappattiṟ kalanta īkkuḻāttiṉiṭai nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻa, mutirā
   īkkaḷiṟ cila muṉivu mītuārak koṇṭu, ataṉāl tammaiyum, tam
   maṉattiṇmaiyaiyum iḻantu, peruṅkalakkattai viḷaivik kum
   nilaimaiyeytiṉa. atupōtu, ikkuḻappattiṟku mutaṟkāraṇamākavirunta ī
   avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, "aṉparkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ kaikkoṇṭa iyakkam naṉmai payakkak
   kūṭiyatē. eṉiṉum, iṭaiyilirukkum oru poruḷai nīṅkaḷ eṇṇātu
   pōyviṭṭīrkaḷē! apporuḷ tāṉ etuveṉiṉ, nīṅkaḷ aṉaivarum mutirntu
   viṭṭamaiyaṉṟō? nāṉ mutirnta uṅkaḷai evvāṟu iṉi araca īkkaḷāka
   māṟṟuvatu, kūṭātaṉṟō. āyiṉum, nāmkoṇṭa iyakkattiṉ veṟṟik kaṭaiyāḷamāka
   yātāṉumoru putiya muṭipai eytutal vēṇṭum. eṉ aṟiviṟkeṭṭiya muṭipoṉṟaik
   kūṟukiṟēṉ kēḷuṅkaḷ. piṟaku uṅkaḷ viruppampōṟ ceyyuṅkaḷ" eṉṟatu. uṭaṉē,
   avvīkkaḷaṉaittum orupaṭiyāka nī kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟai yāṅkaḷ mikka viruppuṭaṉ
   ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṟōm. colliyaruḷka" eṉap pēroliyiṭṭuk kūṟiṉa. kūṟalum, atu
   puṉṉakai tavaḻum iṉmukaṅkoṇṭu, "aṉparkaḷē! nāḷiṉ mutirnta namakku iṉi
   araciyar vēṇṭuvatillai. nām aṉaivarum iṉṟiruppatu pōlavē
   toḻilīkkaḷākavē yiruppōm; nām iṉi oruvarukku aṭimaippaṭṭu avarkaḷ
   kālāliṭum ēvalait talaiyāṟ ceytal vēṇṭā. nāmārkkum kuṭiyallōm! nalamē
   vāḻvōm. nammaik kēṭpavarkkum, tūya vīrattōṭum “yām yārkkum kuṭiyallōm.
   yātum añcōm’ eṉavē, nām iṉikkūṟuvōm. ituvē eṉ ciṟṟaṟiviṟa keṭṭiya
   muṭipākum. yāṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟil kuṟṟaṅkaḷiruntāṟ poṟuttuk koḷḷumpaṭik
   kēṭṭukkoṇṭu, immāṭṭōṭu eṉ ciṟiya virivuraiyai niṟuttikkoḷḷukiṟēṉ,
   eṉṟatu. eṉavē, vaṇṭiṉam muṟṟum kammeṉum icai ceytu "itu takum. takum
   takum!!" eṉa uṭaṉ paṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar, avaikaḷ oruṅkē tiraṇṭu tām
   iṉittamittu vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa iṭam niṟuvutaṟ poruṭṭu maṟumuṟaiyum oru kūṭṭam
   kūṭiṉa. itupōtu ivaṟṟiṟkut talaimaipūṇṭu iyal neṟiyiṟ celutta vallatāya
   oṉṟum varavillai. ākavē, akkūṭṭattil oruvakaikkaṭṭuppāṭum oḻuṅkum
   ilavāyiṉa. eṉ ceyvatu ituvum ulakaviyaṟkaiyē!!
   niṟka, iṭam niṟuvutaṟ poruṭṭu nikaḻnta ikkūṭṭattiṭai, "oṉṟu eḻuntu,
   kāṭcikkiṉiyavum kaḷ niṟaintavumāṉa malarkaḷ ceṟintuḷatum, itō
   nammuṉṉart tōṉṟuvatu māya cōlaiyē nām iṉitu vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa iṭamākum"
   eṉṟatu; maṟṟoṉṟu eḻuntu, "accōlaiyiṉum, ataṟkuc ciṟitu cēyttuḷḷa vayalē
   iṉitākum. cōlaikku varuvārum, varuvaṉavum palavātalāṉum, kāṟṟum
   eññāṉṟum viraivuṭaṉ mōtiyalaittalāṉum namakkum, nam kūṭukaṭkum
   tīṅkuviḷaiyiṉum viḷaiyum." eṉṟatu; vēṟoṉṟu, "naṉkaṉam amaittuḷḷa
   vīṭoṉṟiṉ kūraiyē mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa viraṇṭiṉum naṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum atu nammai
   maḻaikkālattil iṉitu "kākkavallatākum” eṉṟu colliṟṟu. nāṉkāvatāka,
   vēṟoṉṟu, "paḻumaramoṉṟiṉ koḻuviya kiḷaiyē nallatu" eṉa, maṟṟoṉṟu, malai
   muḻaiñciṉaiyum, vēṟoṉṟu kaṟpāṟaiyaiyum kuṟittaṉa. iṅṅaṉam, ovvoṉṟum
   taṉtaṉ karuttiṟ pulaṉāya iṭaṅkaḷaik kūṟic ceṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya, uṟutiyāṉa
   iṭam oṉṟēṉum kuṟikkappeṟavillai. aṉṟiyum, oṉṟu kūṟiyatu maṟṟoṉṟiṉ
   karuttuk kovvā toḻintatu.
   ivvaṇṇam īkkaḷ oṉṟuk koṉṟu muraṇiya karuttuk koṇṭu, kalāyttu niṟpak
   kālamuñ ceṉṟatu; piṟpakal eytiṟṟu. atupōtu, attēṉī ivviyakkattukkuk
   kāraṇamāyirunta attēṉī - vēṟōrīyiṉai nōkki, ‘eṉṉē uṅkaḷ aṟiviṉmai
   yiruntavāṟu! uṅkaḷaik kāṇumtōṟum eṉ uḷattil vekuḷi yeḻukiṉṟatēyaṉṟi
   viruppuṇṭākiṉṟatillai. ettakaiya aṟivuḷḷa ciṟṟuyirum ivvaṇṇam vīṇē
   kālattaik kaḻikkātu. antō! itaṉai aṟiviṉmai yeṉpatā? aṟiyāmai yeṉpatā?"
   eṉa, atu cālavum ciṉantu, eṉṉai kūṟiṉai? ē, aṟivili! uṉakku īyaracu
   peṟavēṇṭum eṉum eṇṇam uḷatu pōlum! aṟintēṉ. aṟintēṉ. iṉṟēl, nī eṉṉai
   iṅṅaṉam kēṭṭalkūṭumā? niṟka, yāṅkaḷ kālattai vīṇēkaḻippatāl uṉakku
   uṇṭākum iḻaveṉṉai? yāṅkaḷ etaiyēṉum ceykiṟōm. atu paṟṟi nī vekuḷutal
   eṟṟukku? nī uṉ kāriyattaip pārttuk koḷka.” eṉṟaṟaintatu.
   ittakaiya māṟṟattai etir nōkkātu peṟṟa attēṉī, maṉamuḷaintu,
   taṉakkuṇṭāṉa māṉattaiyum, iḻivaiyum, uṉṉiyuṉṉip peritum varuntat
   toṭaṅkiṟṟu. appōtu, tāṉ akkiḷarccikkuk kāraṇamāyiruntamaiyaiyum, ataṉāl
   taṉakkē tuṉpa meytiṉamaiyaiyum atu ettuṇaiyō aṭakkikkoḷḷa muyaṉṟatu.
   maṟṟu, ammuyaṟciyāṟ payaṉoṉṟu muṇṭāyiṟṟaṉṟu. ākavē, atu maṟṟa īkkaḷiṉ
   mukttaik kāṇutaṟkē veṭki, civveṉ ṟeḻuntu pūmputaroṉṟai yaṭaintu,
   paṇṭēpōl tēṉṟēṭat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. putarkkuṭ pukuntu, āṇṭu aḻaku oḷira
   malarntu niṉṟa malaroṉṟiṉ uṭpukuntu, tāṉ itukāṟum kaṇṭaṟiyāta cuvaiyum,
   mikutiyumuṭaiya tēṉuṇṭu kaḷittatu. ā" itu pōtaṉṟō atu kaḷivaṇṭeṉṉum
   peyarkku ilakkāyiṟṟu! atu itupōtu koṇṭa kaḷippukku or ellaiyumiṉṟu! atu
   taṉ vāṇāḷi lōrnāḷilēṉum itupōṉṟa kaḷippiṉai yaṭainta tiṉṟeṉiṉ vēṟu
   kūṟuvateṉṉai vēṭkai muṟṟuṅ kāṟum naṟavamuṇṭu, vēṇṭuvaṉavum uṭaṉkoṇṭu
   taṉ kūṭu nōkki, eṉṟum aṟiyāta oruvakaippaṟṟu, aṉṟu ataṉ maṉattai īrppa,
   atu virainteḻuntatu. eḻutalum, ataṉ muṉṉar, paṇṭu tāṉ
   kēṭṭaṟintaṉavaṟṟai mutaṉ mutaluraippak kēṭṭa mutirntavaṇṭu pōtantatu.
   kaṇṭatum, ataṟkup peru nāṇ eḻuntu aṟivai mayakka, arukirunta
   malaroṉṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu maṟaintatu. āyiṉum ammutirvaṇṭu, naṭantaṉavaṟṟaik
   kuṟippāka vuṇarntum, oṉṟum teriyātataiyoppak kūrtta nōkkattōṭu, "eṉkol!
   nī ēṉ iṅku vantaṉai? uṉakku uṟṟa teṉṉai? nī vantiruppāy eṉa yāṉ
   orukālum niṉaittatiṉṟē! ō! uṉ uḻaivaṇṭukaḷ yāṇṭuḷa? avaṟṟai nī
   piritaṟkēynta kāraṇam yātō? kūṟuti” eṉa viṉaviṟṟu.
   itaikkēṭṭa akkaḷivaṇṭu, peritum nāṇi, "yāṉ aṟiyēṉ; cōlaiyiṉ puṟattē yāṉ
   avaṟṟaip pirintu pōntēṉ ātaliṉ" eṉṟatu.
   mu. vaṇṭu:- avai yātu ceytukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa?
   ka.va :- ka-lā-y-k-ki-ṉ-ṟa-ṉa-pō -lu-m
   mu.va :- (viraintu.) eṉṉō kāraṇam?
   ka.va :- tām iṉicceyya vēṇṭuvaṉapaṟṟi.
   mu.va :- ā! eṉṉa aruntoḻil purital nērntaṉa! naṉṟu! iḷavēṉiṟkālattu
   iṉiya kālaiyil mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭataṉṟō ittoḻil naṉṟu! naṉṟu! ceykaṟiya
   ceyvataṉṟō periyōratu toḻil!
   ka.va - :- eḷḷutal vēṇṭām .ē! aṉṉāy!! iṉiyum iṉṉaṇam iḻittukkūṟaṟka.
   eṉṉaip poṟut taruḷka. iṉi yāṉ ceyya vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaiyum paṇittaruḷka. oṉṟu
   maṭṭil kūṟukiṉṟēṉ; yāṉ muṉṉarc ceṉṟirunta ñāṉṟu iyaṟkaiyiṉ iyalpaiyum,
   nammaṉōriṉṟaṉmaiyaiyum paṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟa urai muṟṟilum vāymaiyākavē
   tōṉṟiṟṟu; eṉiṉum, nām avvuraivaḻi niṟpāṉ muyaluṅkālattu, vīṇē
   kalāyttalum veṟuttalum ceytal nērkiṉṟatēyaṉṟip perum payaṉoṉṟum
   peṟumāṟillai. eṉṉai?
   mu.vā :- niṟka, niṉ vayateṉṉai.
   ka.va :- (iḷamait taṉmaiyoṭu nāṇmīkkoḷa) ēḻu nāṭkaḷē.
   mu.va :-eṉ vayateṉṉai yaṟitiyō?
   ka.va :- pala tiṅkaḷāmpōlum.
   mu.va :-ām. ām! pala tiṅkaḷ mutirntu uḷēṉ. nī kūṟiya tuṇmaiyē. yāṉ nāḷ
   mutirnta oru vaṇṭē niṟka. varuka nām iruvarum porutu pārppōm.
   ka.va :-ō! ō!atu kūṭātu; kaṭavuḷāṇaiyāka atu oru kālum kūṭātu. yāṉ
   kaṭṭiḷamaiyuṭaimaiyiṉ payaṉāya valiyuṭaiyē ṉākaliṉ, niṉakku ūṟu
   iḻaikkiṉum iḻaippaṉ.
   mu.va :-eṉavē, nammiruvaruḷ vaṉmaiyaṟṟa eḷiyaṉ yāṉaṉṟē. aṟṟāka,
   iḷamaiyum vaṉmaiyu mikka niṉṉai eṉ māṭṭu aṟivurai kēṭkattuṇṭiyatu yātu
   kol? iḵtu ōr viyappaiyaṉṟō tarukiṟatu!
   ka.va. :-aṟṟaṉṟu. meyvaṉmaikkum aṟiviṉ vaṉmaikkum toṭarpoṉṟu miṉṟu. yāṉ
   niṉmāṭṭaṟivurai kēṭpāṉ tuṇintatu nī aṟivuṭaiyai eṉpatai yāṉ
   naṉkuṇarntamaiyē yākum. yāṉ aḵtil lēṉākaliṉ, itupōtu iḻittakka nilaiyai
   yeytiyuḷēṉ.
   mu.va :- mutumaiyum iḷamaiyum - vaṉmaiyum meṉmaiyum - aṟivum
   aṟiyāmaiyum - ā! ēṉō otta piṟaviyum, otta nilaiyum poruḷākak
   kiḷarcciceyta vaṇṭukaṭku? eṉiṉum, varuntaṟka. nām ivaṟṟai yōr
   oḻuṅkiṟkuk koṇartal kūṭum. niṟka, iṉi nām iruvarum ottu vāḻtalāmā?
   eṉṉai niṉ karuttu?
   ka.va :-eṉ iṉṉuyi rāṇaiyāka, yāṉ - iṉṟē, ēṉ! iṉṉē uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟēṉ. nām
   vāḻtaṟku iṭam yātu? .
   mu.va :-aḵtaṉṟu nāṉ mutaṟkaṇ vēṇṭuvatu. nam iruvarkkum karuttukkaḷ
   muraṇāṅ kālattu iṭainiṉṟu takuti nōkkuvār yāvarātal vēṇṭum.
   ka.vā :- nīyē! eṉṉeṉiṉ nīyē mutumaiyum, aṟiviṉ tiṇmaiyum uṭaiyai.
   mu.va :- naṉṟu, nalluṇavāya tēṉ nāṭic ceṉṟu koṇarunar yāvarātal vēṇṭum?
   ka.va :- yāṉ. yāṉē!
   “iḷaiñaṉ valiyaṉ ēviṉa ceyyum
   uḷaiyā muyaṟciyaṉ oruvaṉ yāṉē.”
   mu.va :- mikavum naṉṟu. ākavē, nī eṉṉai itupōtu araca īyākavum niṉṉait
   toḻiliyākavum karutiviṭṭaṉai yaṉṟō?
   ka.va :- ām ām.
   mu.va :-eṉṉē niṉ aṟiyāmai itu muṭipāyiṉ, namatu paṇṭaiyakūṭṭukkum, nām
   pēṇiniṉṟa. araca īkkum nērnta kuṟai yātō? aṉṟiyum nam iruvarmāṭṭē,
   oruvar talaivarāka oruvar ēvaliḷaiñarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu
   peṟappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō, ākavē, pallōrkūṭiya kūṭṭattuḷ ivaraṉṉa talaivarāvā
   roruvar vēṇṭappaṭuvataṉai uraittalum vēṇṭumō?
   itaṉaik kēṭṭalum avvīkku nalluṇarvu tōṉṟa, atu mikka uvakai pūttut taṉ
   ēṉaiya iṉaṅkaḷai nōkkic civveṉṟeḻuntatu. atupōḻtu, ēṉaiya īkkaḷellām
   kuḻappam ceytukoṇṭēyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilamaṭṭum tam vaṉmaiyum,
   eḻucciyum kuṉṟātiruntaṉavē yaṉṟip piṟavaṉaittum kuṉṟip paciyāl
   varuntiṉa. ciṟitu poḻutu kaḻitalum, cila tam vaḻakkappaṭiyē tēṉ
   tēṭacceṉṟaṉa; cila tam kūṭṭai yaṭaintaṉa.
   iṅṅaṉam ivai perukuḻappaṅ koṇṭu uḻalāniṟpa, iḷamaiyum ūkkamum
   mikutiyākkoṇṭa pala īkkaḷ puṭaicūḻa, ammutirvaṇṭu, naṟuntēṉkoṇṭu
   īyaraciyirunta veḻiṟ peruṅkūṭṭaic ceṉṟaṭaintatu. āṇṭuk kāval purintu
   niṉṟa ciṟṟiyoṉṟu, ataṉaiyuṭpuka viṭātu takaintatu.
   avvaṇṭu ataṉaik kāraṇamviṉava, atu taṉ īyaraci piṟantupaṭṭatākavum,
   ataṟkāṅkaṭaṉkaḷ naṭaipeṟuvatākavum, āṇṭu ēṉaiya ēkutal kūṭāteṉavum
   kūṟiṟṟu.
   kūṟalum, akkaḷivaṇṭu, uḷaṅkalaṅki, "aiyō! evvāṟu iṟantaṉar nam
   araciyār! ā!nellu muyiraṉṟē nīru muyiraṉṟē, maṉṉaruyirttē malartalai
   yulakam ā! iṉi namakkut talaimai tāṅki nammai naṉṉeṟiyil naṭāttuvār
   yāvarō ē!kāvala! nam araciyār iṟantatu. uṇmaiyō kūṟuka."eṉa iṇaintu
   kūṟalum, atu, “ām. ām. iṉṟukālai kūṭṭaik kākkum īkkaḷuṭ cila
   veḷicceṉṟirunta poḻutai nōkki, iḷamaimikka īyaraciyoṉṟu, veḷippōtal
   kūṭātu eṉiṉum, pōtantu, nam araciyoṭu kalāyttuk koṉṟu viṭṭatu" eṉṟatu.
   muṭivil akkaḷivaṇṭu taṉ araci cātaṟku aṭippaṭaiyāka niṉṟatu tāṉē
   eṉpataiyum, taṉṉā ṉikaḻnta kuḻappattiṉ payaṉē itu veṉpataiyum
   niṉaittoṟum ataṉ neñcu ataṉaic cuṭṭatu; piṉṉar ammutirvaṇṭu kaḷivaṇṭai
   nōkki, “īyaracukaṭkē ottanilaimaiyiṉṟu; avai piṟappiṉāl ottaṉa vēṉum
   tam toḻilāṟṟāṉ ciṟappaṭaikiṉṟaṉa: ātaliṉ, piṟappotta tōṟṟattēm eṉiṉum
   ciṟappuṟu toḻiṟ ceytu uyarvupeṟutalaṉṟō amaivuṭaittām"eṉṟatu.
   neñcu cuṭa varuntiya avvaṇṭum piṉṉarttaṉ toḻiṉāṭi malar vaṉam pukkatu.
   yāṇṭum amaitiyē niṉṟu viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
   ----------

                             5.yātum viṉaval

   ceṅkaimāttu vaṭakkil uḷḷatu teṉmalai. malaiccāralil kāṭu pacuntaḻai
   pōrttu iṉiya kāṭci tarukiṟatu. putarkaḷil pala vakaip pūkkaḷ malarntu
   naṟumaṇam kamaḻkiṉṟaṉa. teṉṟal melleṉa vīcukiṉṟatu. naṇpakal veppattāl
   vempi viyartta celvamakkaḷ im meṉkāṟṟiṉ varavu kaṇṭu mikka makiḻcci
   yaṭaikiṉṟaṉar. mālai maṇi mūṉṟukku mēlākiyataṉāl veyiliṉ veppamum
   ciṟitu taṇintu varukiṟatu.
   kāṉattiṉ arukē ōṭum kāṭṭāṟu ciṟitu akalamākavē uḷatu. cekkarc cevēreṉap
   parantu viḷaṅkum cemmaṇal, civanta puṭavai yoṉṟai nīṭṭip puraḷaviṭṭatu
   pōlat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ataṉ karaiyil teṉṉaikaḷ ōṅki niṉṟu kulaikaḷ tāṅkik
   kāṟṟil mella acaikiṉṟaṉa. karaiyarukē aḻakiya ūroṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
   ataṉkaṇ mikavuyarnta māṭi vīṭoṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ piṉ puṟattē
   āṟṟiṟkuk kālaṭippātai varukiṟatu. avvaḻiyē maṅkaiyar iruvar
   varukiṉṟaṉar.
   orutti māntaḷir pōṉṟa niṟamuṭaiyavaḷ. avaḷatu piṉṉiviṭṭa kūntal
   iṭaimaṟaiyumaḷavu nīṇṭirukkiṟatu. poṉṉil kal vaittiḻaitta villaiyoṉṟu
   avaḷ kuḻalil iruntu nalla aḻaku ceykiṟatu. kaivaḷaikaḷ kaṇkavarum
   vaṉappuṭaiyaṉa. nīla niṟaṅkoṇṭa avaḷatu puṭavaiyil nittilam taittatu
   pōṉṟa pūvēlaikaḷ iruntu polikiṉṟaṉa. avaḷarukē celpavaḷ ittuṇaic
   ciṟappuṭaiyaḷāka illai. ottayāṇṭiṉaḷāyiṉum, oppaṉaiyum oḻukkamum
   nōkkiṉ, muṉṉaiyavaṭku, nilaiyil tāḻntavaḷeṉṟē ivaḷ tōṉṟukiṟāḷ.
   maṇiniṟam koṇṭa ivaḷatu uḷḷattiṉ nērmaiyai, mukam iṉitu
   pulappaṭuttukiṟatu. ivaḷai aṭikkaṭi, avaḷ “tōḻi, tōḻi" eṉkiṟāḷ; avaḷai
   ivaḷum "aṉṉāy"eṉa aḻaikkiṟāḷ. ākavē, avaḷai nām talaiviyeṉṟum, ivaḷai
   avaṭkut tōḻi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvōm.
   talaiviyum tōḻiyum āṟṟiṭaikkuṟaikkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.ataṉai turutti (an
   island in the bed of a river) yeṉṟalum marapu. aṅkē, mā, vēmpu, āl,
   teṉṉai mutaliya maraṅkaḷ aḻakuṟat taḻaittup pūttuk kāyttu nalam
   kaṉintirukkiṉṟaṉa. putar taṉpāl ceṟinta pūvāl naṟumaṇam kamaḻa, teṉ
   kāṟṟu mella vantu taḷirkaḷai iṉitacaippa, tarai muḻutum aṟukupaṭarntu
   pacuṅkampalam virittatupōl iṉpam ceykiṟatu. kiḷḷaiyum puṟavum kuyilum
   pūvaiyum icaipāṭum iv viṉiya viṭam iv viruvar maṉattaiyum vēṟiṭam
   peyarātavāṟu piṇittu viṭukiṟatu. “aṉṉāy, iṅkē ciṟitē amarvōmē eṉṉa
   iṉiya iṭam;” eṉkiṉṟāḷ tōḻi.
   talaivi:-eṉṉai yātum viṉaval. tōḻi, iṉpamuṭaiya varkkē iyaṟkaik
   kāṭciyum iṉpamaḷikkiṉṟatu.
   tōḻi :-itaṟkuttāṉ “keṭṭārkku naṭṭārō il" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
   atukiṭakka; ēṉ ivvāṟu pēcukiṉṟāy? iyaṟkai yāvarkkum potu. vāḻkkaiyil
   iṉpamuṭaiyārkkēyaṉṟi, ataṉai iḻantārkkum aḵtu iṉpam vaḻaṅkuvatil
   iḻukkuvatu kiṭaiyātu.
   talaivi:-appaṭittāṉ aṟivuṭaiyōr kūṟukiṉṟaḷar. āṉāl, eṉṉaḷavil atu
   māṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
   tōḻi :- appaṭiyāṉāl, itō! iṅkē tōṉṟum teṉmalaiyum, ceḻuṅkāṉamum,
   cēyāṟum uṅkaṭku iṉpam ceyyavillaiyō?
   talaivi :- ivaṟṟaik kāṇumpōtu eṉ uḷḷam tikīr eṉṟu kalaṅkukiṉṟatu.
   māṉiṉam tuṇaiyoṭu kūṭic celvatum, paṟavaikaḷ tattam tuṇaiyōṭiruntu pāṭi
   makiḻvatum eṉakku varuttam ceyyumē eṉṟu eṉ neñcam añcukiṉṟatu.
   tōḻi :- (maruṇṭu) aṉṉāy, nī colvatu oṉṟum eṉakku viḷaṅkavillai.
   talaivar uṭaṉ irukkumpōtu ivaiyellām uṉakku iṉpattait tāṉē ceyyum?
   avarai viṭṭut taṉiyē vantiruppataṉāl ivvāṟu pēcukiṉṟāyō?
   talaivi :- tōḻi, iṉi, taṉiyētāṉē irukkavēṇṭum. ippōtu uṉṉōṭu taṉiyē
   iruppatu taṉimaiyākumā?
   tōḻi :- (piṉṉum maruṇṭu) aṉṉāy, uṉ karuttai viḷakkamāyc col, eṉakku uṉ
   pēccu ciṟitum viḷaṅkavēyillai.
   talaivi :- nēṟṟu nam kātalar pēciyatil iruntu avar nammaip pirintē
   pōvar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟēṉ. atu mutal eṉ neñcam ivvāṟu tuyaraṭaiyat
   toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.
   tōḻi :- (cuṭṭikkāṭṭi) atō, nam talaivar varukiṉṟār. nī ap putariṭaiyē
   maṟaintukoḷ. nāṉ avaruṭaṉ pēci itaṉ uṇmaiyai aṟikiṉṟēṉ.
   talaivi :- nallatu; appaṭiyē cey. (maṟaital)(talaimakaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ tōḻi
   pūppaṟippataik kāṇkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ arukē varukiṉṟāṉ.)
   talaivaṉ:- (neruṅki) tōḻi, nī iṅkē eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāy?uṉṉōṭu talaimakaḷ
   vantāḷē eṅkē?
   tōḻi :- (kaiyāṟ kāṭṭi) atō tōṉṟum putarkkup piṉṉē pūppaṟikkap pōṉārkaḷ.
   aḻaittu varalāmē. (pōka oruppaṭutal)
   talaivaṉ:- (taṭuttu) vēṇṭā. nil nil. uṉṉiṭam oru ceyti colla vēṇṭum.
   aṉṟum nī tāṉē tuṇai ceytāy?
   tōḻi :- (talaikuṉintu taṉakkuḷḷē) talaimakaḷ coṉṉatu pōl, ivar taṉ
