   pirivaittāṉ ippōtu nampāl colvārpōla irukkiṟatu. irukkaṭṭum.
   talaivaṉ :-oṉṟumillai. āṇmakaṉ oruvaṉukku uyirāvatu etu? teriyumō?
   tōḻi:-aiyaṉē, yāṉō peṇ.eṉakku atu teriyātē.
   talaivaṉ:-(muṟuvalittu) “viṉaiyē āṭavarkku uyir."atāvatu,
   pukaḻtarakkūṭiya viṉaiyaic ceyvatu tāṉ āṇmakaṉukku uyir. uyireṉpatu
   uyirotta kaṭamai.
   tōḻi:-āmām. irukkalām. ataṉai eṉakkuc colvatāl payaṉ?
   talaivaṉ:- antak kaṭamaiyaic ceyya yāṉ pōka vēṇṭum. cila nāṭkaḷē
   pirintirukka vēṇṭum. eṉṉa colkiṉṟāy?
   tōḻi :- itaittāṉ eṉakkuc colla virumpiṉīrkaḷā? appaṭiyāṉāl, itaṉōṭu
   vēṟoṉṟu colvārkaḷē? atu uṅkaṭku niṉaivirukkumē.
   talaivaṉ:-(tiṭukkiṭṭu) eṉṉa atu? eṉakku niṉaivillai.
   tōḻi:-eṉakku niṉaivirukkiṟatu: “viṉaiyē āṭavarkkuyirē, maṉaiyuṟai
   vāḷnutal makaḷirkku āṭavar uyirē"eṉṟu colvārkaḷē.
   talaivaṉ:-(makiḻntu) āmām. makaḷir tam kaṇavaṉai uyir pōlak karuti
   oḻukuvatupōla, āṭavar tām ceyya vēṇṭiya viṉaiyai uyirpōl karutic ceyya
   vēṇṭum. itutāṉē ataṉ karuttu.
   tōḻi:-ēṉ? ataṟku uḷḷa poruḷai uḷḷapaṭiyē......
   talaivaṉ:-(iṭaimaṟittu) nāṉ colvatu tavaṟēr?
   tōḻi:-...āṭavarkku viṉaiyē uyir; avartam maṉaiviyarkku avarē uyir. avar
   pirintāl, avartam makaḷirum uyir pirivar. itutāṉē uṇmaip poruḷ!
   talaivaṉ:-eppaṭiyō pōkaṭṭum. iṉṟu yāṉ talaimakaḷōṭu iṉitiruppataṟku
   aṉṟu nī ceyta
   -------- text (corresponding to printed page 106) missing here!!
   ----------
   veṟṟi peṟṟīrkaḷō eṉa aiyuṟṟup pitaṟṟuvar; ivvāṟu palappala collip
   paḻippar.
   talaivaṉ :- ēṉ? nīṅkaḷum maṉaiyiluḷḷa piṟarum avvāṟu alar uṇṭākātavāṟu
   ceyyalāmaṉṟō?
   tōḻi :- ulaivāyai mūṭalām; ūrvāyai mūṭalāmō?
   talaivaṉ :- uṇṭō , muyaṉṟāl muṭiyāp poruḷ?
   tōḻi :- eṉṉa colkiṉṟīrkaḷ.
   “cevviya tīviya colli avaṟṟoṭu
   paiya muyaṅkiya aññāṉṟu avai ellām
   poyyātal yāṉyāṅku aṟikōm maṟṟu aiya!
   akalnakar koḷḷā alar talait tantu
   pakalmuṉi veñcuram uḷḷal aṟintēṉ
   makaṉ allai maṉṟa iṉi."
   (kali - 19)
   talaivaṉ :- (muṟuvalittu) tōḻi! nī ēṉ vekuḷkiṉṟāy?
   tōḻi :- aiya! nīṅkaḷ aṉṟu ceytavaṟṟaiyum iṉṟu ceyvataiyum pārkkiṉ
   ippōtu avai . ellām poyyē eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. poykūṟi oḻukupavaṉ makaṉā
   (maṉitaṉā)?
   talaivaṉ :- nī talaimakaḷpāl koṇṭa kātalāl ivvāṟu pēcukiṉṟāy. nāṉ
   ataṟku makiḻkiṉṟēṉ. āṉāl -
   tōḻi :- "āṉāl" eṉpatu eṉṉa?
   talaivaṉ :- viṉai ceyya vēṇṭiyaviṭattu ataic ceyyātu viṭupavaṉ
   maṉitaṉākāṉ. 'ceyyat takka ceyyāmaiyāṉuṅ keṭum; alla ceyyavum keṭum.'
   itu nī aṟiyātatallavē! mēlum kātalaraip pirintu oru kāriyam ceytu vanta
   vaḻi, avar kātal māṇpuṟumē tavira, vēṟu oṉṟum uṇṭākātu. nāṉ ceṉṟu
   varukiṉṟēṉ.
   tōḻi :- (neṭitu niṉaintu) aiya,
   -----------
   “celiṉi, ceṉṟu nī ceyyum viṉai muṟṟi
   aṉpu aṟamāṟi yām uḷḷat tuṟantavaḷ
   paṇpum aṟitirō eṉṟu varuvārai
   eṉtiṟam yātum viṉaval; viṉavil
   pakaliṉ viḷaṅkumniṉ cemmal citaiyat
   tavalaruñ ceyviṉai muṟṟāmal āṇṭu ōr
   avalam paṭutalum uṇṭu”
   iṉi nī celka, ceṉṟu aṅkē ceyyum viṉaiyai muṭittukkoṇṭa piṉpē iṅkiruntu
   varuvōraip pārttu aṉpu illāmaiyāl eṉṉāl tuṟakkappaṭṭa talaimakaḷ
   nalattai aṟivīrkaḷō? eṉṟu viṉavuka; viṉaimuṭiyu muṉpu avarkaḷai viṉava
   vēṇṭā. viṉavil, cūriyaṉpōl talaimai koṇṭu viḷaṅkum umatu talaimai
   citaintuviṭum. vēṟu vakaiyāl keṭutaṟkillāta umatu ceyviṉaiyum,
   muṭivaṭaiyātapaṭi avalam uṇṭāyiṉum uṇṭākiviṭum.
   talaivaṉ :- ēṉ? eṉakku viḷaṅkac col.
   tōḻi :- eṉ tōḻi niṉ pirivāṟṟātu, iṟantālum iṟantu viṭuvāḷ. - (putar
   maṟaivilirukkum talaivi veḷippaṭukiṟāḷ)
   talaivṉ :- itō, tirumakaḷ varukiṉṟaṉaḷē.... (ceṉṟu iru kaikaḷaiyum
   paṟṟi) - nī eppōtu iṅku vantāy?
   talaivi :- kātala, ituvaraiyum eṉ tōḻi coṉṉataik - kēṭṭīrkaḷā?
   talaivaṉ :- iṉi atai niṉaiyēṉ. ulaku muḻuvatum oruṅku varuvatāyiṉum iṉi
   uṉṉaip piriyēṉ. vā. ivviyaṟkaik kāṭciyaik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟalām.
   iyaṟkaik kāṭci iṉpa ūṟṟu.
   ------------

                       6. maiyīrōti maṭanallīrē

   "maiyirōti maṭanallirē, maiyirōti maṭanallīrē" eṉṟu moḻintu vāyveruvum
   immaṭa nallāḷ yāvaḷ? ivaḷatu malarmukam vāṭiyuḷatu; maḻaikkaṇ nīr
   tuḷikkiṉṟatu; vāyitaḻ mella acaikiṉṟatu; meyyilum ciṟitu taḷarcci
   pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ivaḷai uṟṟu nōkkuvōm. ivaḷatu maṉattē peruntuyar niṉṟu
   alaikkiṉṟatu; ivaḷ aṭikkaṭi maruṇṭu nōkkukiṉṟāḷ; ām, avaḷatu uḷḷattē
   accam piṟantirukkiṟatu; eṉṉō kāraṇam?”
   ivvāṟu tammaṉattōṭu collāṭi varum ipperiyār uṭaiyālum naṭaiyālum
   ciṟanta cāṉṟōr pōlat tōṉṟukiṟār. ivar piṉṉē celvōm. atō teṉ malaiyiṉ
   aṭiyil niṟkum pāṟaimītu ēṟi niṟkiṟār. pakalavaṉum uccikaṭantu
   mēlaitticaiyai nōkkukiṉṟāṉ. veyiliṉ veppamum mikutiyāka illai. kāṭum
   pacuntalai parappi veṇ pū virintu naṟumaṇam kamaḻkiṉṟatu. māṉiṉam
   pulmēyntu makiḻcci mikukiṉṟaṉa. kiḷḷaiyum puṟāvum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp
   paṟantu tirikiṉṟaṉa. tiṉaippuṉaṅkaḷilum katirkaḷ pālkaṭṭic ciṉṉāḷil
   muṟṟum pakkuvattēyuḷḷaṉa. itō, iccāṉṟōr malaiyaṭiyiṉ mēṟṟicaiyil uḷḷa
   tiṉaippuṉattē tamkaṭ pārvaiyaic celutti ūṉṟi nōkkukiṉṟār; ēṉ? naṉṟu,
   naṉṟu. appuṉattarukē niṟkum vēṅkai marattiṉ aṭiyil vīṟṟirukkum uruvam
   yātu? teyvamō? peṇṇō? terintilatē! ām, peṇṇē: avaḷ eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāḷ?
   ★-★-★-★
   itō iccāṉṟōrum avaḷirukkum tikku nōkkiyē celkiṟār, ivar ēṉ avaḷ
   aṟiyāvaṇṇam putarkaḷiṭaiyē patuṅkippatuṅkip pōkiṉṟār. ammaṅkai yarukē
   celkiṟār pōlum. avaḷōṭu ētēṉum collāṭa virumpukiṉṟaṉarō? aṟṟaṉṟu; avaḷ
   pēccoliyaik kēṭkum aḷavilirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutukiṉṟār pōlum. iṉṟēl,
   ivar apputaraṭiyil amara māṭṭāraṉṟō? eṉṉa naṭakkiṉṟatu, kāṇpōm, neñcē,
   nī oṉṟum vēṟāka niṉaiyātē; cālpu kuṉṟat takuvaṉavaṟṟaic cāṉṟōr ceyyār.
   ★-★-★-★
   “maiyirōti, maṭanallīrē” eṉṟu moḻinta ammaṉṉavaṉ itaṉaiyaṟintāl eṉṉa
   niṉaivāṉ? 'maiyirōti' eṉṟu aḻaitta am maṇimoḻi iṉṉum eṉ ceviyakam
   niṟaiya olikkiṉṟatē! maṭa nallīrē eṉṟa avaratu māṇpu eṉ maṉattē avarai
   uruppaṭutti viṭṭatē! avaratu vāymai kāṇamāṭṭāta eṉ tāyatu tāymai eṉṉa
   payaṉuṭaiyatām. mēṉi melikiṉṟēṉ; vēṟoṉṟum niṉai villēṉ. ētukku eṉ tāy
   ivvāṟu avvēlaṉai vēṇṭukiṉṟāḷ.'
   "viṉaviṉ eṉṉaiyō avaṉai?" eṉṟoru colleḻukiṉṟatu. mēlē kūṟiyavāṟu tāṉē
   pēcippulampiya maṅkai cuṟṟu muṟṟum nōkkukiṉṟāḷ, vāṭiya mukam
   malarkiṉṟatu; paṇitta kaṇ aruḷ vaḻikiṉṟatu; viḷarttavāy nāvāl nīr
   culavap peṟukiṉṟatu. tiṇaippuṉattiṉ uḷḷirunta peṇṇorutti, tiṉaittāḷai
   vilakkik koṇṭu vēṅkaiyaṭikku varukiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷ yār? poṟuppōm; ivarkaḷ
   pēcciliruntutāṉ ivaḷai iṉṉāḷeṉa aṟital vēṇṭum.
   ★-★-★-★
   “tōḻi, muṭintu pōyiṟṟō? eṉ tāy iṉi eṉṉa ceyyap pōkiṟāḷām? uḷḷataṉ
   uṇmaiyai uḷḷavāṟu kāṇum aṟivupeṟṟum, makkaḷ ataṉaic cevvē payaṉ koḷḷa
   aṟiyātu alamaru kiṉṟaṉarē!ivarkaṭku aṟivuccuṭar uṇṭākumō? aḵtu uṇṭāku
   nāḷ eṉṟō teriyavillaiyē" eṉkiṉṟāḷ. vēṅkaiyaṭiyil vīṟṟirukkum melliyal.
   tōḻi :- ammā, nāmē avaṉai viṉaviṉāl eṉ?
   melli :-(accattōṭu) avaṉaiyā? anta vēlaṉaiyā?avaṉai eṉṉeṉṟu viṉavuvatu?
   -
   tōḻi :- murukaṉatu vēl avaṉ kaiyiluḷḷa vēl. avaṉ āṭum veṟiyāṭṭu am
   murukaṉ poruṭṭu. avvēl aṟiviṉ vaṭivam, ākavē, avaṉukku nallaṟivum
   uḷatāyirukkum. ataṉāl, avaṉatu aṟivu veḷiyākumāṟu nāmē ataṉai
   viṉavuvōmē?
   melli :-eppaṭi viṉavuvatu?
   tōḻi :- eppaṭiyā? appaṭittāṉ?
   melli :- eṉakku viḷaṅkavillaiyē?
   tōḻi :- ēṉ viḷaṅkavillai? aṉṟu naṭantatai niṉaivil koṇṭu maṟainta
   coṟkaḷāl avaṉai viṉavuvatu.
   melli :-(mayaṅkiya pārvaiyuṭaṉ) eṉṟu naṭantatai?ataṉai naṉṟākat tāṉ
   col.
   tōḻi :-aṉṟu nām taṭṭai koṇṭu kuruviyōṭṭi viḷaiyāṭi yiruntōmaṉṟō?
   melli :-ēṉ, nām iṉṟumtāṉ avvāṟē ceykiṉṟōm.
   tōḻi :- aṉṟu nam taṭṭai muṟintu pōkavē, itō immūṅkiṟ putariliruntu
   mūṅkiloṉṟai yaṟuttut taṭṭaiyoṉṟu ceytōmē, aḵtēṉum niṉaivilirukkiṟatō?
   melli :-(puṉmuṟuvaluṭaṉ). ām. peru muḻakkam ceytu koṭiya ōcaiyai
   uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟē, antat taṭṭai ceytōmē, aṉṟā?
   tōḻi :-ataṉāl tāṉē nām atai “vevvāyt taṭṭai”eṉṟōm.
   melli :-ataṉālā, aṉṟaṉṟu; ataṉaic cuḻaṟṟiya māttiraiyē, tiṉaip puṉattil
   paṭinta paṟavaikaḷ aṉaittum paṟantu ōṭiviṭṭa tōṭu, tiṉaimaṇikaḷ palavum
   citaṟiviṭṭaṉa.
   tōḻi :-etaṉālō oṉṟu. appōtu nām anta vēṅkai marattiṉ kavaṭṭil kaṭṭiṉa
   paraṇ mītu iruntapōtu eṉṉa naṭantatu?
   melli :- vaṇṭiṉam vantu poṉṉiṟam koṇṭa pūvil tēṉ uṇṭu iṉpamāka
   muraṉṟatu. nām ataṉ iṉpavōcaiyaik kēṭṭu ataṉ mītu karuttaic celutti
   niṉṟōm.
   tōḻi :-appōtu nām tiṭukkiṭṭuc caṭṭeṉap piṉpuṟam . tirumpiṉōmē, ēṉ?
   melli :-(nāṇit talai kaviḻkiṉṟāḷ)
   tōḻi :-"maiyirōti,maṭanallīrē"eṉṟoru kāḷai nammai aḻaittāṉaṉṟō? avaṉ ēṉ
   nammai "maiyirōti maṭanallirē" eṉṟāṉ. ataṉai yaṟivāyō?
   melli :-nam piṉniṉṟa avar kaṇṇiṟ paṭṭatu nam kūntal. ataṉāl avvāṟu
   aḻaittār? itu tāṉē kāraṇam?
   tōḻi :- itaṉai tuṇukkamāka aṟintu koḷḷum uṉakku iṉṉum nāṉ eṉṉa colvatu?
   melli :- illai yillai. itu tāṉ kāraṇamāka irukkalām eṉpatu eṉ karuttu.
   atu kiṭakkaṭṭum nī col.
   tōḻi :-appōtu avaṉ nammai eṉṉa kēṭṭāṉ?
   melli :-keṭuti viṉaviṉār.
   tōḻi :- atu teriyātā? eṉṉa collit tam keṭuti viṉaviṉāṉ?
   melli :-yāṉ eyta ampupaṭṭup puṇ mikuntu perun tuyar koṇṭu yāṉai yoṉṟu
   taṉ uyarnta maruppu viḷaṅka ippuṉat tarukē vanta tuṇṭō? eṉṟār.
   tōḻi :- avaṉuṭaṉ vanta nāykaḷ kuraittuk kutittu nāṟpuṟamum ōṭivara,
   avaṉ ciṟitu nēram kaḻintapiṉ ceṉṟāṉaṉṟō?
   melli :-tōḻi, avaratu cāntaṇinta mārpum, vil cumanta tōḷum,
   taṉittirunta nammiṭattē avar collāṭiya takaimaiyum eṉ neñcaik kavarntu
   koṇṭaṉa. avar colliya coṟkaḷ iṉṉum eṉ ceviyil olikkiṉṟaṉa. avar poruṭṭu
   nām varuntum ivvaruttattai aṉṉai iṉṉum aṟiyavillaiyē. itaṟkoru
   vaḻiyumillaiyā?
   tōḻi :-ataṟkuttāṉ takka vaḻitēṭappaṭukiṟatē? ataṟkuḷ nī ēṉ tuyarkiṉṟāy?
   melli :- vēlaṉaikkoṇṭu veṟi yayarkiṉṟaṉaḷē aṉṉai; ituvō vaḻi.
   tōḻi :- atutāṉ eṉṟu nam tāyum niṉaikkiṉṟāḷ. avvēlaṉum avaṭkut takkavāṟu
   tāṉ kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ.
   melli:- eṉṉa atu?
   tōḻi :- eṉṉavā? "em iṟai aṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉṟu innōy, taṇi maruntu aṟival"
   eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ.
   melli :- (tiṭukkiṭṭu) uṇmaiyākavā? vēlaṉum ivvāṟu poy kūṟuvāṉā?
   vēlaṉatu iṟaivaṉ murukaṉaṉṟō; avaṉ nammai aṇaṅkiṉavaṉ illaiyē;
   poymoḻintu oḻukum vēlaṉ poymmaiyai nam tāy aṟintilaḷē, itaṟku eṉ
   ceyvatu?
   tōḻi :- ippaṭittāṉ vēlaṉ kūṟuvāṉ. aṟiyā mākkaḷ ataṉai uṇmai yeṉṟu
   karutuvar, iṉṟum atait tāṉ nam tāyum kēṭkap pōkiṉṟāḷ.
   melli :- iṉṟum ataittāṉē collap pōkiṉṟāṉ?
   tōḻi :-āmām. āṉāl, iṉṟu yāṉum vēlaṉaik kaṇṭu oṉṟu kēṭkap pōkiṟēṉ. eṉ
   kēḷviyāl vēlaṉ poymmaiyum, nam oḻukkattiṉ meymmaiyum viḷakkamaṭaiyap
   pōkiṉṟaṉa.
   melli :-eppaṭi? eṉakku atai viḷaṅkaccol.
   tōḻi :- vēlaṉ iṉṟu nam tāyiṭam murukaṉ nammai aṇaṅkiṉaṉ; eṉpāṉāyiṉ,
   veṟi koḷvēla'uṉatu iṟaivaṉ, mata mikka vēḻattiṉ meyyil ūṭuruvat taṉ
   ampaic celutti, piṉ ataṉ aṭic cuvaṭupaṟṟi vēṭṭam cellumō? eṉa viṉavak
   karutukiṉṟēṉ. viṉaviṉ eṉ?
   melli :-(muṟuvalittu) viṉaviṉ eṉṉām? yāṉai vēṭṭam purintu vanta vīraṉ
   oruvaṉai nām namakku iṟaivaṉākak koṇṭuḷḷōm eṉ terintu koḷvāṉ.
   ★-★-★-★
   tōḻi ēṉ ciṟaippuṟattai nōkkukiṉṟāḷ. aṅkē ēṉ vēlikkaṇ paṭarntuḷḷa
   taḻaikaḷ acaikiṉṟaṉa. nallatu. aṅkirun toru valviṟ kāḷai eṉ mēṟkut
   ticai nōkkic celkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ ētō taṉakkuḷ collik koḷkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ
   piṉceṉṟu kēṭpōm.
   kāḷai:- iṉi, namakkum im melliyal nallāḷukkum uṇṭākiya oḻukkam avaḷ
   tāykkum tamarkkum pulaṉāki viṭum. ākavē, ivaḷai iṉi iṅkē talaikkūṭal
   aritu. ākavē, iṉic cāṉṟōraic celutti varaintu koḷvatē kaṭaṉ.
   ā, itō iccāṉṟōrum nammuṭaṉ vantu avaṉ collik koḷvataik kēṭṭu
   makiḻkiṉṟār. ivar eṉṉa collukiṉṟār pārppōm.
   cāṉṟōr:- ivvilvīraṉ ceyta muṭivē ciṟantatu. ivaṉ im muṭiviṟku varak
   kāraṇam yātu? it tōḻiyiṉ nalluraiyaṉṟō? ivaḷatu aṟivuṭaimaiyai
   eṉṉeṉpēṉ, ittalai makaṉ ciṟaippuṟam niṟpatai yuṇarntu avaṉ im muṭipu
   ceyyumāṟu pēciviṭṭāḷ. atumaṭṭō, talaimakaṭkum ōrāṟṟāl tēṟutal
   kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. kaṟpuṭai makaḷir kaṇavaṉaiyallatu piṟa teyvaṅkaḷaiyum
   maṉattālum niṉaiyār. vēlaṉ murukaṉ aṇaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟavaḻi, talai makaḷ am
   murukaṉai vaṇaṅkal vēṇṭivarum. aḵtu avaḷ kaṟpu neṟikku māṟākum.
   ataṉiṉṟum ivaḷ avaḷait tappuvittuviṭṭāḷ. veṟiyum vilakkiyāki
   viṭukiṟatu. nallatu, nām iṉṟu iṅkē vantatum naṉṟē yāyiṟṟu. ivaḷatu
   ceykai ēṉaimakaḷirkku naṉṉeṟi kāṭṭum nayamuṭaiyatu. ākavē, itaṉaip
   pāṭṭiṭai vaittut tamiḻulakil niṟuvuvēṉ.”
   ★-★-★-★
   cāṉṟōr, tam uṭaimaṭippil iruntu ōr ōlai naṟukkum eḻuttāṇiyum
   eṭukkiṉṟār, marattaṭiyil amarntu. tām karutiyataṉai eḻutukiṉṟār. ivar
   eḻutik koṇṭirukkaṭṭum. nām ammakaḷir irukkumiṭam celvōm.
   tōḻi :- atumaṭṭumā, nam tāyum namakkum talaimakaṉukkum uṇṭākiya
   toṭarpai yaṟintu koḷvāḷ. piṉpu, nam kaṟpu neṟikkum iḻukku uṇṭākātē.
   melli :- tōḻi, nī ceyvatu naṉṟē. ippōtē viraintu ceṉṟu ataṉaic cey.
   tōḻi :-naṉṟu. avvāṟē ceykiṉṟēṉ. (pōtal)
   ★-★-★-★
   melliyalum taṉ nallakam pukukiṉṟāḷ; tōḻiyum kaṇmaṟaivil ceṉṟuviṭṭāḷ.
   iccāṉṟōr tām eḻutiyatai muṭittuk koṇṭu paṭikkiṉṟār; ataṉaik kēṭpōm;
   "amma vāḻi tōḻi nammalai
   amaiyaṟut tiyaṟṟiya vevvāyttaṭṭaiyiṉ
   naṟuvirai yāra maṟaveṟin tuḻuta
   uḷaikkuraṟ ciṟutiṉai kavartaliṟ kiḷaiyamal
   peruvarai yaṭukkattuk kurīi yōppi
   ōṅkiruñ cilampiṉ oḷḷiṉar naṟuvī
   vēṅkaiyaṅ kavaṭṭiṭai nivanta vitaṇattup
   poṉmaru ṇaṟuntā tūtun tumpi
   iṉṉicai yōrā viruntaṉa māka;
   maiyī rōti maṭanal lirē
   novviyaṟ pakaḻi pāynteṉap puṇkūrntu
   evvamoṭu vanta vuyarmarup poruttalnum
   puṉattuḻip pōka luṟumō maṟṟeṉac
   ciṉavuk koḷ ñamali ceyirttuppuṭai yāṭac
   collik kaḻinta valviṟ kāḷai
   cāntā rakalamun takaiyum mikanayan
   tīṅkunām uḻakkum evva muṉarāḷ,
   naṉṉar neñcamoṭu mayaṅki veṟiyeṉa
   aṉṉai tanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ
   emmiṟai yaṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉ ṟinnōy
   taṇimarun taṟival eṉṉu māyiṉ,
   viṉavi ṉevaṉō maṟṟē, kaṉalciṉa
   maiyal vēḻa meyyuḷam pōka
   ūṭṭi yaṉṉa vūṉpura ḷampoṭu
   kāṭṭumāṉ aṭivaḻi yoṟṟi
   vēṭṭañ cellumō nummiṟai yeṉavē.”
         - (akam, 388)
   ★-★-★-★
   pāṭṭiṉ nalam kāṇṭal
   itaṉkaṇ "ammavāḻi" yeṉpatu toṭaṅki, "iruntaṉamāka" (9) eṉpatu varai,
   melliyal nallāḷ valviṟkāḷaiyākiya talaimakaṉ keṭutiviṉavi varuṅkāl
   iruntanilai viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅkē avarkaḷ mūṅkil aṟuttu 'vevvāyt
   taṭṭai" ceytu. ataṉāl puṉattiṟ paṭinta kuruvikaḷai oppiṉa ceyti,
   "amaiyaṟuttu iyaṟṟiya vevvāyt taṭṭaiyiṉ," (2) "peruvaraiyaṭukkattuk
   kuruviyōppi" (5) eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl kuṟikkap paṭukiṟatu. akkuruvikaḷai
   oppiyataṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvataṟkāka, “uḷaik kural ciṟutiṉai kavartaliṉ"
   (4) eṉkiṉṟār. attiṉaiyiṉ arumaiyai uṇarttum poruṭṭu, attiṉaippuṉam
   āṅkiruntu cantaṉa maraṅkaḷai muṟṟa eṟintu, avviṭattaiyum aritiṉ uḻutu
   payir ceyyappaṭṭateṉkiṉṟār. itaṉai “naṟuvirai yāram aṟa eṟintu uḻuta
   uḷaik kural ciṟutiṉai" eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār. vantu paṭinta kuruvikaḷum
   oṉṟiraṇṭalla. pala eṉṟaṟku “kiḷaiyamal kurīi" eṉkiṉṟār.
   iṉi, ittiṉaip puṉamirunta iṭam teṉmalaiyiṉ carivu; tiṉaimuṟṟum kālam
   vēṅkai malarum kālamākum. appōtu tāṉ appuṉam kākkap paṭutal vēṇṭum.
   tiṉaippayirum mika vuyaramāka vaḷarntiruntatu. ākavē, puṉaṅkāttaṟku
   amaikkum paraṇ mika vuyarntirukka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ik karuttellām
   tōṉṟavē, ic cāṉṟōr, “ōṅkiruñ cilampiṉ oḷḷiṉar naṟuvi, vēṅkaiyaṅ
   kavaṭṭiṭai nivanta itaṇattu’ eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār.
   iṉi, paraṇmītirunta naṅkaiyar, kuruviyōppi, paraṇarukē malarntu
   maṇakkum vēṅkai malariṟ paṭintu tātutum tumpiyiṉ iṉṉicaiyil karuttaic
   celutti yiruntaṉaraṉṟō? iṉṟēl, aṅkē valviṟ kāḷaiyaik kaṇṭiruppar.
   ataṉāltāṉ tōḻi, "tumpi iṉṉicai orā iruntaṉamāka” eṉkiṉṟāḷ.
   iṉi, "maiyirōti" eṉpatumutal, "pōkaluṟumō maṟṟu" eṉpatuvarai
   valviṟkāḷai vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷākum. puṉam nōkki vantavaṉ etirē tam kariya
   kuḷirnta kuḻalaik kāṭṭit tam iḷamai nalam ilaka niṉṟaṉar immakaḷir.
   keṭuti viṉavivaruvōṉ,avar muṉvantu niṉṟu cevviyaṟintu kēṭkum attuṇai
   maṉavamaitiyilaṉāvāṉ. vēṟu ocai ceyyiṉ, avar añcuvar. avaṉ kaṇṇetirē
   tōṉṟuvatu avartam kariya kuḻalē. ataṉāl, "maiyirōti maṭanallīrē"
   eṉkiṉṟāṉ. atu kēṭṭa ammaṭavār tiṭukkiṭṭu avaṉ mukanōkkiṉar. avaruḷ
   talaiviyiṉ kūriya pārvai avaṉukku varuttattaic ceytatu. ataṉai ōrāṟṟāl
   maṟaittuk koṇṭu, "yāṉ eyta pakaḻiyāl puṇ mikuntu eḻunta tuyarattōṭu
   vanta yāṉai yoṉṟaik kaṇṭīrō” eṉṟu kēṭkaluṟṟu, "novviyaṟ pakaḻi
   pāynteṉap puṇkūrntu, evvamoṭu vanta uyar marup poruttal num puṉattuḻip
   pōka luṟumō” eṉkiṉṟāṉ, taṉ ampiṉai "novviyaṟ pakaḻi"eṉṟataṉāl, taṉ
   ampiṉ vaṉmaiyum, avaḷatu pārvaiyiṉ kaṭumaiyum cuṭṭiṉāṉ, “pāynteṉap puṇ
   kūrntu"eṉṟataṉāl, ampu kuṟivaryttu nōy ceytatum, tāṉ vēṭkaiyuṟṟu
   vētaṉai yuṟṟatum kūṟiṉāṉ. "evvamoṭuvanta uyarmarup poruttal" eṉṟu
   kūṟavē, ciṉam mikuntu vārātu, puṇṇuṟṟeytiya āṟāttuṉpattāl vantatu yāṉai
   eṉṟum, āyiṉum aḵtu uyar maruppuṭaiya pēr yāṉaiyeṉṟum, tāṉ talaiviyait
   talaikkūṭa vēṇṭumeṉṉum vēṭkai nōyāl veytuṟṟu vanta ceytiyum
   cuṭṭiyirukkiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ vantatu pōla, yāṉaiyum vantatuṇṭō eṉṟaṟku,
   "numpuṉattuḻip pōkaluṟumō” eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ.
   ivvāṟu kēṭṭavaṉ neṭitu nillātu ciṟitu nērattiṟkuḷ kaḻintāṉ; avaṉōṭu
   vanta vēṭṭai nāykaḷ vilaṅkukaḷiṉ naṭamāṭṭamaṟintu ciṉaṅkoṇṭu kuraittuk
   kutittu ōṭattalaippaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl avaṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē viraintēka
   vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ; iṉṟēl, ciṟitu neṭittē ceṉṟiruppaṉ. ikkaruttai,
   “ciṉavukkoḷḷumali ceyirttup puṭaiyāṭac collikkaḻinta valviṟkāḷai” eṉṟu
   kuṟikkiṉṟār.
   itukāṟum paṇṭu nikaḻntatu kūṟiya tōḻi, iṉi, nikaḻvatu kūṟattoṭaṅki,
   taṅkaḷ nilaiyum, tāyatu nilaiyum kūṟaluṟukiṉṟāḷ:
   “valviṟkāḷai, cāntār akalamum takaiyum mika nayantu īṅku nām uḻakkum
   evvam uṇarāḷ naṉṉar neñcamoṭu mayaṅki veṟiyeṉa aṉṉai.”
   tāy taṉ makaḷatu evvam uṇarāḷāyiṉaḷ; ataṉāl aṉpuṭaiya avaḷatu nalla
   neñcam mayaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu; vēlaṉaik koṇṭu veṟiyeṭukka niṉaikkiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu
   itaṉāl terikiṟatu.
   iṉi veṟiyāṭum vēlaṉ kūṟuvatai muṉṉaṟintu,
   "aṉṉai tanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ emmiṟai aṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉṟu innōy taṇi
   maruntu aṟival" eṉpaṉa, avvāṟu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉ, nām talaivaṉ keṭuti viṉāvi
   vantu talaip peytataik kūṟi "viṉaviṉ evaṉōmaṟṟē? kaṉal ciṉa maiyal
   vēḻameyyuḷam pōka ūṭṭi aṉṉa ūṉpuraḷ ampoṭu kāṭṭumāṉ aṭivaḻi oṟṟi vēṭṭam
   cellumōtum iṟai eṉavē” eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāḷ.
   "nummiṟai vēṭṭam cellumō?" eṉṟāl, avaṉ "avvāṟu celvōṉ nam iṟai" eṉṟu
   uṇarvāṉ.
   ūṉ paṭintu utiram vārac cekkarc cevēleṉac civantu tōṉṟum ampiṉai
   "ūṭṭiyaṉṉa ūṉpuraḷ ampu" eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷārē, ippulavar yārāka irukkalām?
   ūṉmikap paṟṟip puraḷum ampiṉai, ūṉ ūṭṭiyatu pōṉṟa ampu eṉkiṉṟa ivar
   "ūṭṭiyaṉṉa oṇtaḷirc ceyalai" (akam 68) eṉappāṭiya pulavarāṉa avarō
   eṉṉum niṉaivu piṟakkiṟataṉṟō? avarē tāṉ itaṉaip pāṭiyavarum. avar peyar
   ūṭṭiyār eṉpatu.
   --------

                               7.poruṇmoḻi

   yāṉ ciṟitu nāṭkaḷukkumuṉ oru āṅkila nūlaip paṭittu varuṅkāl, ittalaip
   poruḷpaṟṟiya coṟpoḻi voṉṟu ataṉkaṇ irukkak kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉaiyāṉ nuṇukip
   paṭittu varuṅkālattil, cila nuṇporuḷkaḷ amain tiruppataiyuṇarntu ataṉ
   uṭporuḷait tamiḻil eḻutiṉ palarkkum payaṉpaṭu- meṉṟeṇṇiṉēṉākaliṉ,
   itupōtu eḻutuvēṉāyiṉēṉ.
   poruṇmoḻi yeṉpatu paḻamoḻiyaip pōl nuṇ poruḷai yakattē koṇṭa uyarntōr
   kūṟum naṉmoḻiyākum. itu nam tamiḻ nūlkaḷil “poruḷurai" yeṉṟum
   “poruṇmoḻik kāñci" yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu. *[1]"poyyil pulavaṉ
   poruḷurai yeṉṟu cīttalaic cāttaṉārālum, 'mūturai poruntiya"eṉṟa
   cūttirattuḷ"poruṇmoḻikkāñci" yeṉṟu aiyaṉāritaṉārālum kūṟappaṭutal
   kāṇka. poruṇmoḻik kāñciyāvatu, "erintilaṅku caṭaimuṭi muṉivar, purintu
   kaṇṭa poruṇmoḻintaṉṟu"[2](pu-ve-mālai 271) eṉpar. poruṇmoḻi yeṉṟatoṭar
   poruḷai moḻital eṉaviriyum vēṟṟumait tokaiyākum. moḻi, mutaṉilait
   toḻiṟpeyar. poruḷāvatu muṉivarkaḷ virumpik kaṇṭu teḷinta
   uṇmaiyeṉpatākum.
   -----
   [1]. maṇi.22-6/
   [2]. pu.ve.mālai'cū.12.
   ----------
   itaṉkaṇ muṉivarāvār 'caṭaimuṭiyuṭaiyarāy uyarnta voḻukkamum'
   ulakiṉaiyum ataṟku muḻumutalākiya iṟaivaṉaiyum oppakkāṇum ñāṉak
   kaṇṇumuṭaiyarāy, makkaṭku vēṇṭiya aṟivu nūlkaḷum piṟavum ākkittarum
   pēraruḷ uṭaiya periyōrāvar eṉpatu aṟiñar muṭipu. ivartam aṟivum āṟṟalum
   ēṉaiyarkkuḷḷaṉa pōlvaṉa alla. curuṅkac colliṉ, makkaḷuṭ kaṟṟōriṉum
   ciṟanta kalviyaṟivum, oḻukalāṟum uṭaiyōr eṉpatu mikaiyākātu. ākavē,
   muṉivar perumakkaḷ nammaṉōrkku aṟivukoḷuttu māṟṟāl uraippaṉavē poruṇ
   moḻiyeṉpatu aṟiñar koḷḷum koḷkaiyāyiṟṟu.
   ipporuṇmoḻi viḻumiya poruḷait taṉṉakattē koṇṭu perukiyum, curuṅkiyum
   varutal nam nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. curuṅki varutalē perumpāṉmai- yeṉiṉum,
   curuṅkiyē varutal vēṇṭumeṉṟa varaiyaṟaiyiṉmaiyāl perukiyum varutal
   iyalpāyiṟṟu. ivai ettuṇaic curukkamāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō attuṇaiyum poruṭ
   perukkamuṭaiyavātal kaṇkūṭu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tirukkuṟaḷ oṉṟē yamaiyum.
   ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāl viḻumiyaporuḷait taṉṉakattē yuṭaittāy ovvoru
   tirukkuṟaḷum amainti, ruttalum, toṭṭaṉait tūṟum maṇaṟkēṇiyaip pōlak
   kaṟṟaṉaittuṟum poruḷāḻamuṭaimai ciṟattalum koṇṭu poruṇ moḻiyātalil
   evvakaiyiṉum muṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ittirukkuṟaḷai oru periyōr “kaṭukait
   tuḷaittuēḻ kaṭalaip pukaṭṭik kuṟukat taṟitta kuṟaḷ" eṉṟaṉar eṉpa.
   ivviyalpē poruṇmoḻikkaṇ iruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatu āṅkilap periyōrkaḷiṉ
   muṭipumākum. poruṇ moḻikkaṇ varum poruḷ tattam maṉattiṉkaṇ muḷaittut
   tōṉṟiya paḻutta eṇṇaṅkaḷākavum, paranta ulakil nilavum poruḷkaḷaip
   pulamaiyāṟ kaṇṭu ārāyntu teḷinta muṭipukaḷākavum iruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatum,
   avai oru cila coṟkaḷālākiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatum, avai
   yārāyum tōṟum payaṉtaruvaṉavāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatum
   poruṇmoḻiyiyalpukaḷai yārāyum āṅkilap pulavarkaḷiṉ koḷkai. curuṅkac
   colliṉ, paṉaiyaḷaviṉavāya poruḷum muṭiyum oruṅkē tiṇikkap peṟṟut tiṉai
   yaḷaviṟṟāya coṟṟoṭarkkaṇ viḷaṅkuvatu poruṇmoḻiyiṉ uyirnilaiyeṉpar.[1]
   ---
   1. *"The essence of aphorism is the compression of a mass of thought
   and observation into a single saying.” - John Morley.
   -----
   iṅṅaṉam, coṟcurukkamum poruṭperukkamu muṭaimaiyāl, itaṉaip poṉṉēpōṟ
   pulavarkaḷum maṟṟavarkaḷum pōṟṟi oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. peruṅkāppiyattuṭ
   ciṟantu viḷaṅkuñ cintāmaṇipāṭiya tiruttakkatēvarum tirukkuṟaḷāya poruṇ
   moḻikkaṇiruntu cila koṇṭu tam, nūliṉkaṇ vaittiḻaittu aḻaku ceytu
   koḷvārāyiṉar. avaṟṟuḷ, [2]“toḻutakaiyuḷḷum paṭaiyoṭuṅkum oṉṉār -
   aḻutakaṇṇirum aṉaittu’ [3] “ceyka poruḷaic ceṟunar cerukkaṟukkum,
   eḵkataṉiṟ kūriya til’ eṉpaṉa aṭaṅkum.
   ----
   [2]. tirukkuṟaḷ - 828.
   [3]. kuṟaḷ - 759.
   ----
   ivaṟṟuḷ, mutaṟkaṇṇatāya poruṇmoḻiyai,
   [1]. “toḻuta tamkaiyiṉuḷḷuntuṟu muṭiyakattuñcōra
   aḻutakaṇṇiṉuḷḷum aṇikalattakattum āyntu
   paḻutukaṇṇarintukollum paṭaiyuṭaṉeṭuṅkum paṟṟā(tu)
   oḻikayār kaṇṇumtēṟṟam teḷikuṟṟār viḷikuṟṟārē.”
   eṉṟa pāṭṭilum, iraṇṭāvataṉai.
   [2]. “ceykaporuḷyārumceṟuvāraicceṟukiṟkum
   eḵkupiṟitillaiyiruntēyuyirumuṇṇum
   aiyamilaiyiṉpamaṟaṉōṭavaiyumākkum
   poyyilporuḷēporuḷmaṟṟallapiṟaporuḷē.”
   eṉṟa pāṭṭilum amaittuk koṇṭiruttalum pulappaṭum. ------

   vināyaka purāṇam pāṭiya āciriyarum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ poruṭpālē tampurāṇam
   pāṭuntoḻiṟku vāyttaporuḷum karuviyumākak koṇṭatu, ipporuṇmoḻi
   nūṟkaṭkiṭantu aṇiceytalē yaṉṟit toḻiṟkaṇmuṭṭaṟukkum tuṇaik
   karuviyātalum valiyuṟuttum. ivvāṟu ivai payaṉpaṭutalōṭu amaiyātu
   iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl kalaṅkit tikaippārkkuk kalakkantīrkkumaruntumāy uṇmaip
   poruḷaṟitaṟkutavi ceytal kuṟittē pēkkaṉ eṉṉum pulavar perumakaṉār ivai
   aṇiyātalē yaṉṟit toḻilmuṭṭiṟuttum, kalakkattiṭait teḷivupiṟappittum
   nalamceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum karuttuṟak kūṟiṉar. [3]. ippēkkaṉ eṉpavarē
   piṟitōriṭattil ipporuṇmoḻikaḷai uppuṅ kaḻikaṭku oppumai kūṟi,
   kaḻiyiṭattu uppiṉaippeṟṟu, vēṇṭumiṭattuk kalantu cuvaiyākkik kōṭalpōl,
   ivaṟṟiṉiṭattu meypporuḷ kaṇṭu vēṇṭumiṭattut tuṇaiceytu koḷḷalāmeṉṟār.
   ---
   [3]. “These wise sayings, said Bacon, the auther of some of the wisest
   of them, are not only for ornament but for action and business, having
   a point or edge, whereby knots in business are pierced and discovered.
   And he applauds Cicero’s description of such sayings as salpits, that
   you may extract salt out of them and sprinkle it where you will” - John
   Morley. -
   ------------
   ‘muntaiyiruntu naṭṭōr koṭuppiṉ, nañcu muṇpar naṉinā karikar" eṉpatu
   naṟṟiṇaikkaṇ varuvatōr (355) poruṇmoḻiyākum. itaṉai āciriyar
   tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār “peyakkaṇṭum nañcuṇṭamaivar nayattakka, nākarikam vēṇṭu
   pavar" eṉṟa kuṟaḷiṉkaṇ (50) peytu koṇṭaṉar. mēlum, “nīriṉ ṟamaiyā
   vulakam pōlat, tammiṉ ṟamaiyā nantayattu” eṉṟa ataṉ kaṉanīriṉṟamaiyātu
   ulakam eṉpatu oru poruṇmoḻi (naṟ.1) itaṉṉayum tirukkuṟaḷil
   “nīriṉṟamaiyā tulakeṉiṉ yāryārkkum, vāṉiṉ ṟamaiyā toḻukku eṉṉuṅ
   kuṟaḷiṉkaṭ (kuṟaḷ.20) peytu koṇṭaṉar.
   innaṟṟiṇaip pāṭṭil “tāmarait taṇṭā turati mīmicaic cāntiṟ ṟoṭutta
   tīntēṉ pōlap puraiya maṉṟa puraiyōr kēṇmai" “eṉavaruvatu oru poruṇ
   moḻiyākum. itaṉ poruḷ - tāmaraittātiṉaiyum cantaṉattātiṉaiyum ūti
   accantaṉamrattiṟ ceyta vaḷḷattuḷ vaittaviṭattu avai iṭattu nikaḻporuḷum
   iṭamumāyiyaintu tam perumaipulap paṭuttiṉāṟpōla uyarntōr
   tammiṭattē vaittu kēṇmaiyum perumai pulappaṭutti viḷaṅkum eṉpatu.
   itaṉai maṇivācakap perun takaiyār tam tiruk kōvaiyāriṉkaṇ, “cīriya
   lāviyum yākkaiyu meṉṉac ciṟantamaiyāṟ, kāriyal vāṭkaṇṇi eṇṇaka
   lārkamalaṅ kalanta, vēriyum cantum viyaṟan teṉak kaṟpil niṟparaṉṉē,
   kāriyal kaṇṭarvaṇ ṭillai vaṇaṅku meṅkātalarē, ‘ eṉṟa tiruppāṭṭil (301)
   amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. ivai pōlum poruṇmoḻiyāṭcikaḷ palavātaliṉ immaṭṭil
   niṟuttukiṉṟēṉ.
   ipporuṇmoḻikaḷ pulavar perumakkaḷ iyaṟṟum nūlkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kiṭantu
   ulakiyalil makkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar kūṭiyum pirintum oruṅkiyaintum, piṟa
   nikaḻccikaḷaik kaṇṭum. uṇarum uṇmaikaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   vāḻkkaiccākāṭu ukaikkum makaṉ avvāḻkkaikkuriya māṇpukaḷaiyuṇarntu
   vallaṉāyiruppiṉ, atu ūṟiṉṟāki āṟṟai yiṉitu ceṉṟu payaṉtaruvatākum;
   ataṉaiyaṟiyāṉāyiṉ, avvāḻkkai tuṉpanilaiyamāy mikappala nōykaḷait
   talaittalaittaruvatāy muṭiyum. ivviyalpu paṟṟiyē ulakiyalaṟiviṉai
   vaḷarkkum poruṇ moḻikaḷ mikka iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavāya- maintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   vāḻkkai arampum allalum karampum ceṟintiruttalāl, iṉṉōraṉṉa
   poruṇmoḻikaḷaik kiṭantaṉa kiṭantāṅkē eṭuttukkōṭal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟiṉkaṇ
   orārāycciyum vēṇṭāvām. innalam kuṟittē, ivai nāmaṟiyum palavuṇmaikaḷuḷ,
   oṉṟai vilakkiyātal, vēṟoṉṟait taḻuviyātal, oruṇmaiyiṉ
   orupuṭaiyaivilakkiyātal, maṟṟorupuṭaiyai mikaipaṭa viritturaittātal
   cellātu ettakaiya kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkum, nilaikkum, oḻuṅkiṟkuntaka
   amaintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpar. ātalāl, ivai makkaḷ vāḻviṟkeṉavamainta
   vitikaḷ eṉpatu mikaiyākātu.
   itukāṟum nāmporuḷmoḻikaḷāvaṉa ivaiyeṉṟum, avai potuvakaiyiṟ paṭumāṟu
   ituveṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ potuviyalpum aṟintōm. iṉi ivaṟṟāl nām peṟum
   aṟiviyalpaiyum, ipporuṇmoḻikaḷ payaṉpaṭu māṟṟaiyum oruciṟitu kāṇpām.
   aṟivāvatu "nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmaiyum uḷḷa vāṟē yuṇartal"
   eṉpatu. itu makkaḷ tōṉṟumpōtē uṭaṉ tōṉṟi avarait toḻiṟpaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
   nām peṟṟatum peṟuvatumākiya aṟiviṉ iyalpiṉai āṅkilap perumakkaḷ
   nālvakaippaṭuttut talaimai, kaṭaimai, iṭaimai mutaliya ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ
   kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉar. oruvaṉ taṉ iyaṟkaiyaṟiviṉāl poruḷiyiyalpuṇarntu
   peṟuvatu talaiyāya aṟivu eṉṟum, ēṉai makkaḷiṉ oḻukkaneṟikaḷil niṉṟum
   avarōṭu payiṉṟum avvāṟē poruḷkaḷaiyuṇarntum peṟuvatu ataṉiṟ kuṟainta
   ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya aṟivāmeṉṟum, mūṉṟāvatu pala nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟu avai
   kāṭṭum neṟikkaṇ maṉattaic celuttip poruḷkaḷai yārāyntu teḷintu
   peṟuvatāya aṟivu eṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṟ kaṭaiyāya aṟivāvatu nūlkaḷiṭattum
   āciriyarkaḷiṭattum kaṟṟatuṇaiyē peṟuvatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. [1]ippākupāṭṭiṉ
   kaṭ poruṇmoḻikaḷ, mūṉṟām pakutikkaṇ amaintu, uṇmaiyoṉṟiṉ oru cār
   iyalpiṉai namakkuṇartti ēṉaiyavaṟṟai ārāyumāṟu ceyyavallaṉavākiṉṟaṉa.
   --------
   [1]. * “It has been said that our knowledge is this; that we know best,
   first, what we have divined by native instinct; second what we have
   learned by experience of men and things; third what we have learned not
   in books, but by books - that is, by the reflections that they suggest;
   fourth, last and least what we have learned in books or with masters.
   The virtue @f an aphorism comes under the third of these heads; it
   conveys a portion of a truth with such point as to set us thinking on
   what remains.’ - J. Morley.
   ----------------
   immūṉṟām pakutikkaṭpaṭum aṟivu ulakil makkaḷaiyum piṟaporuḷkaḷaiyum
   kaṇṭum paḻakiyum uṇarntum peṟap paṭutalāl, itaṉai vāḻkkaiyaṟivu eṉṟal
   poruntuvatām. itu oruvaṉ nalvāḻkkaikkut tuṇaiceyyum aṟivup poruḷ
   niṟainta moḻiyām. oruvaṉ kaṟpatum, kaṟṟārōṭu paḻakuvatum ellām ulakil
   nalvāḻvu naṭātti nalam peṟutaṟ poruṭṭēyām. ākavē, avaṉ kaṟkum nūlkaḷuḷ
   ip poruṇmoḻikaḷ iṭam peṟāvāyiṉ, avaiyum avaṟṟaip paṭikkuṅkāṟ kaḻinta
   poḻutum payaṉpaṭātoḻivaṉavēyākum. ākavē, ipporuṇmoḻi ovvoru nūlilum
   amaital iṉṟiyamaiyātām.
   nām vāḻum ikkālamum, nammaṉap pāṉmaiyum putiya vuṇarcciyum oḻukkamum
   koṇṭa muṉṉēṟṟa meṉṉum poruḷāl iḻukkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. "oru ūriṉkaṇ oru
   aracaṉ iruntāṉ - eṉṟu toṭaṅkum cuvaṭikaḷ maṟaikiṉṟaṉa; iṉṉayāṇṭil
   ittiṅkaḷil innāḷil innikaḻcci" naṭaipeṟṟateṉṉum muṟai eḻuntu
   nilavukiṉṟatu. epporuḷaik kēṭpiṉum, epporuḷaik kaṟpiṉum, apporuḷaik
   kēṭṭavāṟō, kaṟṟavāṟō uṇarta liṉṟip palvakaiyiṉum ārāyvatum,
   muṭipukūṟutalumē eṅkum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē nampaṇṭai
   varalāṟum, aṟital vēṇṭumeṉṉum avā nākarikamum eṅkum ellōru maṉattilum
   eḻuntulavutalaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvuṇarcci mikkueḻum ikkālattil, ‘paṇṭai
   varalāṟṟārāycciyum, poruḷiyalpuṇarcciyum, araciyal kiḷarcciyum viravic
   cellum ikkālattil, makkaḷiyalpaiyum ārāytal vēṇṭumeṉpatu
   kūṟāmalēyamaiyum. makkaḷiyalpu poruṇmoḻikkaṇ aṭaṅki paruttalāl,
   avvārāycci poruṇmoḻi yārāycciyē-yāyiṟṟu.
   ivvārāyccikkup peritum payaṉpaṭum poruṇ moḻikaḷaik kūṟavallavar oruvar
   vēṇṭumaṉṟē. avaryār? avarē nampulavar perumakkaḷ. avarē, ulakil nām
   vāḻviṉiyalpaiyuḷḷavāṟēyuraikkum āṟṟal paṭaittavarāvar; cila
   uṇmaikaḷaiyum meymmaikaḷaiyum eṭutturaikkum cilariṉum, ivarkaḷ
   pōṟṟaṟkuriyar. pulavarkaḷiṉ ceyal nilaivaṉappum tūymaiyum niṟaintatu.
   carittiraṅ kūṟutal, oḻukkaneṟiyamaittal, araciyalārāytal, coṟpoḻivu
   ceytal ākiya ivaiyāvum oruvaṉatu ulakiyalaṟiviṉ tiṭpanuṭpattukkēṟpa
   vamaintaṉavām. oḻukkaneṟiyum, carittir muṟaiyum, araciyalvātamum,
   avartam kalvi, kaṭaippiṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa makkaḷāl
   matikkappaṭuvaṉavām. maṟṟu, eḷiyatāya urai nūl eḻutum oruvaṉ,
   oṉṟiraṇṭāya poruṇmoḻikaḷait taṉ nūliṭaiyē toṭuppaṉēl avaṉ
   muṉivaraiyoppak karutappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. ituvē apporuḷkaṭkum itaṟkumuḷḷa
   vēṟupāṭu. .
   “tīmai kaṇṭōr tiṟattum periyōr
   tāmaṟin tuṇarka eṉpa mātō.”
         -- (naṟ. 116) eṉṟum,
   “neṭiya moḻiya kaṭiya ārtalum
   celva maṉṟutaṉ ceyviṉaip payaṉē
   cāṉṟōr celva meṉpatu cērntōr
   puṉka ṇañcum paṇpiṉ
   meṉkaṭ celvam celvameṉ patuvē”
         -- (naṟ. 210) eṉṟum,
   “tīyum valiyum vicumpu payantāṅku
   nōyum iṉpamum ākiṉṟu mātō.”
         -- (naṟ. 294) eṉṟum
   varum poruṇmoḻikaḷāl, ivaṟṟai yākkiya periyōrkaḷum nallicaic cāṉṟōr
   varicaiyuḷ iṭam peṟuvarāyiṉar. ittakaiya poruṇmoḻikaḷ ilavāyiṉ,
   irāmāyaṇamum cintāmaṇiyum piṟavum niṉṟunilavutal yāṅṅaṉam amaiyum?.
   mēṉāṭṭār pḷuṭārk (Plutarch) eṉpavarai mika mēmpaṭavuraippatu, katai
   kūṟuvatiṉum makkaḷaiyum avartam paṇpiṉaiyum veḷippaṭuttuvatē cīriya
   karuttāyk - koṇṭamaiyē yeṉpar. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, ‘araciyal piḻaittōrk
   kaṟam kūṟṟāvatu um, uraicāl pattiṉik kuyarntōr ēttalum, ūḻviṉai
   yuruttuvan tūṭṭu meṉpatu um, cūḻviṉaic cilampu kāraṇamākac cilappati
   kārameṉṉum peyarāl, nāṭṭuvatum yāmōr pāṭṭuṭaic ceyyuḷ" (cilappatikam)
   eṉṟu toṭaṅkiṉamaiyum ikkaruttē paṟṟiyeṉka.
   poruṇmoḻi yiṭaiyiṭai viraviya nūlkaḷ cilavē āṅkila nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum,
   avai ettuṇai mikutip paṭukiṉṟaṉavō attuṇai oḻukkamum uyarvum makkaṭkuṉ
   ṭākumeṉṟum, ivaṟṟil muṟpaṭṭu viḷaṅkuvaṉa namnāṭṭu moḻikaḷē yeṉṟum
   jāṉmārli eṉpavar 1887ām āṇṭu navampar mātam 11-ām nāḷ eṭiṉpark eṉṟa
   nakarattil ceyta coṟpoḻivil kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. avar nāṭṭil oru kiṟittava
   āciriyar cūtiṉāl tamkaipporuḷiḻantu, kaṭaṉ vāyppaṭṭu atu tīrkkum
   āṟṟalilarāy amerikkāviṟku oṭip pōy makkaṭkeṉac cilaporuṇmoḻi niṟainta
   nūloṉṟai “cilacolliṟ palaporuḷkaḷ” (Lacon or Many things in few words)
   eṉṟa peyariṭṭu 1820-ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭār eṉṟum, annūṟkaṇ uḷḷaṉa
   poruḷuraiyākātu poyyurai (uḷḷiṭillāta urai, veṟṟurai) yāka iruntamai
   kaṇṭu tām tīyiṭṭerittuviṭṭatākavum avar kūṟukiṉṟār.
   pērilakkiyaṅkaḷum ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷum poruṇmoḻi puṇarttukkūṟal tamakku
   aṇiyākak koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭu māyiṉum avai perumpāṉmaiyum makkaṭku vēṇṭum
   potuvuṇmaikaḷaiyē yuṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
   “immai yulakat ticaiyoṭum viḷaṅki
   maṟumai yulakamu maṟuviṉ ṟeytupa
   ceṟunarum viḻaiyum ceyirtīr kāṭcic
   ciṟuvarp payanta cemmalōr"
   (akam- 66) eṉṟum,
   “aṟantalaip piriyā toḻukalum ciṟanta
   kēḷir kēṭupala āṉṟalum nāḷum
   varuntā vuḷḷamō ṭiruntōrk killeṉac
   ceyviṉai purinta neñciṉar.”
   (akam - 173) eṉṟum,
   ‘neṟiyi ṉiṅkiyōr nīrala kūṟiṉum
   aṟiyā maiyeṉ ṟaṟiyal vēṇṭum”
   (cilap-10) eṉṟum
   “nallatu ceyta lāṟṟī rāyiṉum
   allatu ceyta lōmpumiṉ atutāṉ
   ellāru muvappa taṉṟiyum
   nallāṟṟup paṭuu neṟiyumā ratuvē”
   (puṟam - 195) eṉṟum,
   “celvattup payaṉē yītal,
   tuyppē meṉiṉē tappuna palavē.”
   eṉṟum varuvaṉavaṟṟālaṟika.
   makkaḷ nāṭōṟum iyaṟṟum kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum nalaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭi
   avaṟṟai nīkkutalaiyum, pōṟṟutalaiyum ceyyum poruṇmoḻikaḷ niṟainta
   nūlkaḷ vēṇṭum. āṅkilēyarkaḷ makkaḷiṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum, avar ceyyum
   piḻaikaḷaiyum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭum poruṇmoḻikaḷaip
   puṇarttaṟku katai vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyē karuviyākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivai
   perumpāṉmaiyum eḷiya naṭaiyil vaḻuvillāta iṉiya tīñcoṟkaḷālākkappaṭṭup
   pērilakkiyaṅkaṭkuḷḷa amaiti nirampimiḷirkiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭil ammuṟaiyaik
   kaiyāṭal vēṇṭic cilar puṉaikataikaḷ eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ
   perumpāṉmaiyai makkaḷiṉ oḻukkakkēṭuṇarttum poruṇ moḻi puṇarttalil
   amaintaṉavāyiṉum, ammoḻikaṭkuriya coṟcurukkamum poruṭperukkamumiṉṟi,
   vaḻuvellām niṟaintu ulavukiṉṟaṉa. "murukaṉ" eṉṉum katai nūloṉṟu
   ivvāṇṭil ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka vakuppiṟkup pāṭamāka amaintuḷḷatu.
   ataṉkaṇ makkaḷ vāḻvu peṟuvatum, naṭaippiṟaḻcciyāl kēṭṭiṟkuḷḷāki
   varuntutalum, uḻavut toḻiṟku vēṇṭum poruṇ moḻikaḷum niṟaintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
   maṟṟu, ataṉ coṉṉaṭai mikat tāḻnta nilaiyil uḷatu. nūleḻutappukuvōr.
   --
   “Plutarch’s aim was much less to tell a story than as he says ‘to
   decipher the man and his nature" - Studies in Literature P.66.
   -----------
   emmoḻiyil eḻutavirumpukiṉṟaṉarō ammoḻiyil - iyaṉṟavāṟu piḻaiyiṉṟi
   viḷaṅka eḻututal vēṇṭum. coṟṟoṭarkaḷil ilakkaṇa vaḻukkaḷ amaintiruttal
   orupuṭai niṟpa, "oruvaḷ” mutaliya ilakkaṇa varampukaṭanta coṟkaḷ pala
   viraviyiruttal kaṟpārkkuk kēṭupayakkum taṉmaiyatākiṉṟatu. niṟka, urai
   nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum poruṇmoḻikaḷ eṇṇiṟantaṉavākalāl orucilakūṟi
   ikkaṭṭuraiyiṉai muṭikkiṉṟēṉ.
   “ēṭṭuc curaikkāy kaṟikkākātu. puttakañāṉamēyuṭaiyavarkaḷ viṭayaṅkaḷai
   yārāyumpōtu tamatu conta nilaiyaiyiḻantu apputtaka nilaiyaiyē peṟṟu
   ārāyvārkaḷ. oru tēcattiṉ iyalpiṉai at tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟu tirumpiṉōr
   kūṟakkēṭṭa aṟivu evvāṟu valiyuṭaiyatākumō avvaḷavuvaliyē puttakañāṉamē
   yuṭaiyōṉ ārāycciyiṟpaṭum poruḷumākum. puttakamē kaṟṟōṉ kāṇum poruṇmai
   palliḻantōṉ taṉ vāyilamaittuk koḷḷum pōlip pallaippōl maṉattiṟ
   patikiṟatu. tāmē tamconta aṟiviṉāl aṟintatō iyalpākavēyamainta avayavam
   pōl maṉattil valuppaṭṭu niṟkiṟatu” - ti. celvak kēcavarāya mutaliyār.
   “kāmukaṉāvāṉoruvaṉ oru vaṉitaiyiṭattu aṉpuṭai yaṉ eṉpatu avaḷ okkalaik
   kaṇṭuḻi nikaḻum aṉpiṉ aḷavu paṟṟiyē aṟiyappaṭum.” - civañāṉayōkikaḷ.
   “māṉamāvatu taṉṉilaimaiyiṟ ṟāḻāmaiyum, teyvattāṟ ṟāḻvuvantuḻi
   uyirvāḻāmaiyumām."
   "uyir nilattu viṉaivittiṭṭārkku viḷaivum atuvē - parimēlaḻakar"
   cila āṅkilap poruṇmoḻikaḷ:
   1. nuṇṇaṟivōr iḻaikkum tavaṟukaḷum, aṟiñar ceyyum āravāramum, nallōr
   piḻaikkum kuṟṟaṅkaḷum ākiya ivaiyāvum cēranikaḻvatu kiḷarcciyē.
   Follies committed by the sensible, extravagances uttered by the clever,
   crimes perpetrated by the good, that is what makes revolution.
   2. makkaḷiṉ maṭamai nōkki mikka ciṉaṅkoḷḷutal maṭaṉeṉappaṭṭa yāvaiyum -
   oruṅkē yaḻaittuk koḷḷum ceyalkaḷuḷ mikkatoṉṟām.
   Excessive anger against human stupidity is itself one of the most
   provoking of all forms of stupidity.
   3. iṉpamaṉṟu aṟiñar vēṇṭuvatu; tuṉpattiṉīkkamē. avar tēṭiccelvatum
   ataṉaiyēyām.
   Not pleasure but freedom from pain. is what the sensible man goes
   after.
   4. taṉakku muṉṉiruntōṉ oruvaṟku oppākak karutil, avaṉiṉ iraṭṭitta
   āṟṟaluṭaiyaṉātal vēṇṭum.
   To equal a predecessor one must have twice his worth.
   5. ceytaṟ keḷiyatāyiṉum oṉṟaic ceytaluṟupavaṉ, ataṉai ariteṉṟum,
   ariyatāyiṉ eḷiteṉṟum karutippukutal vēṇṭum.
   What is easy ought to be entered upon as though it were difficult, and
   what. is difficult as though it were easy.
   itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, poruṇmoḻiyeṉpatu tamiḻ nūlkaḷil poruṇmoḻik
   kāñciyeṉṉum tuṟaip pakutiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvateṉpatum, atu coṟcurukkamum poruṭ
   perukkamum peṟṟu nūlkaṭku aṇiyātalēyaṉṟit toḻilmuṭṭaṟukkum
   karuviyāmeṉpatum, poruṇ moḻikaṭku āṅkilēyarkūṟum ilakkaṇam ituveṉpatum,
   oruvaṉ kaṟṟaṟkuriya poruḷkaḷuḷ ivaṟṟaik kaṟṟal avaṉ vāḻkkaic
   cākāṭukaittaṟkaṇ iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭumeṉpataṉāl, ivai makkaḷ
   vāḻviṟkeṉavamainta vitikaḷām eṉpatum, aṟivāvatu iḵteṉpatum, atu
   nālvakaip pākupāṭupeṟum eṉpatum, appākupāṭṭiṉkaṇ poruṇmoḻiyāleytum
   aṟivu mūṉṟām pākupāṭṭilaṭaṅkumeṉpatum, immoḻikaḷ makkaḷiṉ maṉap
   pāṉmaiyaiyuḷḷavāṟu kāṭṭum iyalpiṉa eṉpatum, ārāycciyē poruḷākac cellum
   ikkālattil poruṇmoḻikaḷaik kaṟṟu makkaḷiṉ maṉappaṇpārāytalum
   vēṇṭumeṉpatum, maṉappaṇpu kaṇṭu uṇarntu kūṟum poruṇmoḻiyāṭciyāl oruvaṉ
   naṉku matikkappeṟuvaṉ eṉpatum, namnāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṉ potuvāya
   paṇpiṉai yuṇarttukiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum, ikkāla urai nūlkaḷiṟ cila, makkaḷiṉ
   ciṟappup paṇpiṉaiyuṇarttiṉum vaḻumalintuḷḷaṉaveṉpatum, ēṉaiyurai
   nūlkaḷiṟ cila poruṇmoḻikaḷ ivaṟṟaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum piṟavum
   kūṟappaṭṭaṉavām.
   ----------

                            8. ulaka varalāṟu

   nam kaṭpulaṉām ivvulakiṉ tōṟṟam, vaṭivu mutaliyavaṟṟai ārāyntaṟinta
   pēraṟivāḷarkaḷ mikappalar eṉiṉum, avaruṭciṟantuniṉṟār koṇṭatu ulaku oru
   uruṇṭai vaṭivāyaporuḷ eṉpatu. atu, vaṭivil uruṇṭaiyeṉiṉum,
   eṇṇariyaparumaṉum, oḷiteṟikkum iyalpum porunti, aḷappariya ākāyameṉṉu
   maḷakkaril mitakkum palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ ceṟinta oru tippiyap
   poruḷākum. iṉṉum ataṉaik kūrntu nōkkiṉ, atu, oru nārattam
   paḻattaiyottu, mēluṅkīḻuntaṭṭaiyāy, irumaruṅkiṉum accukkaḷ niṟutta avai
   uṭceṉṟu naṭuvil kūṭumeṉa niṉaittaṟku ētuvāy, avvacciṉ vaḻiyākavē
   cuṟṟukiṟatu eṉak karutuvataṟkum iṭaṉāyirukkiṟatu.
   ivvāṟu, itu cuṟṟuvataṉāl, itaṉ eppākam ñāyiṟṟiṉ muṉ varukiṟatō,
   appākam, aññāyiṟṟiṉ oḷi peṟṟup pakaṟ kālattaip peṟukiṟatu akkālai,
   maṟṟaip pākattiliruḷ parava irākkālam nikaḻum. niṟka, itu peṟum oḷikkuk
   kāraṇamāyatu ñāyiṟu eṉpateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, atu oru oḷiniṟainta aḻaṟpiḻampu
   eṉpar. ataṉiṭattiliruntē ivvulaku tōṉṟiṟṟeṉavum kūṟuvar. vērkkaṭalai,
   moccai mutaliyavaṟṟait tīyiliṭuṅkāl, avai veppattāl veṭippa,
   avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum vitaikaḷ mikunta viraivuṭaṉ. veḷikkiḷampaloppa,
   aññāyiṟeṉṉum aḻaṟpiḻampiṉ kaṇ veṭittu virainteḻunta poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu itu
   eṉavum kūṟuvar. iṉṉaṇam ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum veḷip pōnta poruḷām ippūmikkum
   iññāyiṟṟukkum oru kaṭpulaṉākāt toṭarpuṇṭu. attoṭarpu oṉṟaiyoṉṟu paṟṟiyē
   niṟkum. viḷaṅkakkūṟiṉ, ñāyiṟu pūmiyait taṉ māṭṭiḻuttalum, appūmi ataṉai
   yiḻuttalumāya toṭarpu. itaṉai vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ākarṣaṇa caktiyeṉpar.
   mēṉāṭṭār. Attraction of Gravitation (aṭrākṣa ṉāv kirāviṭēṣaṉ) eṉpar.
   aṉṟiyum, or yāṇṭil nikaḻum kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṇi mutalāya
   paruvaṅkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkum accuṟṟē kāraṇameṉavum kūṟuvar.
   ikkūṟiya koḷkaiyē perumpālār koṇṭa teṉiṉum, itaṟku muraṇāyiṉārumuḷar.
   itiṟ ciṟitu vaḻuviyārumuḷar eṉavuṅ koḷka. ivarkaḷuṭ paṇṭaiya
   muṉivarkaḷum oru cārārāvar. ivarkkuḷ, cilar ippūvulaku oru camaṉāṉaveḷi
   yeṉṟum, itaṉait tāṅkum palatiṇṇiya tūṇkaḷ pala itaṉkīḻē yuḷaveṉṟuṅ
   kūṟuvar. cilar ituvoru pēraraviṉ muṭiyilirukkiṟateṉṟum, vēṟucilar, oru
   aṭalmikkayāmaiyālitu cumakkap peṟukiṉṟa teṉavuṅ kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ kūṟiya
   tuṇ, arā, yāmai mutaliyaṉa niṉṟu tāṅkaṟku uriya iṭam eṉṉeṉak
   kaṭāyiṉārkku viṭaiyiṟut talākāmaiyiṉ, avai koḷḷaṟpālaṉavalla veṉka.
   pirāmaṇarkaḷ, viṇṇulaku maṇṇulakaic cārntu mēṉiṟkiṟateṉavum,
   ivaṟṟiṉiṭaiyil nīriṭaittavaḻum mīṉiṉam pōl, ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum vāṉiṭai
   mitantu kiḻakku mēṟkākac ceṉṟu. tattamakkuriya kālattiṟutiyil ulaku
   viḷimpai (Horizon) yaṭaintu, muṭivil tattam muṟaippaṭit
   tōṉṟumiṭattaiyaṭaiyumeṉavum kūṟuvar.
   niṟka, purāṇikarkaḷ, ippūmiyoru taṭṭaic camaveḷi yeṉṟum, itaṉaic cuṟṟip
   pāṟkaṭalait talaikkoṇṭa eḻuvakaik kaṭalkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉiṭaiyil,
   mērumutalāka eḻuvakai malaikaḷumirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟuṅ kūṟalōṭamaiyātu,
   cūriya cantira kirakaṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa irākuveṉṉum pēraravu
   cilakālaṅkaḷiṟṟōṉṟi avaṟṟuḷoṉṟai viḻuṅkuṅ kālamē eṉavuṅ kūṟupa.
   apulkācaṉali yeṉṉum pēraṟivāḷaroruvar tam nāṭṭu vallār uraikaḷaik
   kaṟṟut tam nuṇṇaṟiviṟkut tōṉṟiya cilavaṟṟait tām eḻutiyuḷḷa "poṉṉilamum
   maṇiccuraṅkamum" (Mourondge-ed-dharab, or The golden meadows, and the
   mines of precious stones) eṉṉum ulaka varalāṟṟuṭ kūṟumāṟu:-
   "ippūvulaku oru taṉipperum paṟavaiyākum. itaṉ talaiyē mekkā, metiṉā
   eṉṉum nakaraṅkaḷ. pāracīkamum intiyāvum ataṉ valacciṟakākavum, kāk
   (Gog) eṉṟa nāṭu iṭacciṟakākavum, āppirikkā ataṉ vālākavum amaintuḷḷaṉa.
   iṉṟaiyappōtu nām vāḻum ippūvulakiṉ muṉpu, vēṟoru ulakiruntatu. atutōṉṟi
   ēḻāyiram āṇṭukaḷē iruntatu. ataṉai ippūppaṟavaiyiṉ kuñceṉiṉum poruntum.
   tōṉṟiyirunta atu, iṭaiyil nikaḻnta veḷḷam, nilanaṭukkam mutalāya
   iṭaiyūṟukaḷāl iṉṉalvāyp paṭṭiṟantoḻiya itu tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam, pūvulakam
   tōṉṟiyaḻital iyaṟkaiyē. avaṟṟiṟkuriya kālam eḻupatiṉāyiram hasarōvam
   ākum. oru hasarōvameṉpatu paṉṉīrāyira māṇṭukaḷām..."
   ikkālak kuṟippum, varaiyaṟaiyum ivar tammōṭirunta pirāmaṇa naṇparkaḷoṭu
   kalantu ceytatātal innūlāṟ ṟerikiṟatu.
   innūlāciriyaraip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ:- ivaratu tantai ālkāṉ; avar
   tantai aliyeṉpār; avar tantai aptur rahimāṉ; avar tantai aptullā; avar
   tantai macauṭal ateli. ivarai yāvarum macauṭi yeṉavē yaḻaippar. piṟaku
   ivaratu aṟivaikuṟittu, ivarkkuk “kutputīṉ" eṉṉum paṭṭamum akkālattār
   aḷittaṉar. eṉiṉum, ivar appaṭṭap perumaiyaiyum karutātu lāhēp arracau
   (paramporuḷiṉ tūtaṉuṭaiya tōḻaṉ) eṉṉu morutāḻvāṉa (akkālattār karuttu)
   paṭṭattaiyē yēṟṟaṉar.
   nūṟkuṟippu:-
   itu ulakiṉ tōṟṟa mutal innūl eḻutiyakālam varaiyiṟ kūṟum varalāṟākum.
   ivaratu kālam mōtipillāviṉ kilāpat kālam. atāvatu hijirā 336.
   itukāṟum kūṟiya koḷkaikaḷum iṉṉōraṉṉapiṟavum pūvulakaip poruttamaṭṭil
   mikappala uṇṭu. ovvoru koḷkaikkum ataṉaiyāyntaṟinta periyōrkaḷ, avvak
   kālattukkēṟpap palacila kāraṇaṅkaḷum, karikaḷum kāṭṭiyē niṉṟaṉar.
   iṉi, ñāyiṟu mutaliya vāṉulakap poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi avarkaḷ
   niṉaittavaṟṟaip pārppōm.
   ñāyiṟṟiṉ piṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟaik kūṟavanta pēraṟiñarkaḷum palarē.
   avarkaḷ ovvoruvarum kāṭṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ, avarotta aṟiñarkaḷai mayakkun
   taṉmaiyavāy niṟpa, avaravar tattammāliyaṉṟavaḷavu piṟappuk kūṟiṉar.
   aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉār tokaiyaittukki nōkkuṅkāl, avarkaḷ mūṉṟu
   vakaiyuḷaṭaṅkuvar. oruvakaiyār mikap paṇṭaik kālattu aṟiñarkaḷāvār.
   avarkaḷ, ñāyiṟu oru aḷattaṟkariya aḻalāḻi (Wheel of fire) yeṉpar; oru
   cārār, oru paḷiṅkotta voḷiteṟikkum (Transparent) kaṇṇāṭi allatu uruṇṭai
   vaṭiviṟṟāya paḷiṅku eṉṟaṉar; mūṉṟām piriviṉar, kallum irumpum kalantu,
   pēraḻalvāyp paṭṭuruki, oḷikālum taṉmaivāynta or arumperum poruḷ eṉpar.
   ivaruṭ ṭalaiyāyavar, aṉaksākoras (Amaxagorus) eṉṭār. aṉṟiyum, ivar
   viṇmīṉ, tiṅkaḷ mutaliya vāṉap poruḷkaḷaṉaittum maṇṇiṉiṉṟum teṟitteḻunta
   kaṟkaḷeṉavum, avai ovvoṉṟiṟkumuḷḷa vāṉiṭait toṭarpu (allatu
   taṉvayaṅkōṭal) Attraction of gravity) vayappaṭṭu, viraintu cuṟṟic
   cellum iyalpuṭaimaiyiṉ payaṉākat tīkkoṇṭu oḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
   ivar ittakaiya ārāycci muṭivai urōma (Rome) tēyattumakkaḷukku
   veḷiyiṭṭakālai, avarkaḷ itaṉaiyēṟṟaṟku maṟuttatōṭamaiyātu, ivaraiyum
   tam nāṭṭiṉiṉṟun turatti viṭṭārātaliṉ itaṉai nām naṉku āytal
   vēṇṭiṟṟillai. aṉṟiyum, paṇṭaik kālattu urōma tēyattu makkaḷ tamakku
   ēṟkātakoḷkai, eṇṇam mutaliyavaṟṟai oruvar veḷiyiṭuvarēl ataṉai maṟukkum
   vakaiyāl avarai veḷināṭukaḷukkut turatti viṭutal marapeṉavum koḷka.
   niṟka, vēṟucilar, maṇṇiṉiṉṟum aḻaṟ poruḷkaḷ pala mēlē kiḷarntu ceṉṟu,
   nāḷāvaṭṭattil vāṉaveḷiyiṟṟirintu niṟpat taṟceyalāka avai oṉṟu kūṭi oru
   pēraḻaṟ piḻampāyiṉa; appiḻampē iññāyiṟu, apporuṭkaḷuṭ cila (avaiyum
   eṇṇiṟantaṉavē) oṉṟu kūṭātu, taṉittō, tampōṉṟa cila kūṭit tiraṇṭa ciṟu
   piḻampukaḷākavō viṇmīṉkaḷeṉat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; ivaikaḷ, kālam nīṭitta
   kālai, vēṟu cila aḻaṟporuḷ tammuḻai vantucēra, oruṅkē oḷipeṟuvatumuṇṭu
   eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
   cilar, namkaṭpulaṉām ñāyiṟṟiṉmuṉ vēṟōr ñāyiṟu iruntateṉavum, atu oru
   kālattut taṉ oḷimuḻutum oḻintumaṟaiya, oru tiṅkaḷkāṟum ulakam
   oḷiyiṉṟiyiruntatu; appōḻtē, iññāyiṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉavuṅ kūṟuvar.
   vēṟucilar, ñāyiṟu eṉpatu orpeṟalarum cīruñciṟappu mamaintu,
   pēraḻakuvāynta pērakameṉavum, aṅku makkaḷ ceṉṟu vāḻtaluṅkūṭumeṉṟum
   kūṟi, appērakattiṉ mītu oru vakaittāṉa oḷi, veppam, nīr mutaliyavum
   vēṟu piṟavum kalanta mēkapaṭalam cūḻavuḷateṉṟum, appaṭalattiṉ ceyalē
   ippūvulakupeṟum ñāyiṟṟoḷiyeṉavum kūṟupa ivaruṭṭalaiyāyavar herskēl
   eṉpavar.
   immuṟaiyē, avaravar kūṟiyuḷḷavaṟṟait tēṭik koṇṭē celvatu
   virivukāṇumātalāl, itaṉai yimmaṭṭiṉiṟutti, iṉi, innilam, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ
   viṇmīṉ mutaliyavai tattam nilaiyiṟṟiriyātu koṭpuṟum taṉmaiyaiyum,
   viraivaiyum paṟṟiyeḻunta periyōrkaḷiṉ palavēṟu vakaippaṭṭa
   mataṅkaḷaiyum kūṟi muṭivil, nam nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟaiyum nōkkuvōm.
   ñāyiṟṟiṉ iyaṟkaiyaip paṟṟip pala vēṟu vakaippaṭṭa āciriyarkaḷ
   kūṟiyavaṟṟaiyum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum nām ārāyntu koṇṭē celliṉ,
   ittalaip poruḷ muṟṟup peṟal aritāmākaliṉ, avvārāycciyai im
   maṭṭiṉiṟutti, ikkaṭṭurait toṭakkattiṟkūṟiya pūmiyiṉ cuḻaṟciyaip paṟṟic
   ciṟitu nōkkippiṉ ataṉ varalāṟṟai yārāyvōm.
   mēl nāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil leyṭeṉ (Leyden) eṉṉum palkalaik kaḻakamoṉṟil,
   pērāciriyarāy vāṉpuṭiṅ kāpṭ (Wompuddingcoft) eṉpāroruvariruntār, avar
   oru pērāciriyaṉukkuḷḷa amaitimuṟṟum nirampap peṟṟavareṉiṉum tērtal
   (Examination) kālaṅkaḷ aṟintu cōrtuyil koḷvār. ataṉāl, avar māṇavarkaḷ,
   kaṟṟaṟṟiṟam ciṟitum kaḷiyāṭṭayartal peritum payiṉṟuviḷaṅkiṉar.
   orunāḷ, ivar māṇavarkku nilavulakaip paṟṟiya ōr virivurai
   nikaḻttumamaiyattu, vāḷi (Tub or bucket) yoṉṟiṟṟaṇṇir koṇṭu,
   ēntiyakaiyarāy ataṉai nīṭṭiya vaṇṇam nērē piṭittuccuṟṟiṉar. cuṟṟuṅkāl
   vāḷiyilirunta nīr kīḻē vīḻātu, ataṉiṭattēyē niṉṟatu. itaṉai or kāṭṭākak
   koṇṭu, avar piṉvarumāṟutaṉ māṇavarkaṭkuk kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
   "ivvāḷiyē pūmiyākavum, ataṉ nīrē kaṭalākavum nīṭṭip piṭitta kaiyē
   pūmikku ñāyiṟṟiṉukkumuḷḷa toṭarpākavum, eṉ ceṉṉiyē ñāyiṟākavum koḷka.
   yāṉ vāḷiyaic cuṟṟuṅkāleḻunta viraiviṉ payaṉāka, eṅṅaṉam ataṉ kaṇirunta
   taṇṇīr kīḻē viḻātu niṉṟatō aṅṅaṉamē ippūvulaku cuṟṟum nērmaiyāl
   ataṉkaṇuḷḷa kaṭaṉīrum irunta vaṇṇamē niṟkiṉṟatu, eṉa aṟika. yāṉ cuṟṟum
   neṟiyiṉiṉṟum uṭaṉē niṟuttiṉālum avvāḷiyē yātarṉu moṉṟāl taṭaipeṟṟālum
   ataṉir kaivaḻiyē eṉ talai micaiviḻum. itu uṇmai. iṅṅaṉamē, pūmiyum taṉ
   virainta cuḻaṟciyiṉiṉṟum yātāṉumoṉṟāṟṟakaiyap peṟiṉ, kaṭaṉīrum, ataṉ
   payaṉāka ñāyiṟṟiṉ mītu viḻum, viḻiṉum ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmaiyum, oḷiyiṉ
   mikutiyum evvāṟṟāṉuṅ kuṟaipaṭā veṉpataic ciṟitum maṟavaṟka. eṉṉai:
   ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmaikku immaṇ cūḻnta kaṭaṉir āṟṟātākaliṉ eṉka."
   itaṉaik kēṭṭa māṇavarkaḷuḷoruvaṉ, taṉ āciriyaruraitta poruḷ
   ettuṉaiyuṟutiyuṭaittu, eṉakkāṇṭal vēṭkai mīturntu, vāḷiyaic cuṟṟi
   niṉṟa. āciriyaratukaiyai iṭainiṉṟu taṭuttāṉ. taṭukkavē avvāḷi nīr avar
   uṭal muḻutum vīḻntu naṉaittatu, vāḷiyum kīḻvīḻntaḻivuṟṟatu.
   āciriyarkkum aṭakkalākā vekuḷipiṟantatu. mukaṅkuruti pāyntu
   cekkarccevēreṉac civantatu. uṭaṉē kaṇkaṟuppa vīcai tuṭippa, avaratu
   maṭittavāyiṉiṉṟum, veṭitta coṟkaḷ pala veḷivantaṉa. kaṇṭār māṇavarkaḷ.
   kaṇṭāratu karuttil pēraccantōṉṟi oru puṭaivaruttiṉum, maṟupuṭai, vāḷi
   nīr pāyntum, taṅkaḷ āciriyar mukam taṉṉoḷi keṭātiruntamai kaṇṭu,
   iṉṉaṇamē kaṭaṉīr viḻiṉum, ñāyiṟṟiṉṟaṉmai tiriyā teṉpatu muṇmaiyē
   eṉamaṉantēṟic ceṉṟaṉar.
   innikaḻcciyil, māṇavaratu aṟiyāmaik kuṟumpu oru puṭait tōṟṟamāyiṉum,
   nām nuṇukinōkki aṟiyakkiṭappaṉa palavuḷa. avai uyarntōr tam
   uṇmaiyaṟivāṉārāyntu kāṇum ariyaporuḷkaḷai, iyaṟkai naṅkaiyum uṭaṉ
   koḷḷātu. avai tāmē mutirntu varuntuṇaiyum vēṟāy niṟpaḷeṉpatum:
   iṉṉōraṉṉa ariya poruḷkaḷ aṟivuṭaiyōr maṉakkaṇṇiṟṟōṉṟi, avarāṉ veḷiyiṭap
   peṟuṅkāl, avaṟṟaiyēṟkum āṟṟalillāp piṟamakkaḷ - iyaṟkai naṅkaiyiṉ
   iḷañciṟārāya immakkaḷ - ēṟkātoḻivataṉṟi, avaṟṟaiyum, veḷiyiṭuvōraiyum
   mikappala iṭaiyūṟṟukkuḷḷākkit tuṉpuṟuttuvareṉpatum; itaṉāleytum kuṟai
   av aṟiñarkaḷatō, maṟṟu, avarkaḷ kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷatōveṉpārkku atu
   avviraṇṭiṭattu maṉṟāy iyaṟkaiyiṉ pāṟṟāmeṉak kōṭal vēṇṭumeṉpatum;
   ivviyaṟkai naṅkaitāṉ, avai veḷi varuṅkāl, makkaḷ avaṟṟai yeḷitilaṟintu
   ēṟkāvaṇṇam mayakkuvatōṭu amaiyātu, av aṟiñarkaḷukku iṉṉa viḻaikkumāṟu
   avarkaḷait tūṇṭutalum ceyvāḷeṉpatum, iṅkukkūṟiya iraṇṭu kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ
   vayappaṭṭa ivaṭaṉ māyaiyē mēlē nikaḻnta nikaḻccikkum kāraṇamāmeṉpatum;
   tam uṭaṟkum vāḷikkum iṭai niṉṟa kaiyē ñāyiṟu pūmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ
   iṭainiṟkum toṭarpeṉṟum, ittoṭarpē ulakam niṉṟu nilavuvataṟku
   iṉṟiyamaiyāp poruḷeṉṟu mavar kūṟiyatu uṇmaiyēyām eṉpatum; itaṉai
   avvāciriyar veḷiyiṭutalumiyalpē yeṉpatum; iṭainiṉṟa toṭarpu
   taṭaippaṭṭaḻiyuṅ kālattut taṉ māṭṭiḻukkum taṉmaittāya ñāyiṟu taṉ taṉmai
   tiriyātu niṟkumeṉpatum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavumām.
   ikkaruttu meykkaruttēyeṉa mēṉāṭṭup perum pulavarkaḷ aṉaivarum
   uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar; iṉṟum uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ "nilattukkum
   ñāyiṟṟiṉukkumuḷḷa toṭarpu or kālattu aḻiyum; avvaḻivāl nilam.taṉnilai
   peyarntu ñāyiṟṟiṉ kaṭ paṭṭaḻiyum.” eṉpataiyē iṉṉum etirnōkki
   niṟkiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟu, avartam nōkkattiṟku māṟāka, immaṇṇulaku taṉ
   toṭarpai iḻattaliṉṟittaṉ nilaimai māṟāmaiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
   ivviyaṟkaip pulavarkaḷuṭaiya koḷkaikaḷ muṟṟup peṟunāḷ ennāḷō! itu
   kāraṇamāka eḻunta koḷkaikaḷum pulavarkaḷum viṇ mīṉ tokaiyiṉum palarē.
   eṉ ceyvar tām āyntaṟinta uṇmaippaṭi ulakam taṉ toṭarpu keṭṭālaṉṟō
   avarkaḷ maṉam amaitipeṟum! iṉṟēl, ippulavarkaḷaiyum iyaṟkaiyaiyum
   oṉṟuvikkum cantām taṉmaiyuṭaiya pulavarkaḷēṉum varal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷum
   vantārilar!!
   ākavē, tām koṇṭa uṇmaippaṭi ulakam naṭai peṟāmaikaṇṭa avarkaḷ ulaka
   naṭaippaṭit tam muṇmaikaḷaic celutti nōkkiṉar. atu koṇṭē mēṟpōnta
   pērāciriyarum "pūmikkum cūriyaṉukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu keṭiṉ, avai
   akkēṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamāyavaṟṟait toṭarātu, oṉṟāṉoṉṟu aḻintoḻiyu meṉiṉum,
   ivvulakam ñāyiṟṟaic cuṟṟi varutaleṉpatuṇmai"yeṉa āṇṭukkūṟātu
   kūṟippiṉṉarō ramayattiṉaṉku viḷakkiṉar. atu mutaṟkoṇṭu, ulakam
   pakalavaṉaic cuṟṟi varukiṟa teṉpatum, accuṟṟukkum ñāyiṟṟiṉukkumuḷḷa
   toṭarpum neṟiyum cuṟṟum poruḷaiccārntaṉaveṉpatum muṭinta
   muṭivukaḷāyiṉa.
   ulakiṉ tōṟṟam
   mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ maṇ, ñāyiṟu mutaliya ulakaṅkaḷiṉ potuviyalpukaṇṭa
   aṉparkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟattaikkāṉa avāvuvaraṉṟē! avvavāvum
   aṟiviṉpālatēyām. āyiṉum, ulakiṉṟōṟṟattaik kāṇṭal cellum nām mutaṟkaṇ
   nam tōṟṟattai yaṟivōmāveṉpatu viṉā. avviṉāviṟkup pōtiya
   viṭaiyaḷikkavalla muṭipukaḷ itukāṟum kiṭaittila kiṭaippatumaritē.
   aṟṟēl, nam nāṭṭuppaṇṭaiya muṉivarkaḷum, periyōrkaḷum kaṇṭāyntavai
   eṉṉāyavōveṉiṉ, avai avaravarkaḷ koṇṭuceṉṟa aṟiviṉṟiṇ maikkēṟpa
   amaintuḷḷaṉavēyaṉṟi, ellāmakkaḷāṉum koḷḷap peṟavillai. iṉṉaṇamē
   piṟanāṭṭār kūṟiya muṭipukaḷum amaintuḷḷaṉa eṉka. eṉavē, irutalaiyum
   tūkki nōkkuṅkāl oru vakai muṭivum peṟutal aritākiṉṟatu. ittaṉaiyum
   nōkkiyō nam nāvaraiyarum "vantavā ṟeṅṅaṉē? pōmāṟētō? māyamām peruvāḻvu"
   eṉat tiruvāymalarntatu!
   iṅṅaṉam nam tōṟṟamē māyameṉiṉ, nām niṉṟu nilavum ulakiṉ tōṟṟamum
   māyamēyām. āyiṉum, nam nāṭṭup periyōratu koḷkaiyōṭu piṟanāṭṭup
   periyōrkaḷatu kūṟṟukkaḷai nōkki maṉattāṉ ārāyvōm.
   ----------
